### [1] [Astrophysical Signatures of Fermionic Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17589)
*Vinit D Tyagi,Suhas S S,Arun Kenath*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究由费米子暗物质构成的引力束缚致密天体，通过微透镜巡天给出质量限制，并评估其热辐射及对重子缺失问题的可能贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米子暗物质是暗物质的主要候选之一，但直接探测实验迄今无正结果，因此需要探索其在引力束缚致密天体形式下存在的可能性。

Method: 回顾微透镜巡天数据以限定这类致密天体的质量范围；分析重子物质向其上的吸积过程，并计算其热学与辐射性质。

Result: 估算的爆发辐射远低于银河系中心GeV过量所对应的能量，表明这类致密天体不太可能是该信号的来源。

Conclusion: 暗物质与重子物质混合的致密天体可能解释部分尚未观测到的重子物质，即为宇宙中部分"缺失重子"提供一种可能解释。

Abstract: Fermionic dark matter particles remain one of the most compelling candidates for the dark matter content of the Universe, yet no positive results have been obtained from direct detection experiments. In this work, we investigate the possibility that such particles form compact gravitationally bound objects supported by degeneracy pressure. Through an extensive review of microlensing surveys, we derive constraints on the masses of these compact objects. We further analyze the accretion of baryonic matter onto these objects and evaluate their thermal and radiative properties. The estimated burst emission is found to be well below the energies associated with the Galactic Center GeV excess, suggesting that these compact objects are unlikely to be the source of the observed signal. Our analysis suggests that admixed dark matter and baryonic matter objects could potentially account for a fraction of the presently unobserved baryonic matter, thereby providing a possible explanation for a fraction of the missing baryons in the Universe.

</details>


### [2] [Domain Adaptation against Background Sculpting in Anomaly Detection at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17659)
*Vincent Benne,Marie Hein,Michael Krämer,Humberto Reyes-Gonzalez,Philipp Soldin,Christopher Wiebusch*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于域自适应的去相关方法，缓解弱监督异常检测中异常分数与共振质量相关性导致的背景雕刻问题，在保持检测性能的同时减少背景 sculpting。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱监督异常检测广泛用于模型无关的新物理搜索（尤其共振搜索），但异常分数与共振质量的相关性会在按异常分数选事件后扭曲背景分布，使基于边带的背景估计复杂化，即背景雕刻（background sculpting）问题。

Method: 采用域自适应（domain adaptation）对异常分数与共振质量进行去相关；使用 LHC Olympics R&D 数据集，并对比多种弱监督异常检测方法进行验证。

Result: 域自适应可显著降低背景雕刻，同时基本保持异常检测性能；当输入特征与共振质量的相关性损害原方法性能时，域自适应还能恢复灵敏度。

Conclusion: 域自适应去相关是一种同时兼顾背景估计可靠性与检测灵敏度的有效策略，适用于弱监督共振异常搜索。

Abstract: Weakly supervised anomaly detection has been shown to be an effective tool for model-agnostic searches for new physics, especially in the context of resonance searches. However, correlations between the anomaly score and the resonant mass can distort the background distribution after selecting on the anomaly score, complicating background estimation from the sidebands. To mitigate this background sculpting, we propose a domain-adaptation-based decorrelation of the anomaly score from the resonant mass. We study this approach using the LHC Olympics R&D data set and several weakly supervised anomaly detection methods. We find that domain adaptation can substantially reduce background sculpting while largely preserving the anomaly detection performance. When correlations between the input features and the resonant mass degrade the original method's performance, domain adaptation can also recover sensitivity.

</details>


### [3] [Collider Detector Observables from Lattice Spin Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17664)
*João Barata,Ying-Ying Li,Bo Wang,Hua Xing Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出用量子模拟平台研究探测器可观测量：构造晶格探测器算符并建立其与连续能量流可观测量的联系，在1+1维量子Ising模型中验证晶格计算与临界共形场论预期定量一致，为在桌面量子平台实现高能对撞机物理提供途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测器可观测量具有内禀实时性与渐近性，对非微扰格点方法构成根本挑战，需要新的研究框架。

Method: 以量子自旋系统为示范，构造晶格探测器算符，建立其与连续能量流可观测量的对应关系，并在1+1维量子Ising模型中进行有限格点计算。

Result: 有限格点计算结果与临界点处共形场论预期定量吻合。

Conclusion: 晶格流算符可解释为连续探测器在标度与渐近极限下的正规化对应物，为在桌面量子平台实现高能对撞机物理提供具体路径。

Abstract: Detector observables pose a fundamental challenge for nonperturbative lattice methods due to their intrinsically real-time and asymptotic nature. We propose quantum simulation platforms as an ideal setting for studying these observables. Using quantum spin systems as a demonstration, we construct lattice detector operators and establish their connection to continuum energy-flow observables. We demonstrate this framework in the $1+1$-dimensional quantum Ising model, where finite lattice calculations show quantitative agreement with conformal field theory expectations at criticality. These results support the interpretation of the lattice flow operators as regulated counterparts of continuum detectors in the scaling and asymptotic limits, providing a concrete route toward realizing high-energy collider physics on table-top quantum platforms.

</details>


### [4] [Proper Time Shifts in Pulsar Timing Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17668)
*Vincent S. H. Lee*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了脉冲星计时残差的规范不变框架，将可测量量识别为地球处连续脉冲到达之间的固有时间隔，在线性化广义相对论下导出其分解为Doppler、Shapiro和Einstein延迟，并给出计算任意扰动下计时残差的通用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星计时阵列是探测低频引力现象和超出标准模型物理的有力工具，但缺乏一个统一、规范不变的计时残差理论框架，以支撑对各类新物理扰动的系统搜索。

Method: 将可测量量定义为地球处连续脉冲到达之间的固有时间隔；在线性化广义相对论中推导该固有时偏移的一般表达式，证明其分解为Doppler、Shapiro和Einstein延迟三部分且总和规范不变；并给出针对任意指定扰动计算计时残差的操作流程。

Result: 获得了规范不变的固有时偏移分解表达式，提供了计算任意扰动下计时残差的通用配方，并通过若干物理上有动机的源示例进行说明，在文献已有结果处成功复现了已知结论。

Conclusion: 该规范不变框架可与现有数值软件直接结合，用于从真实脉冲星计时数据中搜索新物理，为脉冲星计时阵列的引力波与新物理探测提供了普适理论基础。

Abstract: Pulsar timing arrays are a powerful tool for probing low-frequency gravitational phenomena and physics beyond the Standard Model. We establish a general, gauge-invariant framework for the timing residual by identifying the measurable quantity as the proper time interval between consecutive pulse arrivals at Earth. In linearized general relativity, we derive a general expression for this proper time shift and show that it decomposes into Doppler, Shapiro, and Einstein delay contributions whose sum is gauge invariant, similar to frameworks that have been developed for other gravitational-wave detectors such as laser and atom interferometers. We also provide a recipe for computing the resulting timing residual for any specified perturbation, and we illustrate the method with several physically well-motivated sources, recovering known results where they exist in the literature. This recipe can be directly applied to search for new physics from real pulsar timing data using existing numerical software.

</details>


### [5] [WIMPs with Enhanced Annihilation from a Feebly Interacting Unstable Partner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17672)
*Chance Hoskinson,Pearl Sandick,Barmak Shams Es Haghi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 弱相互作用大质量粒子（WIMP）通过逆衰变产生重伙伴粒子，其延迟衰变注入非热WIMP，可能过产暗物质，需增强湮灭截面（最多三个数量级），从而强化晚期宇宙湮灭信号，可被间接探测实验检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WIMP是动机最充分的暗物质候选者之一，但标准热历史下的WIMP遗迹丰度受冻结-out机制严格限制。作者希望探索对WIMP范式的简单扩展——引入只与WIMP耦合、且可衰变为WIMP的更重伙伴粒子，考察其对暗物质丰度与热历史的影响，并寻找可观测信号。

Method: 构建WIMP与重伙伴粒子的扩展模型：早期宇宙中WIMP与标准模型热浴处于热平衡时，通过逆衰变填充伙伴粒子。依据伙伴粒子寿命不同，分情形分析其衰变对WIMP丰度的影响；对玻尔兹曼方程进行半解析与数值求解，研究冻结-out延迟（再湮灭）或继以冻结-in阶段的各种热历史，并计算相对标准情形所需的WIMP湮灭截面增强幅度。

Result: 若伙伴粒子在WIMP冻结-out前衰变，标准热历史不变；若在冻结-out后衰变，则注入的非热WIMP导致暗物质过产，需要更大的湮灭截面。所要求的截面增强可达三个数量级，从而在晚期宇宙产生更强的湮灭信号。当前及未来的间接探测实验可据此探测伙伴粒子的寿命。此外，NMSSM可作为该情景的一个实现，并可能容纳具有正确遗迹丰度的轻希格斯微子暗物质。

Conclusion: 该扩展WIMP情景通过伙伴粒子的延迟衰变改变热历史，可能显著增强湮灭截面并产生可观测的间接探测信号，为探测伙伴粒子寿命提供途径；同时可与宇宙重子不对称性建立联系，并在NMSSM中得到具体实现。

Abstract: Weakly interacting massive particles (WIMPs) constitute one of the best-motivated dark matter (DM) candidates. In this study, we consider a simple extension of the WIMP paradigm in which WIMPs are coupled to a heavier particle that interacts only with them and can decay into WIMPs. In the early Universe, when WIMPs are in thermal equilibrium with the Standard Model (SM) bath, they serve as a portal to populate the heavy partner through inverse decays. Depending on the lifetime of the partner particle, its eventual decay into WIMPs can significantly alter the WIMP abundance. If the partner decays into WIMPs before their freeze-out from equilibrium, the standard thermal history remains unchanged. When the decay occurs after WIMP freeze-out, however, the injected non-thermal population of WIMPs can result in an overproduction of DM, therefore requiring a larger WIMP annihilation cross section relative to the standard scenario without the partner. For a given partner lifetime and WIMP annihilation cross section, freeze-out may be delayed due to re-annihilation or followed by a freeze-in phase. By solving the Boltzmann equations both semi-analytically and numerically, we explore these thermal histories and study the required enhancement in the WIMP annihilation cross section compared to the canonical value. This enhancement, which can reach up to three orders of magnitude, leads to stronger annihilation signals in the late Universe. As a result, current and upcoming indirect detection experiments are able to probe the lifetime of the partner particle. We briefly explore possible connections between our scenario and the baryon asymmetry of the Universe. As an example, we show that the Next-to-Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model (NMSSM) can provide a realization of our scenario, with the possibility of accommodating light higgsino DM with the correct relic abundance.

</details>


### [6] [Quantum Seesaw Cosmology: A Heating Phase from Pauli Blocking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17681)
*Nicolás Bernal,Chee Sheng Fong*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Seesaw Cosmology中由右手中微子介导的重加热机制，发现当RHN快速填充受限动量空间区域时，宇宙膨胀不断开启新的费米子态并由标量衰变再填充，使物理RHN密度近似恒定，导致SM温度随标度因子以T∝a^{+3/8}增长，与常规物质主导重加热的T∝a^{-3/8}截然相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典重加热理论中，物质主导阶段SM温度通常随宇宙膨胀而下降（T∝a^{-3/8}）。作者希望在Seesaw Cosmology框架下，通过右手中微子产生微小中微子质量的同时，探索是否存在一种新的加热机制，能够逆转温度-标度因子关系，从而为早期宇宙热历史提供新的可能性。

Method: 在Seesaw Cosmology中，标量场通过右手中微子（RHN）对标准模型（SM）进行重加热，RHN经由跷跷板机制产生微小中微子质量。分析RHN在受限动量空间区域的填充动力学：当RHN快速占据有限动量区间时，宇宙膨胀不断打开新的费米子态，随后标量衰变将这些态重新填充，从而维持近似恒定的物理RHN密度。基于此动力学推导SM温度随标度因子的演化关系。

Result: 在RHN快速填充受限动量空间区域、膨胀持续开启新费米子态并被标量衰变再填充的条件下，物理RHN密度近似保持恒定。这一新加热机制导致SM温度随标度因子增长，满足T∝a^{+3/8}，与标准物质主导重加热阶段SM温度的T∝a^{-3/8}衰减规律形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了一种由右手中微子介导的新型重加热制度，其中SM温度随宇宙膨胀而升高（T∝a^{+3/8}），颠覆了传统物质主导重加热的温度演化图像，为Seesaw Cosmology及早期宇宙热历史研究提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: In Seesaw Cosmology, a scalar field reheats the Standard Model (SM) through right-handed neutrinos (RHNs), which generate tiny neutrino masses through the seesaw mechanism. When RHNs rapidly fill a restricted momentum-space region, expansion continually opens new fermionic states that are refilled by subsequent scalar decays, maintaining an approximately constant physical RHN density. This gives rise to a novel heating regime in which the SM temperature increases with the scale factor as $T \propto a^{+3/8}$, in sharp contrast to the conventional $T \propto a^{-3/8}$ scaling during matter-dominated reheating.

</details>


### [7] [Assessment of Super- and Hyper-Kamiokande Sensitivity to the DSNB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17705)
*Pablo Blanco-Mas,André de Gouvêa,Iván Martínez-Soler*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于Super-Kamiokande已发表结果，评估当前及下一代水切伦科夫探测器对漫超新星中微子背景（DSNB）的探测灵敏度，发现SK十年数据的灵敏度有限，而Hyper-Kamiokande有望在十年内实现统计显著的DSNB发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSNB探测是当前及下一代中微子实验的主要目标之一，需要评估现有和未来水切伦科夫探测器对该背景的探测能力，并明确大气中微子本底及中微子振荡处理对灵敏度推断的影响。

Method: 基于Super-Kamiokande合作组已发表结果，重现当前约束，并估算SK十年数据及下一代水切伦科夫探测器（如Hyper-Kamiokande）对DSNB的灵敏度；同时详细研究相关大气中微子本底，采用准确的中微子振荡与传播处理。

Result: 即使增加曝光量，SK的灵敏度仍相对有限；而HK有潜力在十年内实现对DSNB的统计显著发现。准确的中微子振荡与传播处理对于正确推断DSNB灵敏度和测量其通量至关重要。

Conclusion: 当前SK对DSNB的探测灵敏度有限，下一代水切伦科夫探测器HK有望实现DSNB的统计显著发现；准确处理中微子振荡和传播是推断灵敏度与测量通量的必要条件。

Abstract: The detection of the Diffuse Supernova Neutrino Background (DSNB) is one of the main goals of the current and the next generation of neutrino experiments. Based on results published by the Super-Kamiokande (SK) collaboration, we study the sensitivity of current and future water Cherenkov detectors to the DSNB. We reproduce current constraints and estimate the sensitivity to the DSNB of ten years of SK data and of a next-generation water-Cherenkov detector like Hyper-Kamiokande (HK). We find that, even with increased exposure, SK's sensitivity is relatively modest, while HK has the potential to deliver a statistically significant discovery of the DSNB in ten years. Along the way, we perform a detailed study of the relevant atmospheric neutrino backgrounds and argue that an accurate treatment of neutrino oscillations and propagation is essential when it comes to properly inferring the sensitivity to the DSNB and measuring its flux.

</details>


### [8] [The production of bottom-flavoured jets at the LHC through bottom-quark pair production at NNLO+PS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17812)
*Rhorry Gauld,Marius Wiesemann,Giulia Zanderighi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在LHC底夸克对产生框架下，计算了保留完整底夸克质量效应的NNLO QCD修正并与部分子簇射匹配，给出了单/双底味喷流的精确预言，并与ATLAS、CMS、LHCb数据比较，结果吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC底味喷流的主要产生机制是底夸克对产生，需要高精度理论预言以配合实验测量；同时喷流味定义方案差异、底夸克质量效应及多部分子相互作用等物理效应的影响尚需系统研究。

Method: 计算保留完整底夸克质量效应的NNLO QCD修正，并与部分子簇射匹配（NNLO+PS），在部分子和强子层次描述含一个和两个底味喷流的事件；比较不同喷流味定义方案；采用实验底味标记方案；并考察底夸克质量幂修正、对称/非对称喷流选择cut及多部分子相互作用等效应的影响。

Result: 不同喷流算法间的主要数值差异源于对含由g→bb̄分裂产生的两个底夸克的喷流的处理；与ATLAS、CMS在7和13 TeV的测量在所示分布上吻合良好，并很好地描述了LHCb数据的形状。

Conclusion: 保留质量效应的NNLO+PS预言能真实描述LHC底味喷流事件，与三大实验测量一致，且不同喷流味定义及多类物理效应对分布存在可辨识的影响。

Abstract: We consider the dominant production mechanism of bottom-flavoured jets at the LHC, which proceeds through bottom-quark pair production. We compute next-to-next-to- leading-order (NNLO) QCD corrections retaining full bottom-quark mass effects and match them with a parton shower (NNLO+PS). Our predictions provide a realistic description of events with one and two bottom-flavoured jets at both the partonic and hadronic level. We compare different jet-flavour definitions and confirm that the dominant numerical differences between the jet algorithms result from the treatment of jets that contain two bottom quarks originating from $g\to b\bar b$ splittings. We compare our predictions, using the experimental bottom-flavour tagging prescription, with measurements by ATLAS, CMS, and LHCb at 7 and 13 TeV, finding good agreement for the distributions presented by ATLAS and CMS and a good description of the shapes of the LHCb data. We also discuss the impact of various physics effects on the considered distributions: power corrections in the bottom-quark mass, the implications of symmetric/asymmetric jet-selection cuts, as well as contributions from multi-parton interactions.

</details>


### [9] [Galactic Endothermic Production and Exothermic Detection of Excited Dark Matter: Implications for LUX-ZEPLIN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17935)
*Chuan-Yang Xing*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用银河系中暗物质的内热自散射产生激发态暗物质，其与氙核的外热散射可解释 LUX-ZEPLIN 实验在 248 keV 处的候选核反冲事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LUX-ZEPLIN 实验报告了一个 248 keV 的候选核反冲事件，需要给出物理解释。

Method: 提出银河系中暗物质的内热自散射 χ1χ1→χ2χ2 产生激发态暗物质种群，该激发态在氙中通过外热散射 χ2A→χ1A 贡献事件；并以轻标量媒介子为基准模型计算转换截面与激发态比例。

Result: 在轻标量媒介子基准下，转换截面足够大，使高速度银河暗物质粒子产生百分比量级的激发态比例；对接近 TeV 量级的暗物质质量和低于 1 MeV 的能级劈裂，氙反冲谱覆盖候选区域并可给出 O(1) 的事件数。

Conclusion: 内热自散射产生激发态暗物质、再由外热散射在氙中沉积能量的机制，可以合理解释 LZ 的候选核反冲事件。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN experiment reported a candidate nuclear recoil at $248\,\mathrm{keV}$. This work proposes that endothermic self-scattering $χ_1χ_1\toχ_2χ_2$ in the Galaxy can produce an excited dark matter population, whose exothermic scattering $χ_2 A\toχ_1 A$ in xenon can account for the LZ candidate event.   <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在卷积模型中，核效应对纵向和横向结构函数表现不同，因为二者 $x$ 依赖的函数形式不同，导致卷积积分结果存在差异。文中给出了氘核 $R$ 的核效应数值结果，以配合 Thomas Jefferson 国家加速器实验室正在进行的氘核 $R$ 测量实验。

Conclusion: $R$ 中确实存在核修正，以往"$R$ 无核修正"的假设不成立。希望未来包括重核靶在内的实验能够观测到这些核效应。

Abstract: In lepton scattering from nuclei, it has been taken for granted that nuclear modifications do not exist for the longitudinal-transverse structure-function ratio $R$, although nuclear effects in $F_2$ have been investigated for a long time. In fact, experimental data of lepton-nucleus scattering have been analyzed with this assumption. It is obviously not appropriate because nuclear modifications exist in the function $R$ theoretically as shown in this work. In this work, we explain nucleon's Fermi motion's effects by using a convolution description for nuclear structure functions, especially on the deuteron. The longitudinal and transverse structure functions are defined by taking the virtual photon momentum direction along the $z$ axis. However, nucleons in a nucleus could move in any direction, so that nucleon's longitudinal and transverse structure functions could mix with each other in the nuclear medium. Such mixing effects could be of the order of $\vec p_\perp^{\,2}/Q^2$, where $\vec p_\perp$ is the nucleon's transverse momentum and $Q^2$ is given by $Q^2=-q^2$ with the virtual-photon momentum $q$, because the transverse Fermi-motion is the source of such a mixture. In addition, nuclear effects are different between the longitudinal and transverse structure functions in the convolution model because their $x$-dependent functional forms are different. Convolution integrals have different results between the longitudinal and transverse structure functions for nuclei. Because the experiment is in progress at the Thomas Jefferson National Accelerator Facility to measure the function $R$ for the deuteron, the numerical results are shown on the nuclear effects of $R$ in the deuteron. Hopefully, such nuclear effects are found experimentally by future experiments including heavy nuclear targets.

</details>

At low energies, it looks like a tetraquark ($K\bar K$ molecule) hadron, but it looks like a $q\bar q$ hadron at high energies. In the similar way, some exotic hadron candidates could become ordinary $q\bar q$ or $qqq$ hadrons at high energies, although they are interpreted as exotic at low energies. Abstract: We propose Cat's Cradle diagrams as a framework to analyse the perturbative accuracy of gauge quantum field theories across different kinematic regions, interpolating between the power countings of fixed-order perturbation theory and soft-collinear resummation. We propose a simple intermediate counting aimed at quantifying parametric accuracy for resolved jet substructure, nested so that $N^nLO \subset N^nLJ \subset N^{n+1}LL$. We show that the resolved jet-substructure power counting, $N^nLJ$, emphasises contributions that are important for resolved substructure but which are lumped together with less dominant terms in both of the two other countings. It may therefore furnish a complementary quantification of relative parametric accuracy in a kinematic region "midway" between the hard (fixed-order) and soft-collinear (resummation) regimes. We discuss practical applications to parton showers, in the context of a hard process with a characteristic hard scale of order 200 GeV.

Abstract: We propose a new source of neutrino decoherence arising from stochastic gravitational lensing by unresolved mass substructure.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BFKL小-x演化是否可解释为概率(跳跃)过程，关系到能否构造满足正性的概率性重求和，从而在微扰匹配之外确立次领头阶核的物理可实现性；这是该领域长期开放的问题。

  With $s_0=q_1q_2$ and the coupling at that argument, the negative cubic collinear pole of the truncated eigenvalue excludes a probability law at every positive coupling and rapidity. Its leading coefficient is independent of $N_c$ and $n_f$. The pole dominates the leading-order simple pole within $\sqrt{\barα/2}$ of the edge in the leading collinear approximation. Translation-preserving multiplicative conjugations cannot restore positivity. No non-negative step measure generates that truncated evolution, while a positive radial completion at zero conformal spin matches the computed order, so the obstruction is a property of the fixed-order truncation.
  The tested symmetric-scheme resummations also fail positivity, as shown in closed form for the pure and matched all-poles forms, except for a degenerate zero process, and by computation at the examined couplings for the full prescription. The improved finite-rapidity Green function fails at the displayed parameters under the contour assumption. A resummed kernel in another rapidity scheme has a non-negative step measure at tested couplings. An unweighted transverse walk describes the leading-order evolution with no approximation, but a probabilistic resummation of the symmetric next-to-leading kernel must establish positivity beyond perturbative matching, and whether one exists is left open.

</details>

Abstract: The recent LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) search at high nuclear-recoil energies reported an intriguing candidate event near 250 keV, motivating interpretations in terms of inelastic dark matter. A simple realization with Higgsino dark matter, however, is strongly constrained by capture and subsequent annihilation in the Sun, which would produce a high-energy neutrino flux excluded by IceCube. We propose an asymmetric inelastic dark-matter scenario that naturally avoids this tension. The dark matter is a predominantly Standard-Model-singlet Dirac fermion that mixes weakly with a nearly degenerate electroweak doublet. Its primordial asymmetry is generated by CP-violating out-of-equilibrium decays of heavy Majorana fermions produced nonthermally from inflaton decays, while the symmetric component is efficiently depleted through dark-sector interactions. The absence of an appreciable antiparticle abundance then suppresses dark-matter annihilation in the Sun and eliminates the associated IceCube constraint. The singlet-doublet structure simultaneously yields an off-diagonal Z-boson coupling proportional to the mixing angle, whereas the diagonal coupling is suppressed by its square. For the unpolarized densities we first ask which transformations also preserve the charge-conjugation and flavor symmetries of the evolution kernels and the momentum and valence number sum rules. The symmetries reduce the transformation to a few blocks, and the sum rules constrain only two Mellin moments, where they leave a single freedom, the split of momentum between the quark singlet and the gluon, and freeze the valence numbers. The resulting classification is complete, because with asymptotic freedom as a boundary condition a change of scheme is determined by its action on the kernels. Inside this group, the transformations that preserve positivity form a semigroup, and the same argument covers the polarized densities. A transformation that fails only the sum rules is not lost either, since a normalization that the sum rules themselves determine repairs it, and we use this to define collinear densities as integrals of transverse-momentum-dependent densities without giving up the sum rules. Result: 在多种质子-质子对撞过程上评估VyPER，并与现有解析及基于机器学习的重建技术比较，结果表明其重建精度具有竞争力且适用于多种过程。

Conclusion: 精确事件重建可在广泛的Standard Model物理过程中实现，为希格斯玻色子、电弱及顶夸克领域的精确测量开辟了新途径。

Abstract: In particle collider experiments, event reconstruction is the task of inferring the kinematics of short-lived particles produced in the hard scatter from the stable final states recorded by detectors. We decompose event reconstruction into two primary tasks: assigning measured jets and charged leptons to parent particles, and predicting unmeasured neutrino kinematics. We present VyPER, a novel geometric learning framework that represents collider events as hypergraphs with a physics-inspired topology. VyPER combines the supervised classification of hyperedges for particle assignment with a diffusion model for predicting neutrino kinematics, leveraging a joint loss function to optimize both reconstruction tasks within a unified framework. We showcase VyPER across several proton-proton collision processes, comparing its performance to existing analytical and machine-learning-based reconstruction techniques. In doing so, we demonstrate that accurate event reconstruction is achievable across a diverse range of Standard Model physics processes, opening new avenues for precision measurements in the Higgs boson, electroweak, and top-quark sectors.

</details>

Abstract: Nonextensive Bose and Fermi state is well studied with counting near black-hole horizons in a one-function, nonextremal, static, spherically symmetric class. The calculation is organized around a $q$-deformed grand ensemble whose total partition function is a $q$-product of single-level partitions. This identifies the bosonic occupation-number sum as the primary level partition and allows us to separate two operations that are often conflated: replacing that sum by a geometric-series form and applying the ordinary reciprocal-log identity to a $q$-logarithm. A finite-energy tunneling prescription yields a leading near-horizon state-counting term controlled by the surface gravity and a horizon-response factor. Entropy matching then gives an energy scale proportional to the Hawking temperature; the geometry factor cancels from normalized nonextensive corrections and from the Bose--Fermi ratio. We derive the first-order coefficients analytically and evaluate the unexpanded $q$-integrals numerically. The linear approximation is accurate to better than one percent when $q$ lies within roughly two percent of unity. All prescriptions recover the extensive limit, but the exact bosonic level sum and the geometric approximations give opposite signs for the leading deformation of the conventional $7/8$ Bose--Fermi relation. The result isolates a robust geometry-factorized structure and quantifies a distinct $q$-algebra systematic uncertainty.

Abstract: Transdimensional Bayesian inference is becoming a key ingredient in gravitational-wave astronomy. In many relevant applications, the number of components needed to describe the data is not known a priori and must be inferred jointly with their parameters. We present a transdimensional Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) framework designed to exploit massive parallelism. The method evolves a population of particles through a sequence of tempered distributions and uses the No-U-Turn Sampler for the exploration of fixed-dimensional parameter space while reversible-jump birth and death moves allow the number of components to vary. The same SMC construction also enables posterior samples obtained from a shorter data segment to be updated as additional data become available, enhancing the overall efficiency of the analysis. We validate the approach on two proof-of-concept problems: the recovery of a sequence of Gaussian pulses and a simplified LISA Galactic-binary inference problem. In both cases, the method recovers the injected number of components and produces consistent posterior estimates. These results indicate that the proposed method is a promising avenue toward scalable and parallel Bayesian transdimensional inference for current and future gravitational-wave astronomy.

Abstract: We study the optical properties of electrically charged black holes sourced by generalized Heisenberg-Euler nonlinear electrodynamics (NLED) in the second-order formalism. Using the exact parametric form of the static spherically symmetric solution, we analyze the photon sphere, the black hole shadow, and the light deflection angle, and compare the results with those of the Reissner-Nordström and Schwarzschild cases. We find that the photon sphere and shadow radii decrease with increasing charge and increase with black hole mass, while NLED effects become significant mainly near extremality. By confronting the dimensionless shadow radius with the Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) bounds from Sgr A* and M87*, we derive phenomenological upper limits on the charge, with Sgr A* providing the stronger constraint. We further show that, for fixed impact parameter and charge, the deflection angle is systematically larger than in the corresponding Reissner-Nordström spacetime, and that the critical impact parameter is shifted to larger values. Our results show that NLED corrections produce observable modifications in the optical properties of charged black holes, especially close to extremality.

Abstract: We investigate a family of $k$-essence cosmological models selected by the requirement that the field equations admit a nontrivial variational symmetry. For the $k$-essence theory with Lagrangian function $F(R,X,φ)=f_{1}R+f_{2}(φ,X)$, where $f_{2}(φ,X)=αX+β\exp\left((n-1)φ\right)X^{n}$, the existence of a scaling symmetry introduces a conservation law which we use to obtain an exact vacuum solution. We generalize our analysis and investigate the structure of the phase-space of the cosmological field equations by performing a dynamical systems analysis, both in the absence and the presence of a pressureless matter source. We employ Hubble-normalized variables and the Poincaré compactification, so as to determine the asymptotic behavior of the model and identify all admissible cosmological epochs, from early-time to late-time behavior. We discuss in detail the existence and stability conditions of each fixed point as functions of the free parameters, and characterize the full asymptotic structure of the cosmological history as provided by this symmetry-driven $k$-essence model. We explore the conditions under which the model can describe cosmic acceleration. We find that the symmetry driven $k$-essence model can describe the inflationary epoch with a natural exit to the matter dominated era, where the $k$-essence mimics the pressureless matter component.

</details>

Abstract: Stationary-axisymmetric black holes are the standard theoretical framework for astrophysical black holes, yet their interiors remain largely inaccessible: the absence of a hypersurface-orthogonal timelike Killing vector renders the metric non-diagonal, and frame dragging obstructs the usual techniques for probing the interior. We prove that any stationary-axisymmetric black hole satisfying the classical energy conditions contains at most one nondegenerate inner Killing horizon per connected interior branch, irrespective of its matter content. For compact horizon sections, we apply the Raychaudhuri equation to the congruence normal to constant-time hypersurfaces: the strong energy condition makes the squared lapse subharmonic, and the maximum principle excludes a second inner horizon. For noncompact planar and hyperbolic sections central to holography, the null energy condition instead yields a monotonicity obstruction through a geometric flow we introduce here, the ``inverse lapse flow,'' whose weak continuation via viscosity solutions we also construct. The attractive nature of gravity, encoded in these energy conditions, thus both drives horizon formation and caps the number of inner horizons, revealing a striking dual role of the same focusing mechanism.

</details>

Abstract: Antispacetime arises naturally in the vielbein formulation of general relativity yet remains invisible to probes coupling only to the metric. We show that domain walls separating spacetime from antispacetime constitute physically distinct and observable structures. Fermions interacting with such walls exhibit striking signatures: scattering induces particle-hole conversion, reflecting tetrad orientation reversal, while the wall hosts localized zero-energy Majorana and chiral modes. In 1+1 dimensions, these modes acquire a pseudoscalar mass term tied to topology and quantum anomalies, providing a direct link to the observability of $θ$ vacua. These results identify fermionic response as a definitive probe of antispacetime and establish its domain walls as physically detectable.

</details>

Abstract: This paper investigates generalized conformal modules over the Virasoro conformal algebra $Vir$, a notion originally defined by V.~Kac but largely unexplored in the literature. First, we present a twisted construction that produces new generalized conformal modules over any Lie conformal algebra from existing conformal modules. Then we give a complete classification of non-trivial generalized conformal modules over $Vir$ that are free of rank one over $\mathbb C[\partial]$, which in turn yields a classification of modules over the Lie algebra \(W_1=\bigoplus_{i\geq -1}\C L_i\) of vector fields on a line that are free of rank one over $\mathbb C[L_{-1}]$ with explicit actions expressed in terms of Bell polynomials. For arbitrary finite rank $n$, we construct a class of generalized conformal modules $V_{a,b,C(\partial)}$ and establish a complete irreducibility criterion in terms of the differential operator $\mathcal L=\frac{d}{d\partial}+C(\partial)$ acting on $\mathbb{C}(\partial)^n$. In the case of $n=2$, this criterion reduces to the absence of rational solutions of a Riccati equation. Then we prove the existence of irreducible generalized conformal modules of arbitrary finite rank, revealing a striking contrast with the conformal setting. We also construct and classify a family of infinite torsion generalized conformal modules over $Vir$, which are non-trivial and irreducible under suitable conditions.

</details>

Abstract: Let $k$ be a field and let $Λ=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}A&N\\M&B\end{smallmatrix}\right)_{φ,ψ}$ be a finite-dimensional Morita context algebra. We introduce a bilateral approximation construction which glues support $τ$-tilting modules over $A$ and $B$ by alternately correcting the two corner components through minimal approximations and pushouts. When this process terminates, it yields a support $τ$-tilting $Λ$-module with the prescribed componentwise torsion class. The one-sided case recovers Zhang's triangular-matrix construction, while the two-sided compatibility conditions give direct corner induction and, for radical-valued connecting maps, are also necessary, extending the Gao--Huang criterion. Examples show that the bilateral correction process can terminate even when neither one-sided compatibility condition is satisfied, while in other examples the process never terminates.

</details>

Abstract: The problem of inverting the Radon integral transform in
  finite-dimensional Euclidean space is considered. The relevance

  natural to consider the integrand as discontinuous function.
  However, the available inversion formulas are only proven for
  differentiable functions. Therefore, the question of obtaining
  formulas for discontinuous functions arises. It is set that the
  required results can be obtained by some modification of available
  proofs for smooth functions.

Abstract: It has been over fifty years since Kenneth Wilson had his Nobel-prize-winning ideas on the renormalization group. In this time frame, despite many attempts, no mathematical results have implemented Wilson's vision to a satisfactory degree. Although a number of predictions stemming from the renormalization group framework have been proven to date, these proofs usually rely on alternative ideas and do not cover the full predictive power of Wilson's renormalization group. The discovery of pathologies within rigorous implementations of renormalization group transformations have further reduced the enthusiasm of the mathematics community for this subject. This paper presents a novel framework to implement Wilson's original idea in a rigorous manner based on inverse limits of finite systems. Doing so, one avoids these pathologies and hence obtains a mathematically sound framework for Wilson's renormalization group. This opens up a completely new path to tackle some of the existing problems in rigorous renormalization group theory like the existence of non-trivial fixed points.

</details>

Abstract: We study conserved and dissipated quantities associated with contact canonoid transformations and their extension to possibly singular smooth maps. A polynomial construction produces first integrals from one-forms having the same dissipation rate as the original contact form. This construction includes zeros of the original Hamiltonian, where the endomorphism vanishes. Abstract: This paper solves the Wehrl-type entropy problem for arbitrary compact connected semisimple Lie groups. Let $G$ be a compact connected semisimple Lie group, and let $π:G\to U(V_λ)$ be a finite-dimensional irreducible unitary representation associated with the highest weight $λ$. We prove that coherent projectors are the unique minimizers of the Wehrl entropy over all density matrices on $V_λ$. They also uniquely maximize every Husimi power moment of order $p>1$. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆盘磁台球具有显式的速度多项式首次积分，核心刚性问题在于是否还有其他光滑凸台球也能拥有此类积分；已有理论仅排除了除有限个强磁场强度外的情形，本文旨在消除这一有限例外集。

Method: 设Ω为有界严格凸域，Larmor半径r=|B|^{-1}满足0<r<r0(γ)/2。刚性机制分两个逻辑独立的阶段：首先由最高反射模得到边界绕数恒等式，确定最高系数的次数并将其实根置于域内；其次将两条前导反射恒等式延拓到复化边界的归一化上，利用无穷远赋值排除坐标函数的公共极点，残余单侧极点迫使最高系数为单一线性因子的重数形式。结合根的定位定理与根坍缩定理，得到边界切线与径向方向间的常角关系，进而推出边界为圆。

Result: 在0<r<r0(γ)/2的强场条件下，若磁台球存在任意有限次、关于速度变量的非平凡多项式首次积分，则Ω必为圆盘。该方法无需实解析边界假设，解析性可由代数强场平行曲线推出。

Conclusion: 磁台球的速度多项式可积性在强场区域具有完全刚性：唯一的可积凸台球是圆盘，此前理论中的有限例外磁场强度被彻底移除。

Abstract: A magnetic billiard describes a charged particle constrained to a planar domain: the particle moves along circular Larmor arcs in the interior and undergoes specular reflection at the boundary. The round disk has an explicit first integral that is polynomial in the velocity, and a central rigidity question asks whether any other smooth convex table can have such an integral.

Let $Ω\subset\mathbb R^2$ be a bounded strictly convex domain with smooth boundary $γ$, let $r=|B|^{-1}$ be the Larmor radius, and assume $0<r<r_0(γ)/2$, where $r_0(γ)$ is the maximal embedded tubular radius. If the magnetic billiard admits a nonconstant first integral polynomial in the velocity variables, of any finite degree, then $Ω$ is a disk. This removes the finite exceptional set of strong field strengths left by the earlier polynomial nonintegrability theory.
  The rigidity mechanism has two logically independent stages. First, the highest reflection mode gives a boundary winding identity. This determines the degree of the top coefficient and places all of its roots strictly inside the table, but it does not show that those roots coincide. Second, after the two leading reflection identities are continued to the normalization of the complexified boundary, their valuations at infinity exclude simultaneous poles of the coordinate functions. The remaining one-sided poles force the entire top coefficient to be one linear factor of multiplicity equal to the Fourier degree. Combining the location theorem with this root-collapse theorem produces a constant-angle relation between the boundary tangent and a radial direction, hence circularity. No real-analytic boundary hypothesis is imposed: analyticity follows from the algebraic strong-field parallel curves.

</details>

Abstract: For the nearest-neighbour Edwards--Anderson Ising model on the discrete torus, we prove existence of the quenched thermodynamic limit and almost-sure self-averaging of the free-energy density. The only moment assumption in the main argument is $\E|J|<\infty$, and no symmetry or centering of the coupling law is needed. The proof avoids periodic subadditivity: the wrap-around bonds form a surface-order perturbation, while a tiling argument and the strong law of large numbers give the free-boundary limit. We also obtain the quantitative bounds $(O(L^{-1})$ for the disorder-averaged finite-volume correction. A precise common probability space is specified, since an almost-sure statement across volumes is otherwise not well defined.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Let $M^n$ be a closed minimal hypersurface of the unit sphere with constant squared norm $S$ of the second fundamental form. The proof combines a symmetrized Hessian estimate, an integral identity for a mixed moment, and a weighted estimate for the repeated-index components of the covariant derivative of the second fundamental form. An exact polynomial argument treats the entire excluded interval. We also prove an explicit positive lower bound for the average of the normalized quadratic defect $P/S^2$, where $P=f_4-f_3^2/S-S^2/n$. Abstract: The completeness properties of spaces of immersed curves endowed with Sobolev-type reparametrization-invariant Riemannian metrics have been studied extensively in recent years. In this work, we characterize the metric completion of the space of real-valued immersed open curves with respect to these metrics. We show that the set of additional limit points in the completion is homeomorphic to $H^n$-diffeomorphisms of a closed interval. This result answers a question of the structure of the completion, posed in (Bauer et al., Ann. Sc. Norm. Super. Pisa Cl. Sci. Abstract: We find Sasaki-Einstein metrics on rational homology $(4n-1)$-spheres for $n>1$ built from cyclic polynomials of index 1 cutting out rational varieties. The Einstein metrics found here are inequivalent to the ones found by Boyer and Galicki in arXiv:math/0311355. Our findings are consequence of an improvement, for hypersurfaces defined by cycle polynomials, on the estimate given by Johnson and Kollár to determine Kähler-Einstein orbifold metrics. We also construct weighted hypersurfaces that contain the rational varieties described above as codimension two subvarieties and, due to the refined estimate for cyclic polynomials, we find conditions on the weights and degrees of these hypersurfaces so their corresponding smooth links admit Sasaki-Einstein metrics. Finally we study the effect of the Berglund-Hübsch transpose rule on the topology and on the existence of Sasaki-Einstein metrics on the links studied and generalize all the results given in arXiv:2311.15998 for rational homology 7-spheres to rational homology $(4n-1)$-spheres, that is, we show invariance of these two features under the transpose rule.

</details>

Abstract: We study the radical and screen distributions of a lightlike hypersurface of an indefinite statistical manifold with respect to the induced connections arising from the ambient dual affine connections. Necessary and sufficient conditions are obtained for each distribution to be parallel with respect to both induced connections. These conditions are expressed through the screen shape operators, the local screen fundamental forms, the mean induced connection, and the tangential difference tensor. When both distributions are parallel, we characterize the screen projection and obtain a block decomposition of the tangential difference tensor, whose mixed radical-screen part vanishes. We also obtain equivalent conditions for the induced connections to be metric connections. In the metric case, the induced screen connections coincide with the Levi--Civita connection on each integral manifold of the screen distribution, and the curvature tensors of the three induced connections agree on screen directions. We further determine the action of these curvature tensors on the radical direction. An explicit example shows that both distributions may be parallel although neither induced connection is a metric connection and their curvature tensors need not vanish.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将欧氏空间中的quadrature-surface自由边界问题推广到紧致有限维黎曼流形，需要在流形框架下建立合适的容许域类，并克服曲率与度量带来的技术困难，以刻画自由边界并得到解的存在性条件。

Method: 把问题表述为形状优化问题；构建基于一致Riemannian $RC$-$GNP$条件的本征容许域类；证明容许域的紧性以及Dirichlet问题在强$RC$-$GNP$收敛下的稳定性（状态在$W^{1,p}$中强收敛，$p$-torsional能量连续）；在附加边界正则性下推导形状泛函一阶变分，得到自由边界Euler--Lagrange条件；建立接触最优性不等式并证明切向接触处第二基本形式的黎曼比较。

Result: 得到自由边界Euler--Lagrange条件 $|\nabla_g u_\Omega|_g^p=\sigma H_{\partial\Omega}+k^p$；建立接触最优性不等式；证明切向接触处第二基本形式的Riemannian比较；给出参考域上排除接触并保证自由边界问题有解的充分条件；在round sphere的测地球上给出显式径向例子。

Conclusion: 该工作为黎曼流形上quadrature-surface自由边界问题提供了系统的形状优化框架与存在性理论，所得Euler--Lagrange条件、接触最优性不等式和排除接触的充分条件将欧氏结果推广至流形情形，并通过球面测地球上的径向算例加以验证。

Abstract: We study a quadrature-surface free boundary problem driven by the Riemannian $p$-Laplacian on a smooth compact finite-dimensional Riemannian manifold. We formulate the problem as a shape optimization problem and develop an intrinsic admissible class based on a uniform Riemannian $RC$-$GNP$ condition~\cite{DS3}. We establish compactness of admissible domains and stability of the associated Dirichlet problems under strong $RC$-$GNP$ convergence. In particular, the states converge strongly in $W^{1,p}$ and the associated $p$-torsional energy is continuous. Under additional boundary regularity, we derive the first variation of the shape functional and obtain the free-boundary Euler--Lagrange condition \[ |\nabla_g u_Ω|_g^p=σH_{\partialΩ}+k^p. \] We also formulate the contact optimality inequality, prove the Riemannian comparison of second fundamental forms at tangential contact, and obtain a sufficient condition on a reference domain which rules out contact and yields a solution of the free-boundary problem. Explicit radial examples on geodesic balls of the round sphere are included.

</details>


### [77] [Rigidity and Non-existence Results of $λ$-Translating Solitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18064)
*Li Xiang,Sun Jun*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文研究 $\mathbb{R}^{n+1}$ 中的 $\lambda$-平移孤子（满足 $H=\langle \mathbf{T},\nu\rangle+\lambda$，即关于对数线性密度 $e^{\langle T,X\rangle}$ 具有常加权平均曲率，是带常力项的平均曲率流的永恒解）。主要证明：在 $\lambda>0$ 且 $\inf_\Sigma H>\lambda$ 时，完备真浸入 $\lambda$-平移孤子至少具有指数体积增长；并给出图形 $\lambda$-平移孤子（$\lambda\geqslant 0$）在梯度有界下的尖锐不存在性结果及两个刚性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平移孤子是平均曲率流的自相似解，是研究奇点的重要模型。经典平移孤子（$\lambda=0$）体积呈线性增长，而 $\lambda$-平移孤子对应带常力项的平均曲率流及加权平均曲率流，其几何与增长性质尚不明确。本文旨在刻画 $\lambda$-平移孤子的体积增长、分类图形解并建立刚性结果，以填补该方向的理论空白。

Method: 从 $\lambda$-平移孤子的方程 $H=\langle \mathbf{T},\nu\rangle+\lambda$ 出发，结合加权平均曲率的变分结构（密度 $e^{\langle T,X\rangle}$）。利用几何测度论与椭圆型方程方法，对完备真浸入情形分析体积增长（指数增长），对图形解在梯度有界条件下进行单调性与极值原理分析，从而得到尖锐不存在性与刚性定理。

Result: 1）证明：任意完备真浸入 $\lambda$-平移孤子，当 $\lambda>0$ 且 $\inf_\Sigma H>\lambda$ 时，其体积至少呈指数增长，与普通平移孤子的线性增长形成对比；2）对梯度有界的图形 $\lambda$-平移孤子（$\lambda\geqslant 0$）给出尖锐的不存在性结果；3）证明两个刚性定理。

Conclusion: $\lambda$-平移孤子与经典平移孤子在体积增长和图形解分类上存在本质差异：正 $\lambda$ 与平均曲率下界共同强制指数体积增长，而梯度有界图形解在给定条件下不存在，刚性结果则说明满足特定条件的 $\lambda$-平移孤子只能是已知的标准解。

Abstract: A $λ$-translating soliton is a hypersurface in $\mathbb{R}^{n+1}$ satisfying $H=\langle \mathbf{T},ν\rangle+λ$; equivalently, it has constant weighted mean curvature with respect to the log-linear density $e^{\langle T,X\rangle}$, and is an eternal solution of the mean curvature flow with a constant forcing term. In this paper, we first prove that every complete properly immersed $λ$-translating soliton with $λ>0$ and $\inf_ΣH>λ$ has at least exponential volume growth, in contrast with the linear growth of ordinary translating solitons. We then prove sharp non-existence results for graphic $λ$-translating solitons ($λ\geqslant 0$) with bounded gradient, and two rigidity theorems.

</details>


### [78] [Generalized Einstein Laurent polynomials, Toric Kähler-Einstein Rigidity, and Finite Exponential Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18067)
*Shaosai Huang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文刻画了满足 $\det\nabla^2\psi=C\exp(\langle b,\theta\rangle-\lambda\psi)$ 的正有限指数和对数 $\psi$，并给出双变量 Laurent 多项式、紧致环面 Kähler--Einstein 度量及有限支撑指数族三类分类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该方程同时是射影空间指数映射诱导度量的 Kähler--Einstein 方程，也是自然指数族中 Jeffreys 先验为 Diaconis--Ylvisaker 共轭先验的条件；作者希望统一分类满足该广义 Einstein 条件的对象，并解决相关猜想与问题。

Method: 通过研究正有限指数和的对数 $\psi$ 所满足的 Monge--Ampère 型方程，结合 Laurent 多项式的单位模支撑分类、光滑紧致环面流形的射影诱导 Kähler--Einstein 度量分析，以及无格点或理性假设下有限支撑指数族的仿射不变量刻画。

Result: 第一，双变量单位模支撑 Laurent 多项式若满足 di Scala--Sombra 广义 Einstein 条件，则在单位与单项式坐标变换下为仿射三项式之幂或两个独立二项式幂的乘积；第二，任意维光滑紧致环面流形若具射影诱导 Kähler--Einstein 度量，则为 Fubini--Study 度量的匹配倍数下射影空间之积，并由完全 Veronese--Segre 系统在自同构意义下嵌入；第三，有限支撑指数族满足该方程时，在仿射统计量变换下恰为具有相同类别与试验比的多项分布族之积。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了此类度量的紧致环面情形下的齐性猜想，以及 Manno--Salis 猜想的固定点芽与单值形式，并解决了 Casalis 问题的有限支撑情形。

Abstract: We study when the logarithm $ψ$ of a positive finite exponential sum on $\mathbb{R}^d$ satisfies $\det\nabla^2ψ=C\exp(\langle b,θ\rangle-λψ)$. This is the Kähler--Einstein equation for metrics induced by exponential maps into projective space and, for natural exponential families, the condition that the Jeffreys prior be a Diaconis--Ylvisaker conjugate prior. First, we classify the bivariate Laurent polynomials with unimodular support satisfying the generalized Einstein condition of Di Scala and Sombra: up to units and monomial changes of coordinates, they are powers of an affine trinomial or products of powers of two independent binomials. Second, we show in every dimension that a smooth compact toric manifold with a projectively induced Kähler--Einstein metric is a product of projective spaces with matched multiples of the Fubini--Study metrics, immersed by a complete Veronese--Segre system up to automorphisms. This proves the compact toric case of the homogeneity conjecture for such metrics and the fixed-point germ and univalent forms of a conjecture of Manno and Salis. Third, without lattice or rationality assumptions, the finite-support exponential families satisfying the equation are, up to affine changes of statistic, exactly the products of multinomial families with a common ratio of categories to trials; this settles the finite-support case of a question of Casalis.

</details>


### [79] [Global Metrizability on Sprays of Scalar Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18292)
*Xingzhi Duanmu,Guojun Yang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量曲率 spray 是一类重要的 spray，包含所有二维 spray，其可度量性判别具有基本意义。本文旨在探讨在曲率与拓扑条件下，特定 spray 的全局可度量性，即局部可度量性何时能提升为全局性质。

Method: 在曲率条件（标量曲率、Ricci 曲率几乎处处非零）与拓扑条件（第一 de Rham 上同调群平凡）下，对 n≥3 维流形上的正则 spray 进行全局与局部可度量性的等价性论证；并对二维奇异 Berwald spray 进行刻画。此外通过具体构造，在柱面 S¹×R 上给出局部可度量但非全局可度量的反例族，以及圆柱面、球面上具特殊性质的二维 spray 实例。

Result: 对 n≥3 维、第一 de Rham 上同调群平凡的流形 M 上具标量曲率且 Ricci 曲率几乎处处非零的正则 spray **G**，证明了 **G** 全局可度量当且仅当局部可度量；刻画了同调平凡流形上某类二维奇异 Berwald spray 的全局可度量性；在 S¹×R 上构造了局部可度量而非全局可度量的奇异 Berwald spray 族。

Conclusion: 第一 de Rham 上同调群的平凡性以及几乎处处非零的 Ricci 曲率，是局部可度量性提升为全局可度量性的关键条件；当上同调群非平凡时该性质可能失效，并可由柱面上的构造加以体现。

Abstract: Sprays of scalar curvature constitute an important class of sprays, and such a class includes all two-dimensional sprays. In this paper, we consider the global metrizability of certain sprays on a manifold under some curvature and topological conditions. We prove that, for a regular spray {\bf G} of scalar curvature with almost everywhere nonzero Ricci curvature on a manifold $M$ of dimension $n\ge 3$, {\bf G} is globally metrizable if and only if it is locally metrizable, provided that $M$ has trivial first de Rham cohomology group. Further, we characterize the global metizability of a class of two-dimensional singular Berwald sprays on a manifold with trivial first de Rham cohomology group. Meanwhile, on a cylinder $S^1\times {\bf R}$ (with non-trivial first de Rham cohomology group), we construct a family of singular Berwald sprays which are locally metrizable but not globally metrizable. Finally, we construct some examples of two-dimensional sprays on cylinders or spheres with special properties.

</details>


### [80] [Harmonic Maps from Punctured Riemann Surfaces to the Hyperbolic Plane with Prescribed Scherk Asymptotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18297)
*Qiongling Li,Jinsong Liu,Yihui Xu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在带反全纯对合与给定穿孔的紧Riemann面上，构造以指定Scherk映射为渐近行为的调和映射到双曲平面，其像在各端趋于理想多边形，并覆盖水平悬链面投影所得调和映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究紧Riemann面穿孔后到双曲平面的调和映射及其在穿孔处的渐近行为，并与H^2×R中水平悬链面的H^2分量建立联系。

Method: 设X为g≥0紧Riemann面，具非空不动点集的反全纯对合ι，D⊂Fix(ι)。在每个穿孔处给定偶次、负首项系数的实多项式二次微分，对应从C到H^2中理想多边形内部的Scherk映射（其Hopf微分为该多项式）。据此构造h:X\D→H^2，使其在每个穿孔处的渐近行为与给定Scherk映射一致。

Result: 构造出调和映射h:X\D→H^2，其在每个穿孔附近匹配预定Scherk映射的渐近行为；特别地，h的像在每个端附近趋于一个理想多边形。

Conclusion: 所得h覆盖由H^2×R中水平悬链面取H^2因子所得到的调和映射，从而将Scherk型渐近与水平悬链面的调和投影统一起来。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a genus $g\geq 0$ compact Riemann surface that admits an antiholomorphic involution $ι$ with non empty fixed-point set, and let $D = \{p_1,\dots,p_k\}\subset \text{Fix}(ι)$. At each puncture, we prescribe a Scherk map associated to a given real polynomial quadratic differential with even degree and negative leading coefficient. Here a Scherk map is the harmonic diffeomorphism from $\mathbb{C}$ to the interior of an ideal polygon in $\mathbb{H}^2$, whose Hopf differential is the given polynomial. We construct a harmonic map
  \[
  h:X\backslash D \to \mathbb{H}^2
  \]
  whose asymptotic behavior at each puncture matches the prescribed Scherk map. In particular, the image of $h$ tends to an ideal polygon near each end. The resulting $h$ covers harmonic maps obtained by taking the $\mathbb{H}^2$ factors of horizontal catenoids in $\mathbb{H}^2 \times \mathbb{R}$.

</details>

*Yuyue Chen,Shiying Wu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明严格凸闭曲面沿平均曲率流演化时Willmore亏量的晚期单调性，细化Huisken的指数收敛结果，并给出Hawking质量的最终非减性；在三维空间形式中讨论全脐解，单调方向依赖环境截面曲率符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Huisken已给出Willmore亏量沿平均曲率流的指数收敛，但该结论尚未刻画亏量随时间演化的单调性。本文旨在强化这一收敛性结果，并由此导出Hawking质量的晚期单调行为，同时考察空间形式中全脐解的情形。

Method: 对R^3中光滑闭嵌入严格凸曲面沿平均曲率流作晚期渐近分析，证明Willmore亏量∫ΣH²dμ−16π的单调性；借助该单调性推导Hawking质量的最终非减性；对三维空间形式中的全脐解，按环境截面曲率符号分类讨论Willmore能量与Hawking质量的单调方向。

Result: 获得了Willmore亏量沿平均曲率流的晚期单调性，细化了Huisken的指数收敛；由此推出Hawking质量最终非减；在三维空间形式中，全脐解下Willmore能量与Hawking质量的单调方向取决于环境截面曲率的符号。

Conclusion: Willmore亏量的晚期单调性统一并强化了已有的收敛性刻画，并为Hawking质量的晚期单调性提供了新途径；在空间形式中，单调方向由截面曲率符号决定，R^3情形（曲率为零）对应所得的非减结论。

Abstract: We prove a late-time monotonicity of the Willmore deficit \(\int_ΣH^2\,dμ-16π\) along the mean curvature flow of smooth closed embedded strictly convex surfaces in \(\mathbb R^3\),which refining the exponential convergence given by Huisken. As a consequence, the Hawking mass is eventually non--decreasing along mean curvature flow. We also discuss totally umbilical solutions in three-dimensional space forms, where the monotonicity directions of the Willmore energy and the Hawking mass depend on the sign of the ambient sectional curvature.

</details>


### [82] [On the existence of coupled extremal Kähler metrics on ruled surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18418)
*Ramesh Mete*

Abstract: We investigate the existence conditions for coupled extremal Kähler metrics on minimal ruled surfaces over a genus 2 Riemann surface. Using the Calabi ansatz to reduce the coupled extremal equations to ordinary differential equations, we prove that a pair of coupled extremal metrics exists for normalized Kähler classes $Ω_a$ and $Ω_b$ if and only if their parameters $(a, b)$ belong to an explicitly defined open region $\mathcal{S}_{\mathrm{ext}}$ in the positive real quadrant. Furthermore, we show that this existence region inherently contains the diagonal segment corresponding to classical extremal Kähler metrics.

We also derive bounded-gradient strictly plurisubharmonic exhaustions and uniform holomorphic kernel estimates for complete $U(n)$-invariant Kähler metrics on $\mathbb{C}^n$ with nonnegative bisectional curvature.

Abstract: We study Hamiltonian stationary Lagrangian surfaces in complex space forms with nowhere-zero mean curvature vector $H$. We prove that if the Gaussian curvature is constant and $\grad|H|$ has positive constant length, then both the surface and the ambient space are flat. Moreover, the immersion is locally congruent to the known surface obtained as the product of a Cornu spiral and its reflection.

Method: 在S\D上构造沿canonical foliation叶平坦的Hermitian度量，研究其Chern-Ricci流演化。对Enoki曲面构造immortal正规化解；对intermediate type Kato曲面，利用affine Green model及其有限指标扩展，在假设0<μ<2下构造显式正规化解。

Abstract: Let $S$ be a Kato surface and $D$ its maximal reduced divisor of rational curves. On $S\setminus D$ we construct Hermitian metrics which are flat along the leaves of the canonical foliation and study their evolution under the Chern-Ricci flow. For Enoki surfaces, we construct an immortal normalised solution which, on every compact sublevel of the natural exhaustion, converges in the Gromov-Hausdorff sense to the elliptic base endowed with an explicit flat metric. For Kato surfaces of intermediate type, the affine Green model and its finite-index extension give, under the assumption $0<μ<2$, an explicit normalised solution which, on every compact Green block, collapses in the Gromov-Hausdorff sense to a circle. In the Enoki case the limiting area is $2πb_2(S)$; in the intermediate case the length of the limiting circle is determined by the Green and leafwise monodromies.

</details>

<details>

Method: 在加权体积测度e^{-f}dv下研究热核H_f，建立一系列基本估计；证明H_f与Ricci收缩子诱导的Ricci流时空热核H(x,t;y,s)的等价性；借助该等价关系将Ricci流分析工具（如Nash熵、约化距离）推广至Ricci收缩子度量测度空间。

Result: 建立了H_f的基本估计；澄清了H_f与Ricci流时空热核的等价性；成功将Nash熵与约化距离等工具推广至Ricci收缩子度量测度空间；作为直接应用，证明若|∇R|=o(f^{3/2})，则R=o(f)。

Conclusion: Ricci收缩子的加权热核理论与Ricci流时空热核存在深刻等价性，使得Ricci流的分析工具可迁移至收缩子度量测度空间，并由此获得曲率衰减的定量结果。

Abstract: As a metric measure space possessing positive Bakry-Émery curvature, a Ricci shrinker with potential function $f$ admits a heat kernel $H_f$ under the weighted volume measure $e^{-f}dv$. In this paper, we study $H_f$ systematically and establish a series of fundamental estimates. Moreover, we clarify the equivalence between $H_f$ and the spacetime heat kernel $H(x,t;y,s)$ under Ricci flow induced by a Ricci shrinker. Inspired by this relation, we extend corresponding analysis tools of Ricci flows to Ricci shrinker metric measure spaces, such as Nash entropy and reduced distance. As a direct application, we prove that $\left|\nabla R\right|=o(f^{3/2})$ implies $R=o(f)$.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We introduce a codimension one construction of coarse Ricci curvature in Lorentzian geometry. Using the $1$-Lorentz-Wasserstein distance $\ell_1$, we compare probability measures supported on small spacelike hypersurfaces through nearby events and recover, in a precise asymptotic regime, the ambient Ricci curvature in future-directed unit timelike directions, with a universal dimensional prefactor reflecting this codimension one construction.
  The construction echoes the Raychaudhuri equation, which relates the evolution of spatial volume expansion along timelike geodesics to Ricci curvature. Our quantitative estimates rely on transport maps with controlled displacement, constructed through a Moser-type flow.
  At leading order, the slice construction is insensitive to smooth weights. By smearing the spacelike slices into thin timelike tubes and calibrating the temporal and spatial scales, we recover the timelike Bakry-Émery tensor $\mathrm{Ric}+\mathrm{Hess}\,V$ associated with the weighted reference measure $\smash{\mathfrak{m}=\mathrm{e}^{-V}\,\mathrm{vol}_g}$.

</details>


### [88] [Morse resolution of mean curvature flows with cylindrical singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18687)
*Richard H. Bamler,Felix Schulze,Lu Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明具有单重柱状切流的紧致平均曲率流存在光滑Morse分解，且不加非退化或孤立性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均曲率流在有限时间会产生奇点，其解的结构难以用经典微分拓扑工具（如Morse理论）直接刻画。此前结果通常需要非退化或孤立性假设，限制了适用性。作者希望在奇点仅具单重柱状切流这一几何条件下，无需附加假设即建立流的Morse分解。

Method: 构造紧致平均曲率流的光滑Morse分解：在奇点集的任意给定邻域外，分解与流的时空轨迹一致；在邻域内通过改造（如平滑时间到达函数）得到光滑函数，使其临界点具有预期指标。平均凸情形通过对整体到达函数进行平滑实现。

Result: 定理成立：单重柱状切流条件下紧致平均曲率流存在光滑Morse分解；分解在奇点集任意邻域外与时空轨迹一致，所有临界点指标符合预期，且无需非退化或孤立性假设。

Conclusion: 该结果为平均曲率流奇点提供了一个稳健的Morse理论框架，弱化了以往假设，并将其推广到平均凸情形下到达函数的平滑处理。

Abstract: We show that a compact mean curvature flow whose singularities have multiplicity-one cylindrical tangent flows admits a smooth Morse resolution. The resolution agrees with the spacetime track outside any prescribed neighborhood of its singular set and all its critical points have the expected index. No nondegeneracy or isolatedness assumption is required. In the mean-convex case the result follows by smoothing the global arrival function.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 在紧致带边 $V$-静态流形上，利用 $V$-静态方程与势函数的结构性质，推导 Laplace 算子第一 Dirichlet 特征值的精确表达式；通过积分恒等式与比较论证建立第一 Steklov 特征值及四阶 Steklov 谱的尖锐估计；结合 Bochner 型公式给出漂移 Laplacian 第一特征值的 Lichnerowicz 型下界，并分析等号成立条件。

Conclusion: 本文系统刻画了紧致 $V$-静态流形的谱性质，所得估计的尖锐性由等号情形的刚性定理保证，表明欧氏球与半球在相应谱极值问题中具有特征性地位。

Abstract: In this article, we investigate spectral properties of compact $V$-static manifolds, namely, compact manifolds with boundary whose metrics are critical points of the volume functional under a scalar curvature constraint. We establish an exact relation for the first Dirichlet eigenvalue of the Laplace operator, and we derive sharp estimates for the first Steklov eigenvalue and the entire fourth-order Steklov spectrum. We further obtain a Lichnerowicz-type lower bound for the first eigenvalue of the drifted Laplacian naturally associated with the $V$-static potential. In the corresponding equality cases, we obtain rigidity results characterizing the Euclidean ball and the hemisphere.

Motivation: 在三维中研究Z/2调和1-形式的构造问题，理解其奇异集结构与monodromy轨迹的拓扑实现，并探索其在一般闭三维流形上的存在性。

Method: 通过变形背景度量来构造Z/2调和1-形式；在R^3上给出具体例子；将有限图实现为B^3上Z/2调和1-形式的monodromy轨迹；构造临界Z/2-eigensection的去奇异化模型，使其在紧集外与齐次模型一致。

Result: R^3上构造出光滑轨迹在奇异集中的补集为Cantor集的例子；每个每顶点正偶价的有限图可实现为B^3上Z/2调和1-形式的monodromy轨迹；为每个临界Z/2-eigensection构造了去奇异化模型；在每个闭连通可定向三维流形上，对合适光滑度量构造了非退化Z/2调和1-形式。

Conclusion: 通过变形背景度量可有效构造三维Z/2调和1-形式，其奇异结构可呈现Cantor型、可与任意正偶价有限图的monodromy轨迹对应，并可在所有闭连通可定向三维流形上以非退化形式存在。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop methods for constructing $\mathbb{Z}/2$-harmonic 1-forms in dimension three by varying the background metric. On $\mathbb{R}^3$, we construct an example for which the complement of the smooth locus in the singular set is a Cantor set. We realize every finite graph with positive even valence at each vertex as the monodromy locus of a $\mathbb{Z}/2$-harmonic 1-form on $B^3$. We also construct desingularization models for every critical $\mathbb{Z}/2$-eigensection, agreeing exactly with the associated homogeneous model outside a compact set. Finally, we construct a nondegenerate $\mathbb{Z}/2$-harmonic 1-form on every closed connected oriented three-manifold for a suitable smooth metric.

</details>


### [93] [Maximal symmetry rank and almost non-negative curvature in low dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19110)
*Samuel Bartel*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 建立4至9维闭单连通几乎非负曲率流形对称秩上界，并在4、5、6维给出最大对称秩情形下的等变微分同胚分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究几乎非负曲率流形的对称性限制，将对称秩的曲率-对称性关系推广到较弱曲率条件下，并探讨最大对称性时的分类问题。

Method: 通过证明对称秩的上界，并在达到最大对称秩时利用等变微分同胚方式对4、5、6维流形进行刻画与分类。

Result: 得到4至9维闭单连通几乎非负曲率流形对称秩的上界；在4、5、6维最大对称秩情形下完成等变微分同胚分类。

Conclusion: 几乎非负曲率条件下对称秩受维数限制，且最大对称秩流形在低维（4、5、6维）具有明确的等变微分同胚类型。

Abstract: We establish an upper bound for the symmetry rank of closed, simply connected, almost non-negatively curved manifolds of dimension 4 through 9 and in the case of maximal symmetry rank provide an equivariant diffeomorphism classification of this class of manifolds in dimensions 4, 5, and 6.

</details>


### [94] [Finite index constant mean curvature hypersurfaces in space forms of dimension at most seven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19120)
*Zihao Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文研究单连通空间形式中余维一常平均曲率(CMC)完备双侧超曲面在有限Morse指标下的紧性。球面情形浸入区域紧；欧氏空间中非紧例子均为极小；双曲空间(H=-1)中在显式依赖于维数的阈值下紧，从曲面的H²>1到六维的H²≥5/3。结论对体积约束指标亦成立，无需properness、体积增长或曲率界假设。核心工具为Green函数论证，组合八组恒等式的显式有理组合与Hong-Li-Wang稳定Bernstein定理。稳定双曲管表明不存在独立于维数的阈值保证全维紧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有限指标CMC超曲面的刚性/紧性现象，并厘清其在球面、欧氏与双曲空间中的差异。此前工作多依赖properness、体积增长或曲率界等假设，本文旨在去掉这些假设并给出显式的、依赖维数的平均曲率阈值。

Method: 采用Green函数论述：构造八组恒等式的显式有理组合，结合Hong-Li-Wang的稳定Bernstein定理，推导有限Morse指标CMC超曲面的结构性质；方法同样适用于体积约束指标。

Result: 球面中浸入区域紧；欧氏空间中所有非紧双侧CMC有限指标超曲面均为极小；双曲空间(H=-1)中当维数2≤n≤6时在显式阈值下紧：n=2需H²>1，逐渐过渡到n=6时H²≥5/3。结论无需properness、体积增长或曲率界假设。稳定双曲管的例子显示不存在与维数无关的阈值可在全维数下保证紧性。

Conclusion: 有限指标CMC超曲面的紧性在球面、欧氏、双曲三种空间形式中呈现不同特征；双曲情形存在依赖维数的显式H阈值，且该维度依赖性本质上不可避免（由稳定双曲管验证）。结果统一并推广了既有工作，去除了额外假设。

Abstract: We study complete two-sided constant-mean-curvature hypersurfaces of dimensions $2\le n\le6$ and finite Morse index in simply connected space forms. In the round sphere the immersed domain is compact; in Euclidean space every noncompact example is minimal; in hyperbolic space of curvature $-1$ we obtain compactness under explicit dimension-dependent mean-curvature thresholds, from $H^2>1$ for surfaces to $H^2\ge5/3$ in dimension six. The conclusions also hold for the volume-constrained index, without properness, volume-growth, or curvature-bound assumptions. A common Green-function argument uses explicit rational combinations of eight identities and the stable Bernstein theorem of Hong-Li-Wang. Stable hyperbolic tubes show that no mean-curvature threshold independent of dimension can give compactness in all dimensions.

</details>

### [95] [Skein theory, line defects, and quantum symmetric pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18902)
*Eric Yen-Yo Chen,David Jordan,Iordanis Romaidis*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 该文为带嵌入线缺陷的3-流形构建skein理论，以ribbon张量范畴及其balanced braided模范畴为出发点，研究量子对称对产生的线缺陷，证明缺陷skein模的有限性，并在特定情形下建立Z_2-等变skein理论与缺陷skein理论的等价，给出AIII型C^∨C DAHA模族的skein理论构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将skein理论从无缺陷流形推广到含线缺陷的3-流形，以便用范畴化代数工具处理量子对称对相关的缺陷，并把等变skein理论与缺陷skein理论联系起来，进而为DAHA模提供拓扑构造。

Method: 以ribbon张量范畴及其balanced braided模范畴为输入数据，构造含嵌入线缺陷的3-流形skein理论；聚焦量子对称对诱导的线缺陷，证明相关缺陷skein模的有限性；再通过考察Z_2-等变skein理论，在特定设定下证明其与缺陷skein理论等价。

Result: 证明了量子对称对诱导线缺陷的缺陷skein模的有限性；在特定情形下建立了Z_2-等变skein理论与缺陷skein理论的等价；由此在AIII型情形构造出一族C^∨C DAHA模。

Conclusion: 缺陷skein理论为量子对称对与DAHA模提供了统一的拓扑范畴框架，等变与缺陷两种skein理论在特定条件下可互相转化，并可用于构造新的DAHA模族。

Abstract: We construct skein theory for 3-manifolds with embedded line defects, starting from the data of a ribbon tensor category and its balanced braided module category. We focus on line defects arising from quantum symmetric pairs, and prove finiteness properties for their defect skein modules. As an application, we consider $\mathbf{Z}_2$-equivariant skein theory and establish an equivalence with defect skein theory in certain settings, leading to a skein theoretical construction of a family of $\mathrm{C}^\vee\mathrm{C}$ DAHA-modules in the Type AIII case.

</details>


### [96] [Qudit stabilizers beyond the free case and the twisted Kitaev model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29896)
*Ruslan Maksimau*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文研究任意维度 qudit 的稳定子形式体系，证明自由情形下基本定理仍成立，并进一步去除自由性假设，给出一般情形下受保护空间与正规化子商的结构刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典稳定子形式体系多针对 qubit（d=2）或假设稳定子子群自由，本文旨在将理论推广到任意维度 d 的 qudit，并处理非自由稳定子子群的一般情形。

Method: 采用代数方法，统一处理：先分析 H 作为 Z/dZ-模自由且不含非平凡标量的自由情形；再去除自由性假设，研究一般结构；并刻画 shifted 自由情形。

Result: 自由情形下，V^H 同构于更少 qudit 的状态空间，N(H)/H 同构于更少 qudit 的 Pauli 群；一般情形下，V^H 为可能更小维度的 qudit 空间的张量积，N(H)/H 为相应 qudit Pauli 群在公共中心上的乘积；shifted 自由情形恰为 N(H)/H 仍为普通 qudit Pauli 群的情形。

Conclusion: 该代数框架统一且普适，特别适用于 qudit Kitaev 模型及其 shifted 与 twisted 变体。

Abstract: We study the stabiliser formalism for qudits of arbitrary dimension $d$. In the free case, we show that the basic theorem of the stabiliser formalism remains valid: if the stabiliser subgroup $H$ is free as a $Z/dZ$-module and contains no non-trivial scalars, then the protected space $V^H$ is naturally identified with the state space of a smaller number of qudits of the same dimension, and the quotient $N(H)/H$ is identified with the Pauli group on a smaller number of qudits.
  We then remove the freeness assumption and describe the resulting structure in general. In this case, the protected space is identified with a tensor product of qudit spaces of possibly smaller dimensions, and the quotient $N(H)/H$ is described by a corresponding product of qudit Pauli groups, possibly of smaller dimensions, over a common center. We also characterise the shifted free case, which is exactly the situation in which $N(H)/H$ is again an ordinary qudit Pauli group. Our approach is algebraic and uniform, and applies in particular to the qudit Kitaev model and to its shifted and twisted variants.

</details>

### [97] [The Kerr/CFT energy gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17656)
*Robert Penna*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用Kinnersley-Kelley单参数推广解（在NHEK与解析延拓Minkowski之间插值）作为参考度规，计算NHEK的共形能量，并讨论Minkowski真空共形能量中的极限次序问题及其修正方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Noether荷在引力中必须相对于某参考度规定义，但NHEK解缺乏显然的参考度规选择（如它并非渐近平坦）。因此需要寻找合适的参考度规来计算NHEK的共形能量。

Method: 采用Kinnersley与Kelley给出的NHEK单参数推广解，该解族在NHEK与解析延拓的Minkowski时空之间插值；以此插值解族作为Minkowski参考度规，计算NHEK的共形能量，并分析极限次序问题。

Result: 计算Minkowski真空的共形能量时出现有趣的极限次序（order of limits）问题；提出若干自然的修正方案，其中包括一个受Kerr/CFT对应启发的处方。

Conclusion: 该插值解族为NHEK提供了可行的参考度规，使共形能量可被定义；极限次序问题需通过特定处方固定，Kerr/CFT对应可为此提供动机。

Abstract: Noether charges in gravity are always defined with respect to a reference metric, but the near-horizon extremal Kerr (NHEK) solution does not come with an obvious choice of reference metric (it is not asymptotically flat, for example). We discuss a one parameter generalization of the NHEK solution due to Kinnersley and Kelley which interpolates between NHEK and an analytically continued version of Minkowski spacetime. We use this interpolating family of solutions to compute the conformal energy of NHEK above the Minkowski reference metric. We find an interesting order of limits issue when computing the conformal energy of the Minkowski vacuum and we discuss a few natural prescriptions for fixing it, including one motivated by the Kerr/CFT correspondence.

</details>


### [98] [Auxiliary Field Deformations of the Lambda Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18831)
*Christian Ferko,Cian Luke Martin,Pranat Sharma*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将辅助场形变范式应用于lambda模型，构造出其可积形变并验证其保持可积结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多可积sigma模型存在通过耦合辅助场（其运动方程为代数方程）得到的可积形变族。作者希望将这一范式推广到种子理论为lambda模型的情形，因为lambda模型在WZW模型与主手征模型的非阿贝尔T对偶之间插值，具有特殊地位。

Method: 以lambda模型为种子理论，引入辅助场进行形变；显式构造Lax联络，使得在施加代数场方程后其平坦性等价于剩余运动方程；并对形变理论进行哈密顿分析，验证其满足相关定理的前提假设。

Result: 给出了lambda模型的辅助场形变，并成功构造出相应Lax联络；哈密顿分析确认该理论满足arXiv:2605.18213中定理的假设，从而保证模型具有Maillet结构（守恒荷对合）以及经典Yangian。

Conclusion: 辅助场形变改变了lambda模型的经典动力学，但基本保持其可积结构不变，与其它情形一致。

Abstract: Many examples of integrable sigma models admit families of integrable deformations that involve coupling the undeformed ``seed'' theory to auxiliary fields with algebraic equations of motion. In this work, we apply this paradigm to the case where the seed theory is the lambda model, which interpolates between the Wess-Zumino-Witten model and the non-Abelian T-dual of the principal chiral model. We present an auxiliary field deformation of the lambda model and exhibit a Lax connection whose flatness is equivalent to the remaining equations of motion after imposing the algebraic field equations. We also carry out a Hamiltonian analysis of the deformed theory, verifying that it obeys the assumptions of theorems in arXiv:2605.18213 which guarantee both (i) that the model enjoys the Maillet structure that ensures involution of its conserved charges, and (ii) that the theory possesses a classical Yangian. Thus, as in other cases, the auxiliary field deformation of the lambda model changes its classical dynamics while leaving the integrable structure essentially unchanged.

</details>


### [99] [Quantum Dynamics of Probe Particles in Thermal Fields and the Emergence of Stochastic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17657)
*Bruno Scheihing-Hitschfeld*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 从一般哈密顿量出发，推导探针粒子约化密度矩阵的量子主方程、热环境下的平衡条件（实时间线算符的KMS性质）及朗之万描述，具有高度普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探针粒子的能量损失与动量涨落通常依赖具体环境模型假设，缺乏从底层量子场论出发的一般性第一性原理描述，本文旨在摆脱这些特定假设、建立普适框架。

Method: 从描述探针粒子与一组环境自由度相互作用的量子动力学一般哈密顿量出发，推导约化密度矩阵的量子主方程，给出热环境下的平衡条件（以实时间延伸线算符的KMS性质编码），并导出朗之万描述；不预设环境自耦合形式、耦合的环境算符形式、关联函数统计性质、动量转移是否为高斯统计，以及探针粒子是否相对论性运动。要求仅为：时空平移、宇称与时间反演是哈密顿量的对称性，且探针粒子色散关系特征尺度与环境特征尺度存在尺度分离。

Result: 在所述一般条件下得到量子主方程、热平衡条件与朗之万描述，并讨论了高斯布朗运动出现的极限情形。

Conclusion: 本工作构成了第一性原理推导，揭示了底层量子场论的哪些性质决定探针粒子的能量损失与动量涨落。

Abstract: Starting from a general Hamiltonian describing the quantum dynamics of probe particles interacting with a set of environment degrees of freedom, we derive the quantum master equation with which the reduced density matrix of a probe particle evolves, the general equilibration condition that they satisfy when the environment is prepared in a thermal state -- encoded in a KMS property for line operators extended in real time -- and the Langevin description that emerges. The novelty of our results resides in their generality: We do not assume a specific form of the environment self-coupling, of the environment operator that couples the particle to it, of the statistics of its correlation functions, whether the statistics of the momentum transfer is Gaussian or not, or whether the probe particles move relativistically. Our results only require that spacetime translations, parity and time reversal be symmetries of the Hamiltonian, and that there exists a separation of scales between those characterizing the dispersion relation of the probe particle and those of the environment. We also discuss the limiting case in which Gaussian Brownian motion emerges. Overall, our results constitute a first-principles derivation of what properties of the underlying quantum field theory govern the energy loss and momentum fluctuations of probe particles.

</details>


### [100] [Bosonic String Amplitudes From Ribbon Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17660)
*Sam Christian,Ben Mazel,Yuchen Wang,Xi Yin,Yutai Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 基于Kontsevich带状图参数化，发展数值计算高亏格CFT关联函数与弦振幅的方法，算至亏格6的紧致玻色子配分函数及亏格3的玻色弦真空振幅模空间被积函数，通过多项检验并复现亏格2、3的Igusa尖点形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高亏格CFT关联函数与弦振幅的显式计算在解析上极为困难，模空间维数随亏格快速增长，缺乏高效、可系统推广的数值方法。作者旨在借助Kontsevich带状图对带 puncture 黎曼面模空间M_{g,n}的参数化，建立一套可实际执行的高亏格数值计算框架。

Method: 采用Kontsevich对模空间M_{g,n}的带状图（ribbon graph）参数化，将模空间上的积分转化为对带状图的组合求和与数值积分，从而数值计算高亏格CFT关联函数和闭弦振幅。具体应用于紧致玻色子配分函数（至亏格6）与玻色弦真空振幅的模空间被积函数（至亏格3）。

Result: 成功数值计算出亏格最高到6的紧致玻色子配分函数，以及亏格最高到3的玻色弦真空振幅模空间被积函数。结果通过多项非平凡数值检验；其中玻色弦振幅的模空间被积函数在亏格2和亏格3分别复现了相应的Igusa尖点形式（Igusa cusp forms）。

Conclusion: Kontsevich带状图参数化可有效支撑高亏格CFT与弦振幅的高精度数值计算，且结果与已知解析结果（Igusa尖点形式）一致，验证了方法的正确性与实用性，为更高亏格及相关模空间积分的数值研究提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We develop a method for numerically computing higher genus CFT correlators and string amplitudes based on Kontsevich's ribbon graph parametrization of the moduli space $\mathcal{M}_{g,n}$ of punctured Riemann surfaces. Using this method, we compute the compact boson partition function up to genus 6 as well as the moduli space integrand for bosonic string vacuum amplitudes up to genus 3. Our results pass a number of nontrivial numerical checks. In particular, the moduli space integrand of bosonic string amplitudes reproduce the Igusa cusp forms at genera 2 and 3.

</details>


### [101] [Full Eigenstate Thermalization in Quantum Field Theory and Gravitational Scrambling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17663)
*Ricardo Espíndola,Viktor Jahnke*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 利用热解析性，推导连续量子场论中完全本征态热化所涉光滑函数的方向性大频率界；最强方向重现格点系统的标度，完整解析域揭示非对称多频结构，热共形关联子与引力Regge贡献分别饱和相应阶数的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本征态热化（ETH）的定量刻画在格点系统中已有标度结果，但在连续量子场论中其大频率行为与解析结构尚不清晰，需要建立统一的解析性框架来约束ETH光滑函数。

Method: 采用热解析性（thermal analyticity）方法，对进入完全本征态热化的光滑函数推导方向性的大频率上界，并分析其完整解析域的多频结构。

Result: 最强方向的上界重现了此前在格点系统中识别的标度；完整解析域展现出非对称的多频结构。热共形关联子饱和二阶与三阶界，领头引力Regge贡献饱和最强的四阶方向界。

Conclusion: 热解析性为连续量子场论中的ETH提供了方向性大频率约束，且共形与引力计算达到了这些界的饱和，表明该框架与已知的全息及格点结果自洽。

Abstract: Using thermal analyticity, we derive directional large-frequency bounds on the smooth functions entering full eigenstate thermalization in continuum quantum field theory. The strongest directions reproduce the scales previously identified for lattice systems, while the complete analyticity domain reveals an asymmetric multifrequency structure. Thermal conformal correlators saturate the second- and third-order bounds, and the leading gravitational Regge contribution saturates the strongest fourth-order direction.

</details>


### [102] [Full Eigenstate Thermalization in Quantum Field Theory: Free Cumulants and Gravitational Scrambling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17669)
*Ricardo Espíndola,Viktor Jahnke*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 利用热解析性推导连续量子场论中完整本征态热化（full ETH）光滑函数的定向大频率界，最强方向复现晶格系统标度；二维共形场论精确两点、三点函数饱和相应界；四阶全息CFT饱和最强full-ETH界，建立full ETH、自由累积量与引力扰乱之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本征态热化（ETH）是理解孤立量子多体系统热化的核心机制，但已有标度结果多来自晶格系统。本文旨在将full ETH的大频率界推广到连续量子场论，并厘清其与自由概率、引力扰乱（gravitational scrambling）之间的深层关系。

Method: 采用热解析性（thermal analyticity）分析，推导进入full ETH的光滑算符函数的定向大频率界，给出完整解析域下的非对称多频率结构；在二维热共形场论中用精确两点、三点函数检验并饱和对应界；在四阶（首次出现真实动力学信息处）考察全息CFT是否饱和最强full-ETH界。

Result: 最强方向上的界复现了此前在晶格系统中识别的标度；完整解析域揭示出更广的非对称多频率结构；二维热CFT的精确两点、三点函数饱和相应界；四阶处所研究的全息CFT饱和最强full-ETH界。

Conclusion: 结果建立了full ETH、自由累积量（free cumulants）与引力扰乱之间的直接联系，并与引力扰乱代数所实现的渐近自由性（asymptotic freeness）的出现相一致。

Abstract: Using thermal analyticity, we derive directional large-frequency bounds on the smooth functions entering full eigenstate thermalization in continuum quantum field theory. The strongest directions reproduce the scales previously identified for lattice systems, while the complete analyticity domain reveals a broader asymmetric multifrequency structure. In thermal two-dimensional conformal field theory, exact two- and three-point functions saturate the corresponding bounds. At fourth order, where genuine dynamical information first appears, we show that the holographic CFTs studied here saturate the strongest full-ETH bounds. These results establish a direct connection between full ETH, free cumulants, and gravitational scrambling, and are consistent with the emergence of asymptotic freeness realized by the gravitational scrambling algebra.

</details>


### [103] [Representation Theory of Three-dimensional Corner Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17666)
*Giulio Neri,Ludovic Varrin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究三维圆形角点对应的扩展角点对称群的表示论，利用诱导表示构造无限维角点对称群的表示族，分析其酉性与不可约性，确定对称群的最大中心扩张并构造相应射影表示，为三维量子引力角点自由度的系统描述奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角点对称性在量子引力中扮演重要角色，边界自由度被认为在角点变换作用下编码了时空量子结构的某些方面。然而，三维量子引力角点自由度的系统性描述尚不完善，需要从表示论角度建立基础框架。

Method: 采用诱导表示技术，构造与圆形角点相关联的三维扩展角点对称群的表示族；分析这些表示的酉性和不可约性；确定对称群的最大中心扩张，并构造对应的射影表示。

Result: 构造了无限维角点对称群的表示族，刻画了其酉性与不可约性，确定了最大中心扩张，并得到了相应的射影表示。所出现的表示论结构与引力研究中已居核心地位的对称群和量子理论密切相关。

Conclusion: 本工作为三维量子引力角点自由度的系统性描述迈出了第一步，揭示了角点对称群表示论与已有引力相关量子理论之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We study the representation theory of the three-dimensional extended corner symmetry group associated with a circular corner. Using induced representation techniques, we construct families of representations of the infinite-dimensional corner symmetry group and analyze their unitarity and irreducibility. We also determine the maximal central extension of the symmetry group and construct the corresponding projective representations. The analysis is motivated by the role that corner symmetries play in quantum gravity, where boundary degrees of freedom --- acted upon by the corner transformations --- are expected to encode aspects of the quantum structure of spacetime. In three dimensions, the representation-theoretic structures that emerge are closely related to symmetry groups and quantum theories that already appeared as central to the study of gravity. This work provides a first step toward a systematic description of quantum gravitational corner degrees of freedom in three dimensions.

</details>


### [104] [Vacuum Structure of Maximally Supersymmetric Two-Dimensional Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18074)
*Richard Eager*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究二维极大超对称Yang–Mills理论的真空结构：低能下理论分解为由orbifold共形场论与有质量真空组成的超选择扇区，扇区由Lusztig广义Springer对应中的尖点数据标记，暗示该对应可增强为全纯因子化代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解极大超对称Yang–Mills理论在二维低能极限下的真空结构，并寻求其与几何表示论（Lusztig广义Springer对应）之间的深层联系。

Method: 分析二维极大超对称Yang–Mills理论的低能有效理论分解，将其超选择扇区与orbifold共形场论及有质量真空对应，并用Lusztig广义Springer对应中的尖点数据对扇区进行标号。

Result: 理论低能下分解为若干超选择扇区，分别表现为orbifold共形场论与有质量真空；这些扇区由广义Springer对应中的尖点数据分类标定。

Conclusion: 该结构暗示Lusztig广义Springer对应可被提升/增强为全纯因子化代数的框架，从而建立量子场论与几何表示论之间的新联系。

Abstract: We study the vacuum structure of maximally supersymmetric Yang--Mills theory in two dimensions.
