At low energies, the theory decomposes into superselection sectors consisting of orbifold conformal field theories and massive vacua. The sectors are indexed by the cuspidal data appearing in Lusztig's generalized Springer correspondence. This suggests an enhancement of the correspondence to holomorphic factorization algebras.

</details>


### [105] [Fixed Points, Floquet Entanglement Asymmetry, and Quantum Mpemba Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17724)
*Jayashish Das,Filiberto Ares,Arnab Kundu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用基于迭代共形映射（尤其是Möbius映射）的动力系统方法，研究周期驱动二维共形场论（带全局U(1)对称性）中纠缠不对称性的动力学，发现局域对称性恢复、量子Mpemba效应及其逆效应等现象都可由共形映射的不变量数据给出几何描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期驱动二维共形场论中纠缠不对称性的非平衡动力学涉及对称性恢复、量子Mpemba效应等多种现象，已有研究缺乏统一、简洁的组织框架，因此作者希望借助共形映射的动力系统视角为这些现象提供统一的几何解释。

Method: 以由局域算符插入制备的破缺对称性激发态为初态，采用基于迭代共形映射（特别是Möbius映射）的动力系统描述，利用映射的不变量数据（Möbius映射的共轭类结构，即不动点）来刻画动力学，并分析态制备算符插入位置、子系统与不动点相对位置对纠缠不对称性演化的影响。

Result: 在Möbius映射情形下，纠缠不对称性的动力学由其不动点、子系统及态制备算符插入位置的相对关系决定，呈现出增长、衰减、振荡和饱和等不同模式；局域对称性恢复、量子Mpemba效应及其逆效应均可用共形映射的不变量数据简单几何化描述。

Conclusion: 该几何图像可推广至一般全纯映射，表明共形映射动力学是组织周期驱动二维共形场论中纠缠不对称性现象学的自然框架。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of entanglement asymmetry in periodically driven two-dimensional conformal field theories with a global U$(1)$ symmetry, using a dynamical-system description based on iterated conformal maps, in particular Möbius maps. Starting from a symmetry-breaking excited state prepared by a local operator insertion, we show that a broad range of nonequilibrium phenomena, including local symmetry restoration, the quantum Mpemba effect, and its inverse, admit a simple geometric description in terms of the invariant data of the conformal map. We explicitly demonstrate this using Möbius maps, for which the invariant structure is described by the conjugacy classes. In particular, the dynamics of entanglement asymmetry is determined by the relative positions of the state-preparing operator insertion, the subsystem, and the fixed points, yielding distinct patterns of growth, decay, oscillation, and saturation. We further argue that this geometric picture extends to general holomorphic maps, establishing the conformal-map dynamics as a natural framework for organizing the phenomenology of entanglement asymmetry in periodically driven two-dimensional conformal field theories.

</details>


### [106] [Growing Einstein-Rosen Bridge with Multi-partite Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18225)
*Takanori Anegawa,Akihiro Miyata,Shota Suzuki,Kotaro Tamaoka*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用多部分纠缠度（genuine multi-entropy）研究子系统纠缠熵饱和后纠缠是否仍增长；在双侧BTZ黑洞中发现该量随时间线性增长，揭示了普通纠缠熵无法探测的多部分纠缠结构，并细化了ER=EPR关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索子系统纠缠熵饱和后，量子纠缠能否继续增长，以揭示超出双部分纠缠的多部分纠缠结构。

Method: 采用探测多部分纠缠结构的genuine multi-entropy，在双侧BTZ黑洞、自旋链模型、空间分割的SYK模型及Haar随机态中进行研究。

Result: 在双侧BTZ黑洞中，固定三分划的每个子系统熵饱和后，genuine multi-entropy仍随时间线性增长，延长了纠缠探测Einstein-Rosen桥增长的区间；自旋链模型和空间分割SYK模型表现出不同的瞬态行为，而全息、SYK模型和Haar随机态的晚期平台值具有普适行为。

Conclusion: 结果通过识别普通纠缠熵不可见的多部分结构，细化了ER=EPR关系，并揭示了晚期平台值的普适性。

Abstract: Can quantum entanglement continue to grow even after the entanglement entropies of subsystems have saturated? We address this question using genuine multi-entropy, which probes multi-partite entanglement structure beyond bipartite entanglement. In a two-sided BTZ black hole, this quantity grows linearly with time after every subsystem entropy of a fixed tripartition has saturated, extending the interval in which entanglement detects Einstein-Rosen bridge growth. The result refines the ER=EPR relation by identifying multi-partite structure invisible to ordinary entanglement entropies. Some spin-chain models and the spatially partitioned Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) model exhibit distinct transients, while the late-time plateau values in holography, the SYK model, and Haar-random states share a universal behaviour.

</details>


### [107] [Matter Swapping between Two Branes: A Local Type IIB D3-D7-D3 Realization of Charged-Fermion Mixing via E3 Instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17858)
*Michaël Sarrazin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Type IIB弦论中构造D3+–D7–D3–/E3局部模型，证明两膜间费米子混合可由E3瞬子非微扰产生，重现双层费米子算符的赝标量结构及其标量伴随，并给出紧致化路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外双层（two-sheeted）费米子混合描述已在多种实现中出现，导致可检验的物质交换效应，并启发低能搜索与宇宙学应用。作者追问：这种反复出现的低能费米子算符结构能否也由D-brane机制产生。

Method: 构造Type IIB中的局部D3+–D7–D3–/E3位形：直接的膜间混合在微扰层面被禁止，改由合适的E3瞬子扇区非微扰生成；D7 Wilson输运提供所需的规范不变相对联络。

Result: 所得低能相互作用重现了双层费米子算符的特征赝标量结构及其标量伴随。该构造在所述瞬子与零模条件下给出同一算符结构的局部弦实现，并提供面向紧致唯象实现的建模路线图。

Conclusion: 紧致化须实现两个费米子扇区的电磁隔离，并支持足够轻的非均匀D7规范模以传递长程相对相互作用；在此完整紧致化中，混合参数g原则上可由微观弦数据计算，并直接对照低能约束检验。

Abstract: Effective infrared two-sheeted descriptions of fermion mixing between braneworlds have emerged in several distinct realizations and lead to testable matter-swapping effects, motivating low-energy searches and cosmological applications. The recurrence of the same low-energy fermion operator motivates asking whether this structure can also arise from a D-brane mechanism. We construct a local Type IIB D3$_+$-D7-D3$_-$/E3 realization in which direct interbrane mixing is perturbatively forbidden but generated non-perturbatively by a suitable E3 instanton sector. The resulting low-energy interaction reproduces the characteristic pseudoscalar structure of the two-sheeted fermion operator, together with its scalar companion, while D7 Wilson transport provides the required gauge-invariant relative connection. The construction therefore provides a local string realization of the same operator structure, subject to the stated instanton and zero-mode conditions. The analysis further provides a concrete model-building roadmap toward a compact phenomenological realization: the compactification must achieve electromagnetic sequestering of the two fermion sectors and support a sufficiently light non-uniform D7 gauge mode capable of mediating a long-range relative interaction. In such a complete compactification, the mixing parameter $g$ would in principle become computable from microscopic string data and directly testable against low-energy constraints.

</details>


### [108] [Six Particles, Infinite Optimism: Towards Positivity Bounds for Six-Point Amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17861)
*Stefan Kremminger,Andrew J. Tolley*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将2→2散射的S矩阵正定性约束推广到更高点振幅，通过单标量EFT的一般可重整N标量UV完备计算4、5、6点树级振幅，发现在排除符号不定立方耦合污染的特定扇区后，部分Wilson系数可组成半正定矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前S矩阵正定性和bootstrap约束主要建立在2→2散射上，向更高点振幅的系统推广一直未能实现，因此需要探究高点多点振幅是否存在类似的普适正定性关系。

Method: 考虑D=4中最一般可重整N标量理论作为单标量EFT的一大类树级UV完备，显式计算4、5、6点散射树级振幅并在低能下展开，搜索展开系数之间的正定性关系，并隔离出不被符号不定立方耦合影响的截面。

Result: 一般情况下由于一类线性依赖于符号不定立方耦合的费曼图污染，高点多点正定性约束不可用；但在不受这些立方耦合影响的交叉对称低能振幅扇区中，选定的6、5、4点Wilson系数可组织成半正定矩阵。

Conclusion: 对这些正定性陈述的违反仅意味着给定EFT与本文的UV完备类不相容，但该结果有助于理解更高点相互作用的一般正定性约束可能及不可能呈现的形式。

Abstract: The systematic generalisation of the well-established $S$-matrix positivity and bootstrap bounds from $2 \to 2$ scattering processes to higher-point amplitudes has, to date, remained elusive. To address this, we consider a large class of tree-level UV-completions of a single-scalar EFT. Specifically, we consider the most general renormalisable $N$-scalar theory in $D=4$. By explicitly computing tree-level amplitudes for four-, five- and six-point scattering and expanding at low energies, we can search for positivity relations among the expansion coefficients. We find that quite generally positivity bounds are unavailable due to contamination from a specific class of Feynman diagrams that are linear in sign-indefinite cubic couplings. However, by isolating a sector of the full crossing-symmetric low-energy amplitude that is unaffected by these sign-indefinite cubic couplings, we find that selected six-, five- and four-point Wilson coefficients organise into positive semi-definite matrices. While a violation of these positivity statements only implies that a given EFT is inconsistent with our class of UV completions, the result helps to guide towards what more general positivity bounds for higher-point interactions could, and could not, look like.

</details>


### [109] [Smooth cutoffs and analytic continuation in Casimir physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17917)
*Lucía N. Helou,Francisco D. Mazzitelli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文基于Tao的平滑和理论，为Casimir能量中发散级数的物理正则化-减除方法与形式解析延拓方法的等价性提供了一个统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Casimir能量计算中的发散级数存在两种看似不同的处理方式：物理动机的正则化-减除，以及直接赋予有限值的形式解析延拓。二者对平行板Casimir能量结果一致，但其一致性的来源通常未被明确阐明。

Method: 引入光滑截断，借助Tao的平滑和理论构造渐近展开；利用Mellin变换表示，将发散与有限贡献编码于被积函数的极点结构中，并将有限、与正则化无关的部分与Dirichlet级数的解析延拓相联系。

Result: 证明有限且与正则化无关的部分由相应Dirichlet级数的解析延拓普遍决定；将方法推广至Casimir问题相关的一类级数，发现对数发散源于极点重合，并得到结果在普适项与截断依赖项之间的自然分解。

Conclusion: 该统一框架使正则化减除与解析延拓的等价性变得透明，其普适-截断依赖分解与量子场论中重整化的结构相对应。

Abstract: Divergent series arising in the computation of Casimir energies admit two seemingly different treatments: a physically motivated regularization and subtraction procedure, and formal analytic continuation methods that assign finite values directly to divergent expressions. The agreement between these approaches for the Casimir energy between parallel plates is well known, but its origin is often left implicit. In this work, we provide a unified framework that makes this equivalence transparent. Building on Tao's theory of smoothed sums, we show that the introduction of a smooth cutoff leads to an asymptotic expansion whose finite, regulator-independent part is universally determined by the analytic continuation of the associated Dirichlet series. This identification follows from a Mellin-transform representation, in which divergences and finite contributions are encoded in the pole structure of the integrand. We extend this approach to a class of series relevant to Casimir problems. In this setting, we show that logarithmic divergences arise from pole coincidences, and we obtain a natural decomposition of the result into universal and cutoff-dependent terms, mirroring the structure of renormalization in quantum field theory.

</details>


### [110] [Central charge and black hole entropy for regular extremal black-bounce spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18028)
*Xu Ye,Shan-Ping Wu,Yu-Kun Zhang,Shao-Wen Wei*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 采用Kerr/CFT方法研究正则极端black-bounce时空的中心荷与熵，发现微观Cardy熵与Bekenstein-Hawking熵一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Kerr/CFT对应在无曲率奇点的正则时空中的适用性，为黑洞熵提供微观统计解释。

Method: 推导极端black-bounce时空（Kerr、Kerr-Newman、Reissner-Nordström对应体）的近地平线几何，施加边界条件分析渐近对称群，提取中心荷与Frolov-Thorne真空左行温度；RN情形通过引入U(1)规范纤维升维至5D。

Result: 近地平线几何呈现SL(2,R)×U(1)或SL(2,R)×SO(3)增强对称性；对称群含微分同胚及电磁场U(1)规范对称性；black-bounce Kerr-Newman情形电磁贡献的中心荷为零；Cardy公式给出的微观熵与Bekenstein-Hawking熵一致。

Conclusion: Kerr/CFT方法对某些无曲率奇点的正则时空仍然有效，为黑洞熵提供微观统计理解。

Abstract: The Bekenstein-Hawking entropy, proportional to one quarter of the horizon area, is fundamental in black hole thermodynamics and can also be understood via the AdS/CFT correspondence, such as the 3D BTZ black hole and 2D CFT. In this work, we adopt the Kerr/CFT approach to analyze the central charge and black hole entropy for regular extremal black-bounce spacetimes, including the counterparts of the Kerr, Kerr-Newman, and Reissner-Nordström black holes. These spacetimes are free of curvature singularities at $r=0$. We derive the near horizon geometries of these spacetimes and find that they exhibit enhanced symmetry, namely SL$(2,\mathbb{R})\times \mathrm{U}(1)$ or SL$(2,\mathbb{R}) \times \mathrm{SO}(3)$. By imposing appropriate boundary conditions, we analyze their asymptotic symmetry groups, which contain diffeomorphisms as well as the $\mathrm{U}(1)_{\rm gauge}$ symmetry arising from the electromagnetic field. We then extract the central charge from the charge algebra and evaluate the left-moving temperature of the Frolov-Thorne vacuum. It is worth emphasizing that in the black-bounce Kerr-Newman case, the central charge from the electromagnetic contribution vanishes. Furthermore, in the black-bounce Reissner-Nordström case, we uplift the 4D geometry to a 5D configuration by incorporating a $\mathrm{U}(1)$ gauge fiber. Our results show that the microscopic entropy calculated from the Cardy formula is consistent with the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy. This agreement suggests that the Kerr/CFT approach remains valid for certain regular spacetimes without curvature singularities, thereby providing a microscopic statistical understanding of black hole entropy.

</details>


### [111] [Deep learning emergent spacetime from fermionic spectral functions in holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18566)
*Koji Hashimoto,Hyun-Sik Jeong,Keun-Young Kim,Daichi Takeda,Kwan Yun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 提出基于神经常微分方程的物理信息机器学习框架，从边界费米子谱函数直接重构带电AdS黑洞的体时空与规范场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全息对偶中，从边界可观测量反演体时空（全息逆问题）通常是不适定的；本文旨在利用物理约束的神经网络可靠地解决该逆问题，并检验其在量子临界区的可辨识性与简并性。

Method: 采用Neural ODE作为体时空与规范场的参数化；将UV渐近行为、视界正则性以及零温极端性作为硬约束编码进网络结构；以边界费米子谱函数为监督信号，联合重构极端Reissner-Nordström AdS几何与U(1)探针电荷。

Result: 在由探针电荷设定的非费米液体、边缘费米液体（奇异金属）和类费米液体三种量子临界区中均可靠重构几何，探针电荷推断精度达亚百分比；放宽近AdS边界约束后出现等谱非唯一性——径向分布不同但共享相同近视界AdS₂×R²数据的体剖面给出相同的费米面附近谱函数。

Conclusion: 该等谱简并正是零温下全息预期的体简并，且在不同训练运行中自发出现，表明网络捕捉到IR CFT普适性而非过拟合单一UV完备。

Abstract: We present a physics-informed machine learning framework based on Neural Ordinary Differential Equations that solves the holographic inverse problem: reconstructing the bulk spacetime and gauge field of a charged AdS black hole directly from boundary fermionic spectral functions. Encoding the UV asymptotics, horizon regularity, and zero temperature extremality as hard constraints in the neural network architecture, our framework reliably reconstructs the extremal Reissner-Nordström AdS geometry across three quantum critical regimes set by the $U(1)$ probe charge---non-Fermi liquid, marginal Fermi liquid (strange metal), and Fermi-liquid-like states---and can jointly infer the probe charge itself to sub-percent accuracy. Relaxing the near-AdS boundary constraint uncovers a geometrical degeneracy: bulk profiles that differ throughout the radial direction but share the same near-horizon $AdS_2 \times \mathbb{R}^2$ data reproduce identical spectral functions near the Fermi surface. This isospectral non-uniqueness is precisely the bulk degeneracy expected on general holographic grounds at zero temperature, and its spontaneous emergence across independent training runs shows that the network isolates the IR CFT universality rather than overfitting a single UV completion.

</details>


### [112] [Reduction of the six-dimensional $q$-form fields to the four-dimensional fields by coupling with gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18277)
*Yong-Tao Lu,Heng Guo,Qun Wei,Bing Wei*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究0-形式标量场、1-形式U(1)规范向量场和2-形式Kalb-Ramond场在余维二厚膜上的局域化，引入依赖于体标量曲率的耦合函数F(R)，发现无质量模可在t₁、t₂为正时局域化，有质量模的局域化性质由t₂决定并可排除快子模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在额外维膜世界模型中，各类q-形式场能否被局域化在膜上是一个核心问题。通过在六维作用量中引入依赖标量曲率的引力耦合函数F(R)，可以系统研究不同q-形式场在余维二厚膜上的局域化行为，并探究耦合参数对无质量模与有质量模局域化性质的影响。

Method: 在余维二厚膜背景下，对0-形式标量场、1-形式U(1)规范向量场和2-形式Kalb-Ramond场的六维作用量引入依赖体标量曲率的耦合函数F(R)，推导相应的运动方程与Kaluza-Klein分解，分析有效势与零模波函数，并考察耦合参数t₁、t₂对无质量模与有质量模局域化及共振模的影响。

Result: 当耦合参数t₁、t₂为正时，各q-形式场的无质量模均可局域化在厚膜上。有质量模的局域化性质由t₂决定：0<t₂<v²/24时，有质量模不能局域化，但可作为共振模存在；t₂=v²/24时，有限个有质量模可局域化，且局域化模数目随t₁增大而增加；t₂>v²/24时，Kaluza-Klein有效势形成无限深势阱，所有有质量模均可局域化。此外，对不同的q-形式场，快子型有质量模总可以被排除。

Conclusion: 通过引入依赖标量曲率的耦合函数F(R)，不同q-形式场（0-形式、1-形式、2-形式）在余维二厚膜上的局域化行为具有统一而相似的规律，其无质量模局域化要求正耦合参数，有质量模局域化性质由t₂阈值v²/24决定，并且可以有效排除快子模。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the localization of various $q$-form fields on a codimension-two brane. In particular, the $0$-form scalar field, the $1$-form $U(1)$ gauge vector field, and the $2$-form Kalb-Ramond field are considered with gravitational coupling, where a coupling function $F(R)$ is introduced into the six-dimensional actions of these fields. The function $F(R)$ depends on the scalar curvature of the bulk. Within this framework, we find that the massless modes of different $q$-form fields can be localized on the thick brane for positive values of the coupling parameters $t_1$ and $t_2$. For the massive modes, the different $q$-form fields exhibit similar localization properties determined by the coupling parameter $t_2$. When $0<t_2<v^2/24$, the massive modes of these fields cannot be localized on the brane, while they may exist as the resonant modes. In the case $t_2=v^2/24$, a finite number of massive modes can be localized on the thick brane, and the number of localized modes increases with the coupling parameter $t_1$. Finally, when $t_2>v^2/24$, the effective potentials associated with the Kaluza-Klein modes of these $q$-form fields form infinitely deep potential wells, so that all massive modes can be localized on the brane. Moreover, the tachyonic massive modes can always be excluded for different $q$-form fields.

</details>


### [113] [Carrollian axion electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18791)
*Hemant Rathi,Ashish Shukla*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 构造了与轴子场耦合的Carrollian电动力学理论，分为电型和磁型两类，并研究了其经典解、约束结构及物理相空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将轴子电动力学推广到Carrollian极限（c→0），探索轴子与电磁场在该极限下耦合所产生的新物理现象，如Carrollian版本的Witten效应。

Method: 通过对轴子电动力学实施c→0展开构造两类理论，推导拉格朗日量并求解一般经典解；对磁型理论进行Dirac-Bergmann约束结构分析，识别其物理相空间。

Result: 电型理论中轴子与电磁场有效解耦；磁型理论具有非平凡轴子耦合。磁型理论存在Carrollian版本的Witten效应：在非零常轴子背景下，磁单极获得电荷成为dyon；同时线性磁电效应完全消失。

Conclusion: Carrollian轴子电动力学的磁型理论呈现出独特的轴子耦合现象（Carrollian Witten效应），而线性磁电效应经约束分析确认完全消失。

Abstract: We construct theories of Carrollian electromagnetism coupled to an axion field. Obtained by performing a $c \to 0$ expansion of axion electrodynamics, these are of two types: electric and magnetic. We derive the Lagrangians and work out the general classical solutions for both. While the electric theory has the axion effectively decoupled from the electromagnetic fields, the magnetic theory is endowed with a nontrivial axion coupling that leads to interesting phenomenology. In particular, a Carrollian analogue of the Witten effect, wherein a magnetic monopole in the presence of a constant non-vanishing axion acquires an electric charge and becomes a dyon, is present in the magnetic theory. At the same time, the linear magnetoelectric effect vanishes entirely, a conclusion we establish by performing a thorough Dirac-Bergmann analysis of the constraint structure of the magnetic theory and a proper identification of its physical phase space.

</details>


### [114] [Turbulent gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18295)
*Giulio Taiocchi,Christiana Pantelidou,Miguel Zilhão*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在渐近AdS₄时空中求解完整非线性爱因斯坦方程，首次构造了湍流引力尾迹，揭示其边界出现相干涡旋结构并呈现经典湍流的自相似标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流尾迹是流体力学中的经典现象，但在引力理论（尤其是渐近AdS时空的全非线性引力动力学）中尚未得到研究，本文旨在将湍流尾迹概念引入引力体系并探讨其对偶场论含义。

Method: 采用特征表述（characteristic formulation）求解完整非线性爱因斯坦方程，在渐近AdS₄时空中，通过作用于boosted黑膜上的局域边界形变生成尾迹，并沿x方向分析其传播。

Result: 尾迹的边界动力学展现出被背景流向下游平流的相干涡旋结构，并呈现经典湍流尾迹的自相似标度律；同时在视界面积密度、无迹视界外曲率及体度规微扰中识别出从渐近边界延伸至视界的相干结构印记。

Conclusion: 这是首个针对四维渐近AdS时空湍流引力尾迹的研究，为引力湍流及全息对偶框架下湍流动力学提供了新的研究途径。

Abstract: We construct turbulent gravitational wakes in asymptotically AdS$_4$ spacetimes by solving the full non-linear Einstein equations in the characteristic formulation. Specifically, we show that a localized boundary deformation acting on a boosted black brane generates a wake whose boundary dynamics exhibit the formation of coherent vortical structures that are advected downstream by the background flow. Analysing a wake propagating along the x direction, we show that it displays the characteristic self-similar scaling of classical turbulent wakes. We then identify the geometric imprint of the wake on the horizon area density, the traceless horizon extrinsic curvature, and bulk metric perturbations, finding coherent structures that extend from the asymptotic boundary to the horizon. These results provide the first study of turbulent gravitational wakes in four-dimensional asymptotically AdS spacetimes.

</details>


### [115] [Differential equations for Dotsenko--Fateev integrals: the case of degenerate fields $Φ_{n,1}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18410)
*Aleksandra Ivanova*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究Dotsenko--Fateev型积分对应的高阶微分方程及其与退化成分为Φ_{n,1}的四点关联函数的联系，给出了方程的一般结构与系数表达式，并建立了与超几何算符的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 积分型关联函数（Dotsenko--Fateev型）通常满足特定微分方程，但高阶方程的一般结构与系数尚缺乏系统刻画；同时需要厘清其与超几何算符的联系，以便统一处理与验证。

Method: 针对Dotsenko--Fateev型积分及含退化场Φ_{n,1}的四点关联函数，构造相应的高阶微分方程；以积分参数{A,B,C,g}为变量推导方程的一般结构与系数；利用CFT中的特殊情形进行验证；并建立高阶微分方程与超几何算符之间的对应关系。

Result: 确定了方程的一般结构及其系数随积分参数{A,B,C,g}的显式表达；通过CFT的特例完成了验证；并明确了高阶微分方程与超几何算符之间的关系。

Conclusion: Dotsenko--Fateev型积分对应的关联函数可由高阶微分方程统一描述，其系数由积分参数决定，且与超几何算符存在明确对应，可作为CFT中相关计算的工具与验证途径。

Abstract: We study higher-order differential equations corresponding to integrals of the Dotsenko--Fateev type and associated with four-point correlation functions with degenerate fields $Φ_{n,1}$. We determine the general structure and coefficients of the equations in terms of the integral parameters $ \{ A,B,C,g \} $. In particular, special cases of these equations in CFT are used for verification. In addition, the relationship between higher-order differential equations and hypergeometric operators is established.

</details>


### [116] [Schwinger stability of T-duality-inspired extremal black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18528)
*Chiang-Mei Chen,Kimet Jusufi,Douglas Singleton*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了零点半径T-对偶正则带电黑洞中与Schwinger对产生相关的近视界带电标量不稳定性。对显式T-对偶形状因子，极端分支终止于r_ext=√2 l_0，此处极端电荷与近视界电场消失，而AdS2半径保持有限(√3 l_0)，精确近视界不稳定性参数为负，半经典荷质比阈值在趋近端点时发散，故固定q/m的大质量组分在端点附近无局域带电标量不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则（无奇点）黑洞通常被认为会在大电荷下通过Schwinger对产生屏蔽自身电场，从而可能限制极端构型的存在。作者希望检验这种"正则性导致近视界Schwinger势垒"是否普适，特别是考察T-对偶零点半径方案下的正则带电黑洞。

Method: 采用与零点半径T-对偶处方相同的有效几何与正则化规范势，构造极端分支、其AdS2×S2近视界喉部以及一般形状因子下的带电标量不稳定性阈值；对显式T-对偶形状因子求解析解，并与ABG（Einstein-非线性电动力学）解进行对比，后者具有非零极端近视界电场，对应局域Schwinger阈值保持有限。

Result: T-对偶极端分支终止于r_ext=√2 l_0，端点处极端电荷与近视界电场为零，AdS2半径有限为√3 l_0；端点精确近视界不稳定性参数为负；半经典电荷-质量阈值随趋近端点而发散，因此每个固定q/m的大质量组分在足够靠近端点的极端分支段中不存在局域带电标量不稳定性。相比之下，ABG解的极端近视界电场非零，附加最小耦合带电标量探针的局域Schwinger阈值保持有限。

Conclusion: T-对偶分支中出现的发散近视界Schwinger势垒并非正则性的普适结果；正则黑洞的近视界不稳定性行为依赖于具体正则化/形状因子选择，ABG型解表现出不同的有限阈值行为。

Abstract: We study the near-horizon charged-scalar instability associated with Schwinger pair production in the charged regular black hole geometry of the zero-point-length T-duality prescription. Using the same effective geometry and regularized gauge potential, we construct the extremal branch, its ${\rm AdS}_2 \times S^2$ near-horizon throat, and the charged-scalar instability threshold for a general form factor. For the explicit T-duality form factor the extremal branch terminates at $r_\mathrm{ext} = \sqrt2 \, l_0$, where the extremal charge and the near-horizon electric field vanish while the ${\rm AdS}_2$ radius remains finite at $\sqrt3 \, l_0$. At this endpoint the exact near-horizon instability parameter is negative. In the semiclassical regime the corresponding charge-to-mass threshold diverges as the endpoint is approached. Thus, for each fixed massive species with finite $q/m$, a sufficiently near-endpoint portion of the extremal branch is free of the local charged-scalar instability. For comparison, the Ayón-Beato-García (ABG) Einstein-nonlinear-electrodynamics solution has a nonvanishing extremal near-horizon electric field. For an additional minimally coupled charged-scalar probe, the corresponding local Schwinger threshold remains finite. Thus the divergent near-endpoint Schwinger barrier found in the T-duality branch is not a generic consequence of regularity.

</details>


### [117] [The geometry of multiloop Feynman Integrals from the Scattering Facet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18668)
*José Ríos-Sánchez,Germán Rodrigo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出散射多面体（Scattering Facet, SF）的一种新的"基-纤维"几何描述：纤维是一族"变形图解 zonotope"，其上自然地编码了费曼图的因果演化，并由此导出规范形式、因果表示与生成树表示，且无需进行积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SF 作为由费曼图定义的正几何，其规范微分形式能重现标量费曼积分中对圈动量能量分量的积分结果，与圈树对偶（LTD）密切相关。作者希望给出更几何化、更结构化的理解，从而统一并简化因果表示与生成树表示的推导，避免显式积分。

Method: 将 SF 分解为"基-纤维"结构：基空间上的每一点对应一种因果序（因果演化），纤维是称为"变形图解 zonotope"的多面体族。利用该分解研究 SF 的正则三角剖分，并推导其规范形式。

Result: 纤维（变形图解 zonotope）具有费曼图因果演化的自然解释，并携带关于散射振幅的有用信息；SF 的这种分解诱导出规范形式的描述，从而给出所谓的"因果表示"。此外，该框架提供了刻画 SF 全部正则三角剖分的新数学工具；由这些三角剖分得到的规范形式对应"生成树表示"，与原始的 LTD 振幅（表示为生成树之和）一致。

Conclusion: 这一几何视角提供了获得因果表示与生成树表示的新方法，且完全无需执行任何积分。

Abstract: The Scattering Facet (SF) of the Cosmological Polytope is a positive geometry defined from a Feynman diagram and encoding its combinatorics. Its canonical differential form reproduces the result of the integration over the energy components of loop momenta in scalar Feynman integrals, and is therefore closely related to the Loop-Tree Duality (LTD). In this article, we present a novel description of the SF as a base-fiber geometry. The fibers are a family of polytopes called deformed graphical zonotopes, which have a natural interpretation in terms of the causal evolution of the underlying Feynman diagram, and capture interesting information on scattering amplitudes. This decomposition of the SF induces a description of the canonical form leading to the so-called causal representation. Moreover, it provides new mathematical tools that allow us to characterize all its regular triangulations. Canonical forms obtained from these triangulations correspond to spanning tree representations, matching the original LTD amplitudes expressed as a sum over spanning trees. This geometrical viewpoint provides us with new methods to obtain the causal and spanning tree representations, without the need of performing any integration.

</details>


### [118] [The Geometry of the CKM matrix, the Standard Model and RG fixed points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18884)
*Brian P. Dolan,Charles Nash*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将标准模型中的CKM混合矩阵视为双陪集空间M=T\SU(3)/T中的元素并赋予黎曼度量，研究其几何结构，发现点奇点对应重整化群不动点，并由Weyl群S_3诱导出相关性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究费米子混合矩阵（CKM矩阵）的几何本质，试图用微分几何与群论框架统一刻画其结构与重整化群行为之间的联系。

Method: 将CKM矩阵V看作双陪集空间M=T\SU(3)/T（T=U(1)×U(1)）的元素，在M上引入黎曼度量，分析其点奇点与线奇点；并借助SU(3)的Weyl群W（即对称群S_3）这一离散结构来刻画相关性质。

Result: M具有点奇点和连接这些奇点的线奇点；点奇点被证明对应于已知的重整化群不动点；Weyl群S_3在V中表现为一个六边形的顶点，是诱导上述诸多性质的关键离散对象。

Conclusion: 费米子混合矩阵的几何可由双陪集空间的黎曼几何描述，其奇点结构对应重整化群不动点，而Weyl群S_3作为核心离散结构支配着该几何的对称性与奇点特征。

Abstract: We investigate the geometry of the Fermion mixing matrix in the Standard Model. The principal structure studied is the CKM matrix, $V$, viewed as an element of the double coset space $M$, where $M=T\backslash SU(3)/T$, and $T=U(1)\times U(1)$. The space $M$ is given a Riemannian metric, and has both point singularities, which it is shown correspond to the known renormalization group fixed points, and line singularities connecting the fixed points. A key discrete object inducing many of these properties is the Weyl group, $W$, of $SU(3)$, $W$ being the symmetric group $S_3$, which appears in the guise of the vertices of a hexagon in $V$.

</details>


### [119] [Reading Topological Hair from Black-Hole Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18944)
*Kumar Ghosh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文将黑洞“毛发”对边界混合态纠缠的影响分解为局域应力能量通道与拓扑信息通道，并以非旋转BTZ黑洞上的Nielsen-Olesen涡旋为对象，通过超选择定理、Wilson-threaded反射矩与RT连通性转变进行区分与计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞毛发既可通过局域应力能量影响边界混合态纠缠，也可通过经典度规不可见的拓扑信息起作用，二者此前未被清晰分离；同时需要避免赋予无支持的缠绕相关熵。

Method: 在非旋转BTZ黑洞上研究Nielsen-Olesen涡旋：证明超选择定理使Shannon扇区熵从Markov gap中抵消，并指出热U(1)_k CFT中标准U(1)对称分辨反射熵在领头阶为均分；在探针U(1)_k Chern-Simons完成中定义Wilson-threaded反射矩，分析连结数ν下的归一化相位2πκpnν/k及gcd(κν,k)=1时的DFT重构；结合RT连通性转变与尺度ℓ_*r_+/L^2=1.128378…；用带完整一阶变分核（含纠缠楔截面端点运动）的horizon-anchored Einstein-Abelian-Higgs解计算带电矩模量与普通Markov gap。

Result: Shannon扇区熵从Markov gap中抵消，标准U(1)对称分辨反射熵领头阶均分，排除了imbalance-resolved gap中普适的log|n|项；Wilson-threaded反射矩的归一化相位为2πκpnν/k，当gcd(κν,k)=1时可经离散傅里叶变换重构n mod k；RT连通性转变控制指定连结轮廓的开关，独立尺度ℓ_*r_+/L^2=1.128378…决定涡旋诱导转变移动的方向；带电矩模量与普通Markov gap仍为几何可观测量并由该解算出。

Conclusion: 通过相位与模量的分离，可区分拓扑毛发与引力dressing，而无需引入无支持的缠绕依赖熵。

Abstract: Black-hole hair can affect boundary mixed-state entanglement through local stress-energy and through topological information invisible to the classical metric. We separate these channels for a Nielsen--Olesen vortex on a nonrotating BTZ black hole. A superselection theorem shows that Shannon sector entropy cancels from the Markov gap, while standard $U(1)$ symmetry-resolved reflected entropy in a thermal $U(1)_k$ CFT is equipartitioned at leading order, excluding a universal $\log|n|$ term in the imbalance-resolved gap. We therefore define a Wilson-threaded reflected moment in a probe $U(1)_k$ Chern--Simons completion coupled to the compact vortex-flux class. In a reflected-replica sector with linking number $ν$, its normalised phase is $2πκpnν/k$, where $κ\in\mathbb Z$ is the mixed topological coupling; for $\gcd(κν,k)=1$, a discrete Fourier transform reconstructs $n\bmod k$. The RT connectivity transition switches the specified linked contour on or off, whereas the independent scale $\ell_\star r_+/L^2=1.128378\ldots$ determines the direction of the vortex-induced shift of that transition. The charged-moment modulus and the ordinary Markov gap remain geometric observables and are computed from a horizon-anchored Einstein-Abelian-Higgs solution with a complete first-variation kernel including the motion of the entanglement-wedge cross-section endpoints. This phase and modulus separation distinguishes topological hair from gravitational dressing without assigning an unsupported winding-dependent entropy.

</details>


### [120] [Majoron dark matter detection via hybrid magnon transmon qubit system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19056)
*Le Bin Ho,Do Thi Huong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 提出一种混合磁振子-量子比特腔光镜方案，利用Majoron与电子自旋耦合产生的赝磁场，共振驱动YIG球的Kittel模，并通过色散相互作用将磁振子布居转导至超导transmon量子比特进行探测，评估其对Majoron暗物质的投影灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻Majoron是有良好动机的暗物质候选者，可与电子自旋耦合产生振荡赝磁场，但缺乏高灵敏度的探测平台。论文旨在利用混合量子系统的增强效应来搜索这类暗物质。

Method: 构建混合磁振子-量子比特腔光镜：Majoron场共振驱动铁磁钇铁石榴石（YIG）球的Kittel模，产生由大自旋系综增强的集体磁振子响应；通过腔介导的色散相互作用将磁振子布居转导至超导transmon量子比特，并用量子非破坏性Ramsey干涉测量进行读出。基于实验已演示的磁振子-腔-量子比特参数推导信号、评估灵敏度，并分析由磁场调谐实现的质量扫描策略。

Result: 推导了Majoron诱导信号，给出了投影灵敏度，并分析了基于磁场调谐的质量扫描策略；将投影探测范围与现有的轴子/Majoron-电子耦合的实验室及天体物理限制进行基准比较。

Conclusion: 混合磁振子-量子比特架构是搜索Majoron暗物质以及更广泛地与电子自旋耦合的超轻玻色子场的颇具前景的量子传感平台。

Abstract: Ultralight Majorons are well-motivated dark-matter candidates that can couple to electron spins, generating an oscillating pseudo-magnetic field. We propose a hybrid magnon-qubit haloscope that exploits this interaction to search for Majoron dark matter. In our scheme, the Majoron field resonantly drives the Kittel mode of a ferrimagnetic yttrium iron garnet (YIG) sphere, producing a collective magnon response enhanced by the large spin ensemble. The resulting magnon population is transduced to a superconducting transmon qubit through a cavity-mediated dispersive interaction and detected using quantum-nondemolition Ramsey interferometry. Using experimentally demonstrated parameters for magnon-cavity-qubit systems, we derive the Majoron-induced signal, evaluate the projected sensitivity, and analyze the corresponding mass-scan strategy enabled by magnetic-field tuning, benchmarking the projected reach against existing laboratory and astrophysical constraints on the axion/Majoron-electron coupling. Our results establish hybrid magnon-qubit architectures as a promising quantum-sensing platform for searches for Majoron dark matter and, more generally, ultralight bosonic fields coupled to electronic spins.

</details>


### [121] [When duality changes the poles: $SL(2,\mathbb{Z})$ transformations of linear response EFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19075)
*Andrea Amoretti,Daniel K. Brattan,Jonas Rongen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究SL(2,Z)变换对(2+1)维极点截断线性响应有效理论的作用，推导了静磁化率、爱因斯坦关系和AC输运系数的显式映射，并在D3/D5探针膜系统中验证了该构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性响应有效理论在有限低频域内的关联函数是亚纯的，其EFT数据同时包含导数展开的输运系数和保留的非流体力学极点。由于S变换反转光学电导率并交换其极点和零点，它不一定与红外截断对易，因此需要理解SL(2,Z)变换如何作用于极点截断的有效理论。

Method: 研究了SL(2,Z)变换在(2+1)维极点截断线性响应有效理论上的作用，推导了静磁化率、爱因斯坦关系和AC输运系数的显式映射，分析了纯流体力学部门和准流体力学区域的行为，并在替代量子化的D3/D5探针膜系统中进行了检验。

Result: 在纯流体力学部门，映射在原始输运数据上封闭；在准流体力学区域，变换后的极点位置和（一般而言）留数构成额外的红外数据。在D3/D5探针膜系统中，保留一个原始有隙极点的EFT可对应于保留零个、一个或两个的变换后EFT。全息结果验证了解析映射，并在控制参数较大时表现出逆德鲁德电导率。

Conclusion: SL(2,Z)变换与红外截断的不对易性导致极点截断EFT在变换下可能改变保留的极点数目，准流体力学区域需要额外的红外数据来表征变换后的理论，全息计算验证了解析构造。

Abstract: We study how $SL(2,\mathbb Z)$ transformations act on pole-truncated linear-response effective theories in $(2+1)$ dimensions. For correlators meromorphic within a finite low-frequency domain, the EFT data include both derivative-expanded transport coefficients and the retained non-hydrodynamic poles. Since the $S$-trans\-for\-mation inverts the optical conductivity and exchanges its poles and zeros, it does not necessarily commute with the infrared truncation. We derive explicit maps for static susceptibilities, the Einstein relation and AC transport coefficients. In the purely hydrodynamic sector the map closes on the original transport data, while in quasihydrodynamic regimes the transformed pole positions and, in general, residues constitute additional infrared data. We test the construction in the alternatively quantised D3/D5 probe-brane system, where an EFT retaining one original gapped pole can correspond to transformed EFTs retaining zero, one or two. The holographic results verify the analytic maps and exhibit an inverse-Drude conductivity at large values of the control parameter.

</details>


### [122] [Channel-Resolved High-Overtone Quasinormal Modes Decode Kasner Scaling inside Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17426)
*Zi-Qing Xiao,Jun Nian,Li Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 高泛音准正规模谱编码黑洞内部近奇点Kasner标度，可独立重构Kasner指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 试图从黑洞外部可观测的准正规模谱中读出视界内部近奇点几何信息，避免依赖Kasner约束或全息方法。

Method: 分析高泛音准正规模谱结构，从中分离出分别决定时间与空间Kasner指数的谱数据；在五维渐近平坦Schwarzschild、平面Schwarzschild-AdS5以及连续变化的Gibbons-Maeda黑洞族上进行重构。

Result: 重构得到的指数事后满足Kasner关系，构成非平凡的几何自洽性检验；高泛音谱可分解为全局谱数据与局域代数修正，其分数幂由近奇点Kasner几何确定。

Conclusion: 高泛音准正规模谱可作为视界后局域几何的直接谱探针。

Abstract: The high-overtone quasinormal-mode spectrum encodes the near-singularity Kasner scaling of black-hole interiors. We show that distinct structures in this spectrum independently determine the temporal and spatial Kasner grades, allowing the corresponding metric exponents to be reconstructed without imposing Kasner constraints or invoking holography. We demonstrate this independent reconstruction at the fixed Kasner point of five-dimensional asymptotically flat Schwarzschild and planar Schwarzschild--AdS$_5$, and then extend it to a continuously varying Gibbons--Maeda black-hole family. The reconstructed exponents satisfy the Kasner relation a posteriori, providing a nontrivial geometric consistency check. More generally, the high-overtone spectrum separates into global spectral data and local algebraic corrections whose fractional powers are fixed by the near-singularity Kasner geometry. These fractional corrections therefore provide a direct spectral probe of the local geometry behind the horizon.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Affine Fixed Points on Flat Manifold Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17860)
*Aaron Reite*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究了紧致平坦流形对之间的仿射自映射，给出了相对Nielsen数的一个显式公式，该公式以环境全纯与提升子流形稳定子的全纯表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对Nielsen数在原有工作（Wong的幂零流形对公式、作者的模型可解流形对公式）中已有刻画，但缺乏覆盖有限全纯情形的平坦流形对显式公式，且不清楚稳定子全纯能否由子流形内蕴全纯替代。

Method: 对紧致平坦流形对上的仿射自映射进行代数拓扑分析，利用提升子流形稳定子的全纯与环境的全纯推导相对Nielsen数的显式计算公式。

Result: 得到相对Nielsen数的显式公式；该公式推广了Wong幂零流形对公式的平坦特例至有限全纯情形，在平坦重叠部分与作者的模型可解流形对公式一致并有所扩展；证明稳定子全纯一般不能用子流形的内蕴全纯替代；给出体现有趣Schirmer理论并包含模型可解流形类之外平坦对的例子。

Conclusion: 该显式公式统一并拓展了已有结果，明确了稳定子全纯的不可替代性，并通过具体例子展示了平坦流形对情形下相对Nielsen数与Schirmer理论的丰富结构。

Abstract: In this paper we consider affine selfmaps of compact flat manifold pairs. We derive an explicit formula for the relative Nielsen number in terms of the ambient holonomy and the holonomy carried by the stabilizer of the lifted submanifold. The formula extends the flat specialization of Wong's nilmanifold-pair formula to finite holonomy, agrees with the author's earlier model-solvmanifold-pair formula on the flat overlap, and extends beyond it. We also show that the stabilizer holonomy cannot in general be replaced by the intrinsic holonomy of the submanifold. Examples exhibit interesting Schirmer theory and include flat pairs outside the model-solvmanifold class.

</details>


### [124] [Condensed Brown Comenetz Duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18162)
*Roey Hel-Or,Amos Kaminski*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 在凝聚谱上构造对合对偶，推广经典Pontryagin对偶；对偶化在同伦群上诱导出度反转的Pontryagin对偶，且双对偶映射为等价；从而任意普通谱（视为离散凝聚谱）均满足双对偶，无需同伦群的有限性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Pontryagin对偶适用于局部紧交换拓扑群，但谱的范畴中缺乏类似的对合对偶结构。作者希望在凝聚谱框架下推广该对偶，使对偶理论适用于更一般的同伦群（不再要求有限性），从而为稳定同伦论提供新的对偶工具。

Method: 利用凝聚数学（condensed mathematics）框架，限定同伦群为局部紧的凝聚谱。在此范畴上构造对合对偶函子，定义典范双对偶映射，并通过对同伦群逐度的计算与比较，证明对偶化在同伦群层面实现度反转的Pontryagin对偶。

Result: 构造出凝聚谱上的对合对偶；证明对偶化诱导同伦群上的Pontryagin对偶且度数反转；证明典范双对偶映射是等价。特别地，任意普通谱作为离散凝聚谱均满足双对偶，且无需对同伦群作有限性假设。

Conclusion: 该工作将Pontryagin对偶从拓扑群推广至凝聚谱层面，消除有限性限制，为稳定同伦论与凝聚数学的交叉提供了统一的对偶框架。

Abstract: We construct an involutive duality on condensed spectra with locally compact homotopy groups, extending classical Pontryagin duality. We prove that dualization induces Pontryagin duality on homotopy groups, with degrees reversed, and that the canonical biduality map is an equivalence. In particular, every ordinary spectrum, viewed as a discrete condensed spectrum, satisfies biduality, without any finiteness assumptions on its homotopy groups.

</details>


### [125] [A brief note on the Hopf and Kervaire invariant one problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18942)
*J. P. May*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文是一篇阐述性笔记，介绍Hopf不变量一问题一个鲜为人知但非常古老的"一行"解法，并推测该思路对Kervaire不变量一问题的（不）适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hopf不变量一问题是代数拓扑中的经典难题，已有多种解法，但作者认为其中存在一个鲜为人知且极为简洁的古老解法值得推广介绍；同时希望探讨这种思路能否迁移到结构相似的Kervaire不变量一问题。

Method: 阐述性（expository）方法：梳理并呈现一个古老的一行式解法，并对该核心思想尝试推广至Kervaire不变量一问题进行分析与推测，属于概念性与思辨性讨论而非新定理证明。

Result: 给出了Hopf不变量一问题的一个简洁的一行解法介绍；对于Kervaire不变量一问题，作者仅给出该思路是否适用的推测，未给出确定结论。

Conclusion: 该古老的一行解法值得被重新关注与传播；其思想能否应用于Kervaire不变量一问题仍有待探讨，作者对此持谨慎的（不）适用性推测态度。

Abstract: This expository note advertises a little known but very old one-line solution of the Hopf invariant one problem and speculates on the (in)applicability of the idea to the Kervaire invariant one problem.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [Xiao's Genus-Two Fibration: Branched Covers and Braid Monodromy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17634)
*Anar Akhmedov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文直接从Xiao亏格二Lefschetz纤维化的分歧覆盖构造出发，借助Moishezon的辫单调方法计算其几何单调分解，并给出七个消失圈的有序正分解及Artin坐标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Xiao亏格二Lefschetz纤维化的几何单调分解此前缺乏从其分歧覆盖构造出发的直接计算，本文旨在通过辫单调方法填补这一空白。

Method: 遵循Moishezon辫单调方法，从分歧覆盖构造直接计算。完全四边形决定六个分歧点的运动，给出四个非分离和三个分离消失圈；在每个3+3退化处，由分歧运动确定球面映射类，并利用Xiao的局部全纯模型确定其亏格二提升；固定特异路径系统后得到有序正分解。

Result: 得到类型为(4,3)的有序正分解，并为七个因子给出Artin坐标。

Conclusion: 该工作为Xiao亏格二Lefschetz纤维化提供了显式的单调分解与Artin坐标描述。

Abstract: We compute the geometric monodromy factorization of Xiao's genus-two Lefschetz fibration directly from its branched-cover construction, following Moishezon's braid-monodromy method. The complete quadrangle determines the motion of the six branch points and gives four nonseparating and three separating vanishing cycles. At each of the three $3+3$ degenerations, the branch motion determines the spherical mapping class, and Xiao's local holomorphic model determines its genus-two lift. After fixing a distinguished system of paths, we obtain an ordered positive factorization of type $(4,3)$ and give Artin coordinates for the seven factors.

</details>


### [127] [Transitive automorphism groups of maximal curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17611)
*Saeed Tafazolian*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 对亏格至少为2的F_{q^2}-极大曲线，若其全几何自同构群在F_{q^2}-有理点上传递，则当h>1时必为Hermitian曲线；h=1时q=5额外出现唯一的F_{25}-极大亏格三曲线（Klein四次曲线的S_4模型）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类满足自同构群在有理点上传递这一强对称性的极大曲线，刻画其结构并补全素数幂情形的完整分类。

Method: 设q=p^h为奇素数幂，X/F_{q^2}为亏格≥2的极大曲线；证明分驯顺与野性两种作用。驯顺情形用亏格界、商映射签名、特征零特化、低亏格自同构分类与Hasse–Witt计算归约到Klein四次曲线；野性情形把有理点等同于自同构群的Sylow p-子群，非循环Sylow用有限群分类定理与Henn大自同构分类处理，循环Sylow用局部分歧与Riemann–Hurwitz公式排除。

Result: h>1时X为Hermitian曲线；h=1时仅q=5有额外可能，即唯一的F_{25}-极大亏格三曲线，为Klein四次曲线的极大S_4-模型。结合已知特征二结果，得到所有素数幂情形的分类。

Conclusion: 该分类完全确定了在有理点上传递的亏格≥2极大曲线，表明除特征二已知情形外，仅Hermitian曲线及q=5的Klein四次曲线S_4-模型例外。

Abstract: Let $q=p^h$ be an odd prime power and let $X/\F_{q^2}$ be a maximal curve of genus at least two. We classify the curves for which the full geometric automorphism group is transitive on the set of $\F_{q^2}$-rational points. We prove that, if $h>1$, then $X$ is the Hermitian curve. If $h=1$, the only additional possibility occurs for $q=5$: the unique $\F_{25}$-maximal genus-three curve, namely the maximal $S_4$-model of the Klein quartic. The proof separates tame and wild actions. In the tame case, genus bounds, signatures of quotient maps, specialization to characteristic zero, low-genus automorphism classifications, and a Hasse--Witt computation reduce the problem to the Klein quartic. In the wild case, the rational points are identified with the Sylow $p$-subgroups of the automorphism group. Noncyclic Sylow subgroups are treated using a finite-group classification theorem together with Henn's large-automorphism classification, while cyclic Sylow subgroups are excluded by local ramification and the Riemann--Hurwitz formula. Combined with the known characteristic-two result, this yields the classification for all prime powers.

</details>


### [128] [Bicomplex Characteristic Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17692)
*José Antonio Arciniega-Nevárez,Yesenia Bravo-Ortega,Inácio Rabelo,Agustín Romano-Velázquez*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文引入双复纤维丛，建立双复特征类与流形上几乎双复结构的理论框架，并给出其分类、特征类性质及应用与障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典复丛的Chern类理论已成熟，但双复（bicomplex）丛及相应的特征类理论尚未系统建立。本文旨在推广复丛与几乎复结构理论，研究双复纤维丛的分类与特征类，并刻画几乎双复流形的存在性问题及障碍。

Method: 1）给出双复纤维丛的基本定义与性质，包括幂等分解；2）建立其同构类与到双复无穷Grassmannian映射的同伦类之间的对应；3）定义双复特征类，并对比其与底层复丛Chern类的关系；4）构造双复Chern特征并讨论其代数性质；5）将理论应用于几乎双复流形。

Result: 双复特征类一般不能由底层复丛的Chern类决定；建立了双复纤维丛的同伦分类对应；构造了具良好代数性质的双复Chern特征；在几乎双复流形上给出若干例子与存在性障碍。

Conclusion: 双复纤维丛理论提供了区别于经典复丛的新不变量，双复特征类包含Chern类未捕捉的信息，对几乎双复结构的存在性给出有效障碍与例证。

Abstract: We introduce bicomplex fiber bundles and develop a framework for bicomplex characteristic classes and almost bicomplex structures on smooth manifolds. In the first part, we present the basic definitions and properties of these bundles, including their fundamental idempotent decomposition and the correspondence between their isomorphism classes and homotopy classes of maps into the bicomplex infinite Grassmannian. We then define bicomplex characteristic classes and show that, in general, they are not determined by the Chern classes of the underlying complex bundle. We also construct the bicomplex Chern character and discuss its algebraic properties. Finally, we apply the resulting theory to almost bicomplex manifolds, providing examples and obstructions to the existence of such structures.

</details>


### [129] [Hodge integrals on admissible covers and tautological projections of Prym classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17703)
*Yoav Len,Sam Molcho,Navid Nabijou*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在阿贝尔簇的模空间中研究Prym轨迹的参数化，给出计算Prym轨迹类的基本投射（tatuological projection）的算法，并应用于Prym映射的度与Jacobian类的不可除性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prym轨迹在模空间中刻画了固定次数与monodromy数据下曲线覆盖所对应的Prym簇；以往关于Prym映射度等结果多为零散的特殊计算，缺乏统一框架，因此需要一套系统算法来计算其基本投射。

Method: 关键工具是 admissible covers 模空间上三类不同Hodge丛之间的比较公式；据此推导并实现了一个计算Prym轨迹类的基本投射的算法，并对二重、三重覆盖进行了计算机与手工计算，其中超椭圆Jacobian轨迹作为特例。

Result: （1）在余维为零的情形计算了Prym映射的度，统一了此前一系列零散结果；（2）在低亏格下证明了Jacobian类不能被Prym类整除。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一而可操作的计算框架，将Prym轨迹的基本投射计算系统化，并在Prym映射度与Jacobian类不可除性两个方向给出新应用。

Abstract: In the moduli space of abelian varieties, Prym loci parametrise Prym varieties associated to covers of curves with fixed degree and monodromy data. We derive and implement an algorithm computing the tautological projections of classes of Prym loci. The key input is a comparison formula relating three different Hodge bundles on moduli spaces of admissible covers. We present computer and hand calculations for double and triple covers, with the locus of hyperelliptic Jacobians as a special case. Two applications follow. First, we calculate the degrees of the Prym maps in the codimension-zero cases, unifying a series of previously ad hoc results. Second, we prove in low genera the non-divisibility of the Jacobian class by the Prym class.

</details>


### [130] [On Logarithmic Donaldson-Thomas invariants for local Calabi-Yau $4$-folds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17720)
*Xiaolong Liu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究局部对数Calabi-Yau 4-流形（带简单正规交叉除子）的对数DT不变量，构造相对与族对数DT₄理论并证明退化公式，最终显式计算局部曲面snc对的零维对数DT₄不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对数Donaldson-Thomas理论是计数几何的重要方向，但对高维（4-流形）局部对数Calabi-Yau情形及其退化性质尚缺乏系统构造，作者旨在建立DT₄的对数与族版本框架以填补这一空白。

Method: 利用Khan-Kinjo-Park-Safronov宣布的移位拉格朗日类的族版本，构造曲线对数Hilbert概形的相对与族对数DT₄理论并证明退化公式；对点对数Hilbert概形构造虚拟基本类并在Chow群中证明退化公式；再结合对数配边进行计算。

Result: 对曲线对数Hilbert概形建立了相对与族对数DT₄理论并证明退化公式；对点对数Hilbert概形构造虚拟类并证明Chow群中的退化公式，且该公式不依赖于移位拉格朗日类；结合退化公式与对数配边，显式算出局部曲面snc对的零维对数DT₄不变量。

Conclusion: 本文系统建立了局部对数Calabi-Yau 4-流形的对数DT₄理论及退化公式，并给出局部曲面snc对零维不变量的显式计算结果，为高维对数计数几何提供了新工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we study logarithmic Donaldson-Thomas invariants for local log Calabi-Yau $4$-folds with simple normal crossing divisors. Using the family version of shifted Lagrangian classes announced by Khan-Kinjo-Park-Safronov, we construct relative and family logarithmic $\mathsf{DT}_4$-theories for logarithmic Hilbert schemes of curves, and prove a degeneration formula. For logarithmic Hilbert schemes of points, we construct virtual classes and prove a degeneration formula in Chow groups; in particular, it is independent of shifted Lagrangian classes. Finally, combining our degeneration formula with logarithmic cobordism, we explicitly compute the zero-dimensional logarithmic $\mathsf{DT}_4$-invariants for local surface snc pairs.

</details>


### [131] [An elementary way to find a counterexample to the Jacobian Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17795)
*Arno van den Essen*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文用初等数学方法构造出Jacobian猜想的一个反例，该反例在相差线性坐标变换的意义下与Levent Alpöge所给出的例子一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jacobian猜想是代数几何与多项式映射理论中的经典未决问题，通常的处理依赖较深的高等工具。作者希望表明，寻找反例并不必然需要复杂的理论，而可以通过初等方法实现，从而降低对该问题的理解门槛，并澄清Alpöge例子与初等构造之间的等价关系。

Method: 采用初等数学手段（如多项式计算与线性坐标变换的化简）直接构造候选映射，检验其Jacobian行列式为非零常数且映射非单射（或不可逆），从而得到一个反例。随后通过线性坐标变换将其与Alpöge的例子进行比较，证明二者等价。

Result: 成功给出一个Jacobian猜想的反例，并证明该例子与Alpöge的反例在相差线性坐标变换下本质相同。

Conclusion: Jacobian猜想的反例可以用初等方法获得，且与已知的Alpöge反例实质等价；这说明该问题的反例结构可以以更通俗的方式呈现。

Abstract: We show how a counterexample to the Jacobian Conjecture can be found by elementary mathematics. The example we find is, apart from linear coordinate changes the example found by Levent Alpöge.

</details>


### [132] [On the singularities and the Kodaira dimension of unitary Shimura varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18146)
*Shuji Horinaga,Yota Maeda*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究$\mathrm{U}(p,q)$型Hermitian Shimura簇的几何，构造余维1边界处的环面紧化，证明在$p\ge w_E$且$(p,q)\neq(2,2),(2,3)$时其仅有至多典范奇点；结合Arthur重数公式得出满足$p+q\equiv1\pmod{w_E}$的非一般型情形仅有有限多对$(p,q)$；并将$\mathrm{O}^+(2,n)$的奇点界改进至$n\ge6$且为最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Hermitian Shimura簇的奇点性质及其是否一般型（general type），是与Kodaira维数、模形式几何、乃至算术应用相关的基本问题；同时希望改进Hulek等对$\mathrm{O}^+(2,n)$型正交Shimura簇的奇点界。

Method: 对虚二次域$E$上符号为$(p,q)$的Hermitian形式所定义的Shimura簇，构造带环面（toroidal）紧化，并对边界余维1奇点作显式分析；结合Arthur重数公式将奇点分析与自守表示的重数条件相联系。

Result: 在$2\le p\le q$、$p\ge w_E$（$w_E:=\#\mathscr{O}_E^\times$）且$(p,q)\neq(2,2),(2,3)$时，得到仅具至多典范奇点的环面紧化；由此证明满足$p+q\equiv1\pmod{w_E}$的非一般型unitäres Shimura簇至多对应有限多对$(p,q)$；并把$\mathrm{O}^+(2,n)$的奇点界从Gritsenko–Hulek–Sankaran的范围改进到$n\ge6$，且说明该界是尖锐的。

Conclusion: 结果给出unitäres Shimura簇在边界处“可控奇点”的构造性机制，并显示其一般型性质在算术条件下几乎必然成立；同时为$\mathrm{O}^+(2,n)$型给出最优奇点界。

Abstract: We study the geometry of Shimura varieties associated with a Hermitian form of signature $(p,q)$ over an imaginary quadratic field $E$, where $2\leq p\leq q$. We prove that when $p\geq w_E$ and $(p,q)\neq(2,2),(2,3)$, where $w_E:=\#\mathscr{O}_E^\times$, there exists a toroidal compactification with at worst canonical singularities. As an application, combining this singularity analysis with Arthur's multiplicity formula, we prove that only finitely many pairs $(p,q)$ satisfying the above conditions and $p+q\equiv1\pmod{w_E}$ give rise to unitary Shimura varieties that are not of general type. Our method also improves the singularity bound of Gritsenko--Hulek--Sankaran (Invent.\ Math., 2007) for $\mathrm{O}^+(2,n)$ to the range $n\geq6$ and shows that this bound is sharp.

</details>


### [133] [Geometry of three--generated ideals in the plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17799)
*Felipe Monteiro*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究 k[x,y,z] 中由同次数三个形式极小生成齐次理想的参数空间几何，按基概型次数与 syzygy 初始次数分层，利用 Quot/Hilbert 概型实现、证明光滑性与不可约性，并给出向量丛解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画三生成齐次理想参数空间的几何结构，理解其分层、光滑性、不可约性与梯度理想、平面曲线之间的联系，并为 du Plessis–Wall 界提供向量丛视角。

Method: 通过 Quot 与 Hilbert 概型实现各分层，构造 Bourbaki 概型的万有族；分析 free loci 的光滑性与不可约性；利用 syzygy 丛的斜率半稳定性给出向量丛解释；对三次形式三元组计算所有非空次数分层。

Result: 证明 free loci 的光滑性与不可约性，得到任意三生成理想的相同数值限制，并对三次形式三元组确定所有非空次数分层、证明其光滑不可约，同时用约化四次平面曲线的 Bourbaki 分层刻画梯度 loci。

Conclusion: 该工作统一了参数空间的实现、分层与向量丛方法，深化了对三生成齐次理想几何及其与梯度理想、平面曲线关系的理解。

Abstract: We study the geometry of parameter spaces of homogeneous ideals in k[x,y,z] minimally generated by three forms of the same degree, stratified by the degree of their base scheme and by the initial degree of their syzygies. We realize these strata through Quot and Hilbert schemes, construct universal families of Bourbaki schemes, and prove smoothness and irreducibility results for the free loci. We then compare these spaces with the loci of gradient ideals and plane curves. Using slope semistability of the associated syzygy bundles, we give a vector-bundle interpretation of the refined du Plessis--Wall bounds and obtain the same numerical restriction for arbitrary three-generated ideals. Finally, in the case of triples of cubic forms, we determine all nonempty degree strata, prove that they are smooth and irreducible, and identify their gradient loci in terms of the Bourbaki stratification of reduced quartic plane curves.

</details>


### [134] [Toric vector bundles with trivial Chern class and flag decorations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17898)
*Sergio Cristancho*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究完全环面簇上等变陈类平凡的环面向量丛，采用Kaveh–Manon的tropical环面向量丛（分段线性映射）方法，证明秩r且等变陈根仿射无关的丛经扭后可退化为维数≤r-1的有限族拉回，推广了Payne关于r≤3的结果，并构造了不存在此类非平凡丛的完全环面簇例子，同时引入permutohedra的flag decorations组合工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Payne已证明秩r≤3、等变陈类平凡的环面向量丛具有强约束（可退化为低维拉回），自然的问题是这一现象对任意秩r是否成立，以及在高维完全环面簇上是否存在完全不存在此类非平凡丛的例子。作者希望用tropical（分段线性）视角统一并推广该分类结果。

Method: 采用Kaveh–Manon提出的tropical环面向量丛概念，将其视为分段线性映射；分析等变陈类平凡的条件与等变陈根（equivariant Chern roots）的仿射无关性；利用并引入permutohedra的flag decorations这一组合结构，借助其凸性性质来实现对向量丛的等价分类与拉回分解。

Result: 证明：秩r、陈类平凡且等变陈根仿射无关的环面向量丛，在字符扭的意义下等变同构于来自某个维数不超过r-1的有限族簇的拉回丛；该结论推广了Payne关于r≤3的定理。作为应用，构造出维数n的完全环面簇，其不存在满足上述性质的秩r≤n+1的非平凡环面向量丛。

Conclusion: tropical方法为环面向量丛的陈类分类提供了有效框架，等变陈根的仿射无关性与permutohedra的flag decorations凸性共同刻画了丛的退化行为；结果给出高维完全环面簇上此类丛的刚性/存在性限制。

Abstract: We study toric vector bundles on complete toric varieties whose total equivariant Chern classes are trivial. Our approach is tropical, using the notion of tropical toric vector bundles as piecewise linear maps introduced by Kaveh and Manon. We prove that any toric vector bundle of rank $r$ with trivial Chern class and affinely independent equivariant Chern roots is equivariantly isomorphic to a toric vector bundle pulled back from one of a finite set of varieties with dimension at most $r-1$ after twisting by a character. This extends a theorem of Payne about toric vector bundles of rank $r\leq 3$ with trivial Chern class. As an application, we construct examples of complete toric varieties of dimension $n$ that admit no nontrivial toric vector bundles of rank $r\leq n+1$ with the aforementioned properties. We also introduce combinatorial gadgets we call flag decorations of permutohedra, whose convexity properties are key for our results.

</details>


### [135] [Koszul Duality for Symmetric Algebras and Derived Loop Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18115)
*Isshin Hisamichi*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了连通复形的对称代数的Koszul对偶性（不要求下有界），并由此构造了任意特征为零域上分离有限型概形的导出环路空间与切复形对称代数之间的导出范畴等价，推广了Kapranov光滑情形下导出环路空间与余切丛的经典对偶，并给出派生形式堆的几何解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kapranov的经典结果建立了光滑情形下导出环路空间与余切丛之间的对偶。本文旨在将这一对偶从光滑情形推广到任意特征为零域上的分离有限型概形，为此需要发展不要求下有界的连通复形对称代数的Koszul对偶理论。

Method: 证明由有限秩局部自由层构成的连通复形（不必下有界）的对称代数的Koszul对偶性；将其应用于构造导出环路空间与切复形对称代数之间的导出范畴等价；并通过派生形式堆给出该等价的几何解释。

Result: 建立了不要求下有界的连通复形对称代数的Koszul对偶性；构造了任意特征为零域上分离有限型概形的导出环路空间与切复形对称代数之间的导出范畴等价；给出了该等价在派生形式堆下的几何解释。

Conclusion: 本文将Kapranov关于导出环路空间与余切丛的经典对偶推广到更一般的奇异情形，揭示了导出环路空间与切复形对称代数之间的对偶关系，并提供派生形式堆的几何视角。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove Koszul duality for symmetric algebras of connective complexes consisting of locally free sheaves of finite rank, which is not necessarily bounded below. As an application, we construct an equivalence between derived categories of derived loop spaces and the symmetric algebra of the tangent complex for arbitrary separated schemes of finite type over a field $k$ of characteristic zero. This can be regarded as a generalization of the classical duality between derived loop spaces and cotangent bundles by Kapranov in the smooth cases. We also give a geometrical interpretation of our equivalence in terms of derived formal stacks.

</details>


### [136] [Nodal deformations of hypersurfaces with an ordinary $m$-fold point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18313)
*Remke Kloosterman*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究含一个ordinary m重节点的nodal超曲面X，利用谱(spectrum)半连续性给出δ_X的上界，并构造出若干下界；这些下界是m的n次多项式，具有不同首项系数，改进了Ciliberto-Galati的先前结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定nodal超曲面X的δ_X（X落入含δ个节点的Severi簇闭包中的最大δ）是一个自然的模空间/退化问题。已有Ciliberto-Galati给出的界需要改进，且谱半连续性可提供上界，而缺乏对δ_X的更强下界。

Method: 首先回顾谱的半连续性，由此得到δ_X的上界；然后通过构造/分析方式获得δ_X的若干下界，这些下界以ordinary m重节点数m为变量的n次多项式表示，并比较不同构造对应的首项系数。

Result: 得到δ_X的多个下界，均为m的n次多项式且具有不同首项系数；同时给出基于谱半连续性的上界。这些下界优于Ciliberto-Galati先前给出的界。

Conclusion: 论文刻画了含ordinary m重节点的nodal超曲面在Severi簇闭包中的最大节点数δ_X的估计：谱半连续性给出上界，新构造的下界为m的n次多项式并改进已有结果。

Abstract: Let $X\subset \mathbb{O}^n$ be a nodal hypersurface of degree $d$ with an ordinary $m$-fold point. Let $δ_X$ be the largest value of $δ$ such that $X$ is contained in the closure of the Severi variety of degree $d$ hypersurface in $\mathbb{P}^n$ with $δ$ nodes.
  After recalling how the semicontinuity of the spectrum yields an upper bound for $δ_X$, we produce various lower bounds for $δ_X$. These bounds are given by polynomials in $m$ of degree $n$, with different leading coefficients. This improves previous bounds given by Ciliberto-Galati.

</details>


### [137] [Virtual classes: An introduction with exercises](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18454)
*Xuanchun Lu,Rachel Webb*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文引入Behrend-Fantechi意义下的虚拟类，说明其直接推广了Fulton的局部化拓扑Chern类，并在具有整体分解的完美障碍理论的Deligne-Mumford栈上推导出虚拟类的Siebert公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟类是代数几何（尤其Gromov-Witten理论、模空间相交理论）中的核心工具，但其与经典局部化拓扑Chern类的关系需要厘清，且虚拟类在一般情形下的计算公式（如Siebert公式）尚需系统推导。

Method: 在Behrend-Fantechi框架下定义虚拟类，将其与Fulton的局部化拓扑Chern类进行对比以建立推广关系；针对具有整体分解（global resolution）的完美障碍理论，采用局部化与分解技术推导Siebert公式。

Result: 证明了Behrend-Fantechi虚拟类直接推广Fulton局部化拓扑Chern类，并给出了任意具有整体分解的完美障碍理论的Deligne-Mumford栈上虚拟类的Siebert公式。

Conclusion: 虚拟类可视为局部化拓扑Chern类的自然推广，Siebert公式在更一般的Deligne-Mumford栈情形下成立，为虚拟类的计算提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We introduce virtual classes in the sense of Behrend-Fantechi, explaining how these directly generalize Fulton's localized top Chern class. We derive Siebert's formula for a virtual class on any Deligne-Mumford stack with a perfect obstruction theory having a global resolution.

</details>


### [138] [Deformations of Canonical Bundle for Smooth Weakly Kähler Morphisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18507)
*Liqingjing Wang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 在某光滑紧复流形族的中心纤维为Kähler且K_{X_0} nef（dim X_0=4）时，邻近纤维的典范丛仍为nef。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知中心纤维Kähler可推出邻近纤维Kähler且π为弱Kähler态射，但典范丛的正性（nef性）能否在族中保持尚不清楚，本文即研究这一形变稳定性问题。

Method: 设π: X→Δ为光滑紧复流形族，中心纤维X_0 Kähler，dim X_0=4且K_{X_0} nef；在此设定下分析邻近纤维X_t的典范丛K_{X_t}的nef性。

Result: 证明了当dim X_0=4且K_{X_0} nef时，对原点邻域内所有t，K_{X_t}均为nef。

Conclusion: 在四维中心纤维Kähler且K_{X_0} nef的情形下，典范丛的nef性在纤维族中局部保持。

Abstract: Let π: \mathcal X \rightarrow Δbe a smooth proper family of compact complex manifolds such that the central fiber \mathcal X_0 is Kähler. Then all the fibers close to 0 are Kähler and π is a weakly Kähler morphism, even if \mathcal X is not a Kähler space. In this paper we show that if dim \mathcal X_0 = 4 and K_{\mathcal X_0} is nef, then K_{\mathcal X_t} is nef for all t in a neighborhood of the origin.

</details>


### [139] [Pedal Curves of a Bicorn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18608)
*Thierry Dana-Picard,Moshe Hanau,Shmuel Krichevsky*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究双角曲线（bicorn）的垂足曲线，分析其尖点导致的奇异性，比较参数化与符号表示对输出质量的影响，并在动态几何与计算机代数系统环境中开展工作，最后探讨其与艺术及STEAM教育的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双角曲线含有两个尖点，会诱导垂足曲线产生奇异性，因此需要针对每种情形研究其奇异性。同时，不同曲线表示方式（参数化与符号）对结果质量有影响，值得探讨。

Method: 采用参数化表示和符号表示两种方式描述曲线；特别地，确定自交点需要参数化表示，其中有理参数化最为高效。工作在包含动态几何、自动化命令和计算机代数系统的环境中完成。

Result: 分析了双角曲线垂足曲线在各情形下的奇异性，比较了不同表示方式的性质及其对输出质量的影响，并建立了平面曲线与艺术之间的联系。

Conclusion: 该主题适合用于STEAM教育，因其结合了数学、几何计算与艺术元素。

Abstract: We explore pedal curves (a special case of a geometric locus) of a the plane curve called a bicorn. This curve has two cusps, which induce the existence of singularities of the pedal. We study the singularities of the pedals in each case. For this various presentations are used for the curves, parametric and symbolic presentations. Their properties and influence on the quality of the output is briefly discussed. In particular, the determination of points of self?intersection requires a parametric presentation; in this case a rational parametrization is the most efficient. Work is performed in an environment involving Dynamic Geometry, automated commands and a Computer Algebra System. Finally we describe a connection between our plane curves and some art, making the topic here suitable for STEAM education.

</details>


### [140] [On curves with one place at infinity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18719)
*Abdallah Assi,Wael Mahboub*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述，回顾了与K^2上"在无穷远处仅有一个分支"的平面曲线相关的数值半群的算术性质，以及由此获得的几何结果，并提出了若干未解决问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K^2上具有无穷远处单分支性质的平面曲线的许多几何性质，可从与其关联的数值半群的算术中导出。作者希望系统梳理这一联系，强调半群这一代数工具在几何研究中的核心作用。

Method: 综述性方法：在特征为零的代数闭域K的框架下，回顾这类曲线的数值半群的主要性质，并总结基于这些性质所得到的几何结果，同时提出若干待解决问题。

Result: 论文汇总了无穷远处单分支平面曲线所对应数值半群的关键性质，及其所对应的几何结论（如曲线的拓扑与奇点性质等），并列举了开放问题。

Conclusion: 数值半群的算术性质为研究无穷远处单分支平面曲线的几何提供了有效框架，该领域仍有待解决的重要问题。

Abstract: Let K be an algebraically closed field of characteristic zero. Many of the geometric properties of the plane curves in K^2 with one place at infinity can be obtained from the arithmetic of numerical semigroups associated with these curves. In this survey, we recall the main properties of these semigroups as well as the geometric results obtained from these properties. We also address some problems.

</details>


### [141] [Monodromic Perverse Sheaves on Shifted Contact Stacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18796)
*Efe İzbudak*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 在-1-shifted contact derived Artin stack的导出symplectification上应用BBDJS极小模型并沿$\mathbb{G}_m$作用代数下降，构造$\ell$-adic perverse sheaf与tame twisted monodromy算子$\theta$，建立contact版Joyce猜想，并给出Legendrian不变量与DT不变量的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将BBDJS极小模型与导出symplectification结合，把symplectic侧的Joyce猜想与不变量理论推广到contact情形，从而在contact derived Artin stack上定义local class、单值化算子与不变量，并刻画其与virtual dimension奇偶性的关系。

Method: 对-1-shifted contact derived Artin stack的导出symplectification应用BBDJS极小模型，沿结构自由$\mathbb{G}_m$-作用代数下降，构造$\ell$-adic perverse sheaf；利用Verdier关于monodromic sheaves的specialization等价赋予tame twisted monodromy算子$\theta$；用$\ell$-adic pull-push函子构造categorified Legendrian 2-categories $\mathfrak{L}\mathcal{F}_c(X)$与$LLeg_0$。

Result: 证明Legendre $L$的local fundamental class满足$\theta\circ\mu_L=(-1)^{\mathrm{vdim}L}\mu_L$，故奇数virtual dimension的Legendrian上该类因$\theta$-不变性而消失；两种奇偶性在$A_1$ chart上均已出现，且奇Legendrian可携带非零local class。contact Behrend函数恒等于1，故相关DT不变量等于经典截断的紧支étale Euler示性数，$\theta$的高次迹恢复首次迹所丢弃的奇点类型。应用上，cotangent bundle中conic Lagrangians导出交的symplectic不变量恒为零，而contact不变量计算射影化交的Euler示性数并给出conormal bundle显式公式。

Conclusion: 建立了contact版Joyce猜想的框架，构造了categorified Legendrian 2-categories，并说明contact不变量相较symplectic不变量能保留更多信息（如奇点类型与射影化交的Euler示性数），同时给出contact DT不变量的Euler示性数解释。

Abstract: Applying the BBDJS minimal model to the derived symplectification of a $-1$-shifted contact derived Artin stack and descending algebraically along the structural free $\mathbb{G}_m$-action, we construct an $\ell$-adic perverse sheaf on any oriented such stack, and use Verdier's specialization equivalence for monodromic sheaves to equip it with a tame twisted monodromy operator $θ$. We show that the local fundamental class of a Legendrian $L$ satisfies $θ\circ μ_L = (-1)^{\mathrm{vdim} L}μ_L$, so that on Legendrians of odd virtual dimension, the class vanishes due to $θ$-invariance. Moreover, both parities occur already on the $A_1$ chart while an odd Legendrian can carry a nonzero local class. We further formulate a contact analogue of Joyce's conjecture for a graded orientation, in which the orientation datum is twisted by the parity of the virtual dimension. Under the assumption of a monodromic refinement of the symplectic conjecture, we construct the categorified Legendrian 2-categories $\mathfrak{L}\mathcal{F}_c(X)$ and $LLeg_0$ via $\ell$-adic pull-push functors. Finally, we show that the contact Behrend function is identically $1$, so that the associated Donaldson-Thomas invariant is the compactly supported étale Euler characteristic of the classical truncation, and that the higher traces of $θ$ recover the singularity type that the first trace discards. As an application, we show that the symplectic invariant of a derived intersection of conic Lagrangians in a cotangent bundle vanishes identically, while the contact invariant computes the Euler characteristic of the projectivized intersection, with an explicit formula for conormal bundles.

</details>


### [142] [Surfaces of general type with extremal cotangent dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18798)
*Damian Brotbek,Bruno de Oliveira,Erwan Rousseau*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究具有极值余切维数的广义型曲面的地理学：既包括无全局全纯对称微分（ct=0），也包括余切丛丰沛（ct=2）。重点处理低斜率 K^2/χ 曲面，证明极小有理曲面上对称对数微分的消没定理及其双覆盖上微分的消没定理，并将 Sakai 的丰沛判据推广到 Campana 意义下的一般型纤维化。由此得到：一般 Horikawa 曲面无非平凡对称微分，同时存在余切丛丰沛的 Horikawa 曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关注极值余切维数的广义型曲面（ct=0 或 ct=2），尤其是此前方法难以触及的低斜率 K^2/χ 曲面，以刻画其对称微分的存在性与丰沛性。

Method: 证明极小有理曲面上对称对数微分的消没定理，以及其双覆盖上微分的消没定理；将 Sakai 的丰沛判据推广至 Campana 意义下的一般型纤维化。

Result: 一方面证明一般 Horikawa 曲面没有非平凡对称微分；另一方面证明存在余切丛丰沛的 Horikawa 曲面。

Conclusion: 通过新的消没定理与丰沛判据，将极值余切维数曲面 geography 的研究扩展至低斜率情形，并给出 Horikawa 曲面的两个互补结论。

Abstract: We study the geography of surfaces of general type with extremal cotangent dimension, in other words surfaces which have either no global holomorphic symmetric differentials at all or the maximal asymptotic growth of their number, i.e. big cotangent bundle. We are mainly interested in surfaces with low slope K^2 /$χ$, which, as far as maximal cotangent dimension is concerned, were out of reach of previous methods. We prove vanishing theorems for symmetric logarithmic differentials on minimal rational surfaces and for differentials on their double covers and extend a bigness criterion of Sakai to fibrations of general type in the sense of Campana. As a consequence, we prove, on the one hand that generic Horikawa surfaces have no nontrivial symmetric differentials and on the other hand that there exist Horikawa surfaces with big cotangent bundle.

</details>


### [143] [Bertini's theorem for $F$-rationality is false](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18921)
*Thomas Polstra,Austyn Simpson*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 在 k=overline{F_2} 上构造了九维 F-有理仿射簇及射影簇，并证明其超平面截口几乎都不 F-有理（也不 F-单射）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 F-有理性与 F-单射性在超平面截口下是否保持，构造反例说明即使原簇为 F-有理，其一般超平面截口也可能失去该性质。

Method: 在代数闭域 k=overline{F_2} 上显式构造九维 F-有理仿射簇 X，嵌入 P^19，并构造射影版本 \mathfrak{X} 嵌入 P^29。

Result: 构造出九维 F-有理仿射簇 X⊂P^19，存在稠密开集 U⊂(P^19)^∨，使所有 H∈U 上 X∩H 非 F-有理甚至非 F-单射；射影情形在 P^29 中得到类似结论。

Conclusion: F-有理性和 F-单射性在超平面截口下不保持，即使原簇为 F-有理，一般超平面截口也可能丧失该性质。

Abstract: Let $k=\overline{\mathbb{F}_2}$. We construct a nine-dimensional $F$-rational affine variety $X$ admitting a locally closed embedding $X\hookrightarrow \mathbb{P}_k^{19}$ together with a dense open set $U\subseteq (\mathbb{P}^{19})^\vee$ such that $X\cap H$ is not $F$-rational (or even $F$-injective) for all $H\in U$. We also construct a nine-dimensional projective $F$-rational variety $\mathfrak{X}$ and a closed embedding $\mathfrak{X}\hookrightarrow\mathbb{P}^{29}_k$ under which the same conclusion holds.

</details>


### [144] [Relative cone of curves and extremal contractions of a successive blowup](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18936)
*Yuto Masamura*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究正规簇沿简单正规交叉子簇序列的连续爆破，证明相对曲线锥由有限个初等曲线类生成、每个面都可收缩，并刻画极射线收缩的例外轨迹以及小极射线收缩的D-flip存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对极小模型纲领中，曲线锥结构与收缩存在性是核心问题。对于沿子簇序列的连续爆破这一具体几何构造，人们希望明确其相对锥的性质，从而把一般MMP理论的结果落实到此情形，并理解极射线收缩的几何形态。

Method: 设X为正规簇，π为沿余维至少二的子簇Z_1,…,Z_n的连续爆破，各Z_i具简单正规交叉且满足包含关系条件Z_h⊉Z_i(h<i)。作者直接分析相对曲线锥，利用爆破的纤维结构与初等曲线的显式构造，结合相对锥定理与flip理论进行论证。

Result: 证明相对曲线锥的闭包由有限多条初等曲线的类生成；每个面都允许一个相对X的收缩；给出极射线收缩例外轨迹的描述；并证明对每条小极射线收缩及在其对应射线上为负的任意R-Cartier除子D，都存在D-flip。

Conclusion: 沿简单正规交叉子簇序列的连续爆破具有良好控制的相对锥结构，其极射线收缩可由有限初等曲线刻画，且小收缩的flip存在性成立，为后续MMP应用提供了基础。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a normal variety, and let $π\colon\tilde X\to X$ be the successive blowup along subvarieties $Z_1,\dotsc,Z_n\subseteq X$ of codimension at least two that have simple normal crossings and satisfy $Z_h\not\supseteq Z_i$ whenever $h<I$. We prove that the relative cone of curves $\overline{\operatorname{NE}}(\tilde X/X)$ is generated by the classes of finitely many elementary curves, and that every face admits a contraction over $X$. We describe the exceptional loci of extremal ray contractions, and prove that every small extremal ray contraction admits a $D$-flip for every $\mathbb R$-Cartier divisor $D$ negative on the corresponding ray.

</details>


### [145] [Belyi's theorem: coverings, dessins, and fields of definition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19053)
*Djounvouna Dinamo*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文对Belyi定理给出完整自足的证明（含全部下降机制），并研究该证明产生的Belyi多项式与极值Belyi映射的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Belyi定理通常的证明依赖被引用的下降理论；本文希望提供无需引用、完全自足的阐述，并深入研究证明过程中产生的具体数学对象（Belyi多项式、dessins、单值群、极值映射）。

Method: 对Belyi定理两个方向给出自足证明；研究Belyi多项式P_{m,n}(z)=((m+n)^{m+n}/(m^m n^n))z^m(1-z)^n的dessin结构与分解恒等式；分析极值Belyi映射的单值群与质量公式，并用自足的Frobenius计算重证Boccara循环分解计数。

Result: P_{m,n}的dessin为双星；满足P_{m,n}=π_k∘P_{m/k,n/k}（k=gcd(m,n)）；单值群为S_{(m+n)/k}≀Z/k，当gcd(m,n)=1时为全对称群。达到d=2g+1的极值映射单值群落在交错群中，未必循环；最小非循环例子次数5、亏格2、单值群A_5、定义于Q。质量公式Σ1/|Aut|=2(d-1)!/d(d+1)，已由枚举d≤7验证。有理化步骤次数上界为N!（N为无理分支值个数）。

Conclusion: Belyi定理可完全自足证明；其产物具有精细结构，极值映射满足单值群与质量公式约束，并给出全计算示例及G_Q轨道与Q(∛2)嵌入的对应。

Abstract: Belyi's theorem asserts that a smooth projective curve over $\mathbb{C}$ is defined over a number field if and only if it admits a non-constant morphism to $\mathbb{P}^1$ ramified over at most three points. This article gives a complete, self-contained account of the theorem -- both implications, with all descent machinery proved rather than quoted -- together with a study of the objects the proof produces. Beyond the exposition it contains original results, proved in full. The principal one is a structure theorem for the Belyi polynomials $P_{m,n}(z)=\frac{(m+n)^{m+n}}{m^{m}n^{n}}z^{m}(1-z)^{n}$: their dessins are double stars, they satisfy the exact identity $P_{m,n}=π_{k}\circ P_{m/k,n/k}$ with $k=\gcd(m,n)$ and $π_{k}(w)=w^{k}$, and their monodromy is the wreath product $\mathfrak{S}_{(m+n)/k}\wr\mathbb{Z}/k$, the full symmetric group precisely when $\gcd(m,n)=1$. Around the sharp lower bounds $d\geq 2g+1$ (all Belyi maps) and $d\geq 4g$ (clean maps) we study the extremal maps attaining $d=2g+1$: their monodromy lies in the alternating group, they need not be cyclic -- the smallest non-cyclic one has degree $5$, genus $2$, monodromy $A_{5}$, and is defined over $\mathbb{Q}$ -- and they satisfy the exact mass formula $\sum 1/|\mathrm{Aut}|=2(d-1)!/d(d+1)$, obtained from Boccara's cycle-factorization count -- reproved by a self-contained Frobenius computation -- and verified by complete enumeration for $d\leq7$. We also bound the degree of the rationalization step of Belyi's algorithm by $N!$ in the number $N$ of irrational branch values. The theory is illustrated by fully computed examples, and by an explicit $G_{\mathbb{Q}}$-orbit of three plane trees whose fields of moduli are the three conjugate embeddings of the non-Galois cubic field $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt[3]{2})$.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [The Global Geometry of the Gaussian Bures Manifold: Admissible Domain, Spectral Boundary, and Asymptotic Classicality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17563)
*Christian Kerskens*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于高斯对称对数导数方程构造中心玻色高斯态的协方差扇区Bures几何，并确定其量子容许域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究协方差高斯态Bures几何的边界结构与量子容许域，以厘清量子几何与经典Fisher–Rao几何的过渡与区别。

Method: 从高斯对称对数导数方程构造协方差扇区Bures几何，分析Williamson不确定度边界处的各向异性、协变余度量核，并结合势函数与热族比较，辅以Bures、Fisher–Rao与作用匹配Bures–Wasserstein行列式密度的比较。

Result: 在Williamson不确定度下界处边界几何呈各向异性：改变谱的径向系数发散，而纯高斯轨道上的压缩与旋转方向保持有限；当m个模式变纯时，协变余度量获得精确m^2维核，但边界仍处于有限径向Bures距离。导出势Φ并等于固定哈密顿热族上的βF。大辛特征值下相对量子修正为O(ħ^2/ν^2)，恢复协方差Fisher–Rao几何。三类行列式密度在一模式处存在精确交汇点，此交汇为运动学性质，分支解释是有条件的。在给定有限非零压缩率下，Bures作用发散而Bures–Wasserstein代价有限，有界Bures预算则迫使径向减速。

Conclusion: 协方差Bures几何在不确定度边界上表现出各向异性与有限径向距离，并在大辛特征值极限下退化为协方差Fisher–Rao几何；其内在经典区制与纯态边界不同，需要指定测量通道。三者行列式密度的交汇点为运动学性质的、分支解释有条件的现象。

Abstract: We construct the covariance-sector Bures geometry of centered bosonic Gaussian states from the Gaussian symmetric-logarithmic-derivative equation and determine its quantum-admissible domain. At the Williamson uncertainty floor, the boundary geometry is anisotropic: spectrum-changing radial coefficients diverge, whereas squeezing and rotation directions along the pure Gaussian orbit remain finite. Dually, the covariance cometric acquires an exact \(m^2\)-dimensional kernel when \(m\) modes become pure, yet the boundary remains at finite radial Bures distance. We derive the potential \[ Φ=-\tfrac12\sum_k\log(ν_k^2-\tfrac14), \] which generates fixed-frame covariance dilation and equals \(βF\) on fixed-Hamiltonian thermal families. At large symplectic eigenvalue, the relative quantum correction is \(O(\hbar^2/ν^2)\), recovering covariance Fisher--Rao geometry. This intrinsic classical regime differs from the pure-state boundary, where classical statistics require a specified measurement channel. As a secondary comparison, the normalized Bures, Fisher--Rao, and action-matched Bures--Wasserstein determinant densities possess an exact one-mode junction at \[ (x,η)=(\sqrt{\varphi},\sqrt{\varphi}),\qquad x=2ν/\hbar. \] This junction is kinematic, and its branch interpretation is conditional. At a prescribed finite nonzero compression rate, the Bures action diverges while the Bures--Wasserstein cost remains finite; a bounded Bures budget instead enforces radial deceleration.

</details>


### [147] [The Golden Junction of Gaussian Covariance Geometries: Sector Anisotropy, Relative-Entropy Response, and Local Penalties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17574)
*Christian Kerskens*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了中心单模玻色高斯协方差的四种局部几何：Bures、协方差 Fisher–Rao、Bures–Wasserstein 与归一化 Bogoliubov–Kubo–Mori 度量。在共同的迹正交归一化下，Bures 与 Fisher–Rao 余度量的行列式在 x²=φ 处内禀交叉；引入常数作用量匹配参数 η 后，Bures–Wasserstein 仅在 (x,η)=(√φ,√φ) 加入该交叉。行列式密度相等并不蕴含张量相等：在交界处 Bures 余度量相对于 Fisher–Rao 为 Q_gold=φ⁻²Π₀+φΠ₂，det Q_gold=1，其逆具有精确的标量—权重二二次惩罚比 φ³。归一化 BKM 度量提供独立的相对熵诊断，径向与 Bures 一致，但形状余度量本征值为 4ħ²x/log[(x+1)/(x−1)]，故不形成第四个行列式交界，并在纯态边界切向变硬。操作上，权重二运动由仅系统的高斯幺正生成，径向运动则需高斯信道或辅助膨胀。已知的 Uhlmann–Ruan 纯化商实现局部 Bures 态度量，但显式 sp(4,R) 检验表明无权右不变 Frobenius 门范数不能重现黄金惩罚。条件分支选择与有限速率 Bures–Wasserstein 代价分离作为次级结论给出，而非动力学相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望厘清四种中心单模玻色高斯协方差局部几何之间的异同，避免因行列式或径向重合而混淆其操作含义。核心动机是在统一归一化下判别这些度量是否真正等价（张量层面），以及它们与相对熵诊断、纯化商、门范数惩罚之间的关系。

Method: 采用共同迹正交归一化比较 Bures、协方差 Fisher–Rao、Bures–Wasserstein 与归一化 Bogoliubov–Kubo–Mori 度量的行列式与本征结构；引入作用量匹配参数 η 定位 Bures–Wasserstein 的交界；通过 Q_gold=φ⁻²Π₀+φΠ₂ 分析交界处张量差异及逆度量的二次惩罚比；用 BKM 形状余度量本征值检验纯态边界行为；并用显式 sp(4,R) 检验无权右不变 Frobenius 门范数。

Result: Bures 与 Fisher–Rao 行列式在 x²=φ 处交叉；引入 η 后 Bures–Wasserstein 仅在 (x,η)=(√φ,√φ) 加入。交界处 det Q_gold=1，逆度量标量—权重二惩罚比为 φ³。BKM 径向与 Bures 一致，但形状本征值为 4ħ²x/log[(x+1)/(x−1)]，不形成第四个行列式交界，并在纯态边界切向变硬。无权右不变 Frobenius 门范数不能重现黄金惩罚。

Conclusion: 四种局部几何的行列式重合不代表张量等价；操作含义需区分权重二（仅系统高斯幺正）与径向（高斯信道或辅助膨胀）运动。BKM 提供独立诊断但不形成第四交界。条件分支选择与有限速率 Bures–Wasserstein 代价分离仅为次级结论，不应解释为动力学相变。

Abstract: We compare four local geometries of centered one-mode bosonic Gaussian covariances while keeping their operational meanings distinct. In a common trace-orthonormal normalization, the Bures and covariance Fisher--Rao cometric determinants cross intrinsically at \(x^2=\varphi\). After introducing a constant action-matching parameter \(η\), the Bures--Wasserstein determinant joins them only at \((x,η)=(\sqrt\varphi,\sqrt\varphi)\). Equal determinant density does not mean tensor equality: relative to Fisher--Rao, the Bures cometric at the junction is \[
  Q_{\rm gold}=\varphi^{-2}Π_0+\varphiΠ_2,
  \qquad \det Q_{\rm gold}=1, \] so its inverse has the exact scalar-to-weight-two quadratic penalty ratio \(\varphi^3\). The normalized Bogoliubov--Kubo--Mori metric supplies an independent relative-entropy diagnostic. It agrees radially with Bures but has shape cometric eigenvalue \(4\hbar^2x/\log[(x+1)/(x-1)]\); it therefore does not form a fourth determinant junction and becomes tangentially stiff at the pure-state boundary. Operationally, weight-two motion is generated by a system-only Gaussian unitary, whereas radial motion requires a Gaussian channel or ancillary dilation. The known Uhlmann--Ruan purification quotient realizes the local Bures state metric, but an explicit \(\mathfrak{sp}(4,\mathbb R)\) test shows that an unweighted right-invariant Frobenius gate norm does not reproduce the golden penalty. Conditional branch selection and finite-rate Bures--Wasserstein cost separation are presented as secondary statements, not as dynamical phase transitions.

</details>


### [148] [Adaptive Record Consistency in a Schulman-Type Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17607)
*D. M. Theshan N. Weerasinghe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为有限宽度共面Schulman型源建立了精确的定量复合约束，给出自适应信号见证、唯一标量修正及探测器记忆实现，并在设定反馈下求得最坏扰动最小值与线性改进系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验回溯因果模型在经典反馈连接实验时能否保持可观测记录，量化孤立源精度与自适应记录保持之间的兼容性。

Method: 采用正卷绕Cauchy传播权、有限宽度共面Schulman型源，构造复合约束与自适应信号见证；引入唯一确定的标量修正与制备记忆探测器；通过最坏扰动最小化、有限误差界、连续多项式证书及五原子探测器构造进行证明。

Result: 原始分段复合存在自适应信号见证；标量修正保持记录延续；精确校准强制等质量对跖原子响应；设定反馈下ε⋆(0)=[2cosh(2Γ)]⁻¹；四设定Γ=0.4时ε⋆(δ)=ε⋆(0)-C₀.₄δ+O(δ²)，C₀.₄≈9.351091771057010，且正五原子探测器达到该系数。

Conclusion: 结果量化了孤立源精度与自适应记录保持的兼容性，并为具有显式制备、记忆与控制的回溯因果装置模型提供了精确设计条件。

Abstract: Adaptive measurements test whether a retrocausal model preserves observable records when experiments are connected by classical feedback. We establish exact and quantitative composition constraints for a finite-width, coplanar Schulman-type source with positive wrapped-Cauchy propagation weights. Raw segment composition admits an explicit adaptive signalling witness. A uniquely determined scalar correction preserves recorded continuations, and a detector with preparation memory implements this correction while retaining the source correlations. For common, independently prepared terminal detectors coupled to the source through arrival angles, exact calibration forces equal-mass antipodal atomic responses. Under setting-only raw feedback, the sharp minimum worst disturbance of an earlier record is $ε_\star(0)=[2\cosh(2Γ)]^{-1}$, where $Γ$ is the total source-path width. We derive explicit finite-error bounds and prove linear small-error improvement. For four settings at $Γ=0.4$, a uniform reduction over arbitrary nonnegative response measures and a continuous polynomial certificate establish $ε_\star(δ)=ε_\star(0)-C_{0.4}δ+O(δ^2)$, with $C_{0.4}\simeq9.351091771057010$. A positive five-atom detector attains this coefficient. The results quantify the compatibility of isolated-source accuracy and adaptive record preservation, and provide precise design conditions for retrocausal apparatus models with explicit preparation, memory, and control.

</details>


### [149] [Quantum Encodings, Private Messages, and Communication Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17690)
*Tom Gur,Miryam Mi-Ying Huang,Er-Cheng Tang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子可分解随机编码（QDRE）的复杂度，建立其与量子私有同时消息协议（QPSM）通信复杂度模型的双向联系，并给出在预共享纠缠不同约束下的量子通信与编码尺寸上/下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究预共享纠缠在量子编码尺寸与量子通信复杂度中的作用，厘清QDRE与QPSM之间的对应关系，并刻画不同量子信道在资源受限下的复杂度界限。

Method: 建立QDRE模型与QPSM模型（经典通信免费、量子通信为主要复杂度度量）之间的双向连接，据此将QPSM协议的上/下界转化为QDRE编码尺寸的对应界，并分析一般信道、常量/经典输出信道、单量子比特输出信道及Clifford诱导信道。

Result: (1) 对任意n到m量子比特的量子信道，给出量子通信为m的QPSM协议，使用指数级预共享纠缠；常量输出信道仅需O(1)量子通信，经典输出信道无需量子通信。(2) 在O(n)预共享纠缠下，每个单量子比特输出信道存在O(n)量子通信的QPSM协议；反之存在某信道与常数c>0，使任何预共享纠缠不超过cn的协议需Ω(n)量子通信。(3) Clifford诱导信道存在使用O(n)预共享纠缠量子比特且仅O(1)量子通信复杂度的QPSM协议。经上述连接可得QDRE量子编码尺寸的类似上/下界。

Conclusion: 预共享纠缠量在量子编码尺寸与量子通信复杂度中起核心作用；QDRE与QPSM之间存在可迁移的复杂度对应，Clifford诱导信道可在有限纠缠下实现常数量子通信。

Abstract: We study the complexity of quantum decomposable randomized encodings (QDRE). We establish a bi-directional connection between the QDRE model and the communication complexity model of quantum private simultaneous message protocols (QPSM), where classical communication is free, and quantum communication is the primary complexity measure. We show the following upper and lower bounds. (1) For every quantum channel mapping $n$ to $m$ qubits, we give a QPSM protocol with quantum communication $m$, using exponential pre-shared entanglement. In particular, constant-output channels have $O(1)$ quantum communication, and classical-output channels require no quantum communication. (2) With $O(n)$ pre-shared entanglement, every one-qubit-output channel has an $O(n)$-quantum-communication QPSM protocol. Conversely, there exists a channel and a constant $c>0$ for which any protocol with at most $cn$ pre-shared entanglement requires $Ω(n)$ quantum communication. (3) In contrast, we identify a structured class of quantum channels, Clifford-induced channels, for which there exist QPSM protocols with $O(n)$ pre-shared entangled qubits and only $O(1)$ quantum communication complexity. Through our connection, we obtain analogous upper and lower bounds on the quantum encoding size of QDRE.
