Our results show that the amount of pre-shared entanglement plays a central role in quantum encoding size and quantum communication complexity.

</details>


### [150] [Efficiency-Resolved Recovery Dynamics of an Free-Running InGaAs/InP Single Photon Avalanche Detector Operated in Gated Mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17694)
*Bruno Santos Souza,Vitor Tavares,Filippo Ghiglieno,Celso Jorge Villas-Boas,Paulo Henrique Dias Ferreira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过扫描门控频率（100 kHz–1 MHz）并全局拟合四种死时间下的计数率，提取了自由运行 InGaAs/InP 单光子探测器在 1550 nm 的门控恢复时间常数，无需脉冲激光器或时间数字转换器。恢复时间随探测效率线性下降，10%–25% 之间从 301 ns 降至 161 ns（9.3 ns/百分点），并观测到与偏压相关电容门馈通一致的周期性计数率调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 门控模式单光子雪崩探测器的探测效率在每个门边缘后逐渐恢复，限制了可达计数率，但定量恢复模型此前仅适用于自由运行模式，缺乏门控条件下的恢复时间定量表征手段。

Method: 对 1550 nm 自由运行 InGaAs/InP 探测器，将门频率从 100 kHz 扫至 1 MHz，并在四种死时间下对计数率进行全局拟合，提取门控恢复时间常数；方法无需脉冲激光器和时间数字转换器。

Result: 恢复时间随探测效率线性下降，10% 至 25% 间由 301 ns 减至 161 ns，斜率为 9.3 ns/百分点；同时分辨出与偏压相关电容门馈通相符的周期性计数率调制。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了门控模式下的恢复时间定量提取方法，并揭示了恢复时间与探测效率的线性关系及门馈通引起的计数率调制，为门控单光子探测器计数率优化提供依据。

Abstract: Detection efficiency in gated-mode single-photon avalanche detectors recovers gradually after each gate edge, limiting attainable count rates, yet quantitative recovery models exist only for free-running operation. We extract the gated recovery time constant of a free-running InGaAs/InP detector at 1550 nm by sweeping the gate frequency from 100 kHz to 1 MHz and globally fitting count rates across four dead times, requiring neither a pulsed laser nor a time-to-digital converter. The recovery time decreases linearly with detection efficiency, from 301 to 161 ns between 10 % and 25 %, at 9.3 ns per percentage point. We further resolve a periodic count-rate modulation consistent with bias-dependent capacitive gate feedthrough.

</details>


### [151] [Advances in the quantum-state texture theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17704)
*Jose Alfredo de Leon,Miguel Gonzalez,Alejandro Fonseca,Pedro C. Azado,Fernando Parisio*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文围绕量子态纹理(Quantum-State Texture, QST)的资源理论展开，以实验可访问的"总合"(grand sum，即密度算符在给定基下所有矩阵元之和)为核心，澄清并拓展了QST的资源理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QST概念已被应用于未知量子门识别、量子相变与临界性研究，并已有实验验证，但其资源理论尚需进一步规范化与完善；本文旨在推进其资源理论表述，澄清近期文献中的若干争议点。

Method: 以密度算符在给定基下所有矩阵元之和(总合)为中心的可访问量，研究QST单调量与纹理蒸馏的关系；对单比特完全刻画自由操作(free operations)并研究相应的态转换序；对任意维度分类相关自由操作族；推导复合系统与其子系统间的纹理界限；提出基无关的集合纹理(set texture)概念。

Result: 澄清了纹理蒸馏与一个基于总合的简单QST单调量之间的关系；完全刻画了单比特的自由操作并给出态转换序；分类了任意维度下的相关自由操作族；得到复合系统与子系统纹理之间的界限；给出基无关的集合纹理概念。

Conclusion: 上述结果强化并拓宽了QST的资源理论，为QST资源理论的进一步发展和应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: The recently introduced concept of quantum-state texture (QST) has found applications ranging from the identification of unknown quantum gates to the study of quantum phase transitions and criticality, and has already been experimentally investigated. Here, we advance its resource-theoretic formulation in several directions. Our approach centers on the experimentally accessible grand sum--the sum of all matrix elements of a density operator in a given basis. We clarify several points raised in the recent literature, including the relation between texture distillation and a simple grand-sum-based QST monotone. We completely characterize free operations for a single qubit and study the resulting state-conversion order; classify relevant families of free operations in arbitrary dimensions; derive bounds relating the texture of composite systems to that of their subsystems; and introduce the basis-independent concept of set texture. Together, these results strengthen and broaden the resource theory of QST, providing a foundation for its further development and applications.

</details>


### [152] [Rényi and Tsallis information entropies for a harmonic position-dependent mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17941)
*Dorcas A. Addo,Daniel Sabi-Takou,Eugene Adjei,Latévi M. Lawson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究位置相关质量谐振子系统在Gegenbauer多项式与第二类修正贝塞尔函数表示下的Renyi与Tsallis信息熵，通过渐近极限克服位置空间积分困难，数值验证熵不确定关系并发现α→1时趋于Shannon熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 位置相关质量哈密顿系统在谐振子势中的量子信息熵（Renyi、Tsallis）因涉及特殊函数积分而计算困难，需要寻找可行求解途径并与已有模型结果对照，同时验证熵不确定关系。

Method: 用Gegenbauer多项式求位置本征函数，用第二类修正贝塞尔函数确定动量本征函数；基于两种表示的几率密度解析与数值计算类Heisenberg不确定度；位置表示的Renyi与Tsallis熵作为Gegenbauer多项式的积分泛函难以处理，故在系统渐近极限（对应未变形谐振子行为）下求解，而动量表示则不使用近似；最后数值评估熵不确定关系。

Result: 两种表示下，Renyi与Tsallis熵在熵参数α→1时趋于Shannon熵，且与文献中类似模型结果接近；类Heisenberg不确定度被解析和数值确定；通过数值熵不确定关系验证了上述结论。

Conclusion: 对位置相关质量谐振子系统，采用渐近极限可有效获得位置表示的信息熵，动量表示可精确求解；结果与已有模型一致，且熵不确定关系得到数值支持，α→1极限下熵值收敛于Shannon熵。

Abstract: In this paper, we study Renyi and Tsallis information entropies for a Hamiltonian system with position-dependent mass confined in harmonic oscillator potential. Gegenbauer polynomials are used to obtain the position eigenfunction of such a system, and the modified Bessel function of the second kind is used to determine the equivalent momentum eigenfunction. By means of probability densities of both representations, we analytically and numerically evaluate the Heisenberg-like uncertainty of this system. Because the Renyi and Tsallis information entropies in position representation are described by integral functionals of the Gegenbauer polynomials, these quantities are much more difficult to calculate. To get around this problem, we evaluate these information entropies at the system s asymptotical limit, which corresponds to the behavior of an undeformed harmonic oscillator. Nevertheless, no approximation method is used to obtain the Tsallis and Renyi information entropies in momentum representation. In both representations, we find that these information entropies approach the Shannon entropy when the entropic parameter alpha-> 1 and are closed to the results of similar models of the literature. Finally, we evaluate the related entropic uncertainty relations numerically to validate the latter obersevations.

</details>


### [153] [Diagnosing and Restoring the Degraded Fault Distance of Magic State Cultivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17706)
*Tim Chan,Armands Strikis,Zhu Sun,Zhenyu Cai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了T态培养与S态培养之间的逻辑错误率差异（T-S discrepancy），揭示了T态培养中Pauli钩错误经双检查电路后传播为相干Clifford错误，导致故障距离降低，并设计了flag电路加以修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T态培养是资源高效的逻辑T态制备方案，但近期基准测试显示其逻辑错误率远高于预期，且显著高于类似的S态培养方案。论文旨在解释这一T-S差异的根源。

Method: 在电路级去极化噪声模型下进行解析分析，证明距离3（5）的T态培养实际故障距离仅为2（3），原因是Pauli钩错误在最终双检查电路后传播为相干Clifford错误；推导了逻辑混合态在Clifford错误影响下接受概率的通用公式及固定逻辑比特数下的O(n^3)时间算法；并设计flag电路检测恶性钩错误。

Result: T态培养中距离3（5）的故障距离实为2（3），而S态培养保持故障距离3（5）。所设计的flag方案将逃逸前逻辑错误率从O(p^3)提升至O(p^5)，在p=10^-3时改善4.9倍，代价是每次接受尝试次数增加1.36倍。

Conclusion: T-S差异源于钩错误在T态培养中转化为相干Clifford错误从而降低故障距离；通过flag电路可有效检测并抑制此类错误，以较小开销显著提升逻辑错误率。

Abstract: T-state cultivation is a resource-efficient protocol producing logical T states but recent benchmarks show that its logical error rate is considerably higher than intended i.e. than S-state cultivation, which is the analogous protocol for producing logical S states. In this paper, we explain this T-S discrepancy by analytically showing, under circuit-level depolarising noise, that distance-3 (-5) T-state cultivation has fault distance 2 (3) due to Pauli hook errors that propagate to coherent Clifford errors after its final double-check circuit; such errors remain Pauli in S-state cultivation, which consequently retains fault distance 3 (5). As part of our analysis we derive a general formula, and an $\mathcal O(n^3)$-time algorithm for fixed logical-qubit count, for the acceptance probability of a logical mixed state afflicted with a Clifford error, where $n$ is the physical qubit count. We then design flags that detect the malignant hook errors in cultivation, improving the pre-escape logical error rate from $\mathcal O(p^3)$ to $\mathcal O(p^5)$. At noise level $p =10^{-3}$, this is a 4.9$\times$ improvement, costing a 1.36$\times$ increase in attempts per accepted shot.

</details>


### [154] [Exact damping-basis continued fractions for few-atom lasers: collective correlations beyond second-order cumulant closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17785)
*Stephan Hartmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将损耗腔模的Briegel–Englert阻尼基与N个全同非相干泵浦二能级发射体的置换不变Liouville基结合，构造出在单一径向场指标上精确块三对角的Liouvillian（原子块维数C(N+3,3)）；通过矩阵连分数求解稳态（证明在耗散区A>0唯一可解），并在相干一扇区得到发射谱。方法经直接对角化、弱泵浦微扰论和闭系统谱验证，少量子区N=16可在数十秒内求解，大光子数下有量化精度极限。应用给出精确双原子光子平衡恒等式、零温腔连接关联的泵浦阶层级与最小集体自旋扇区宇称性质，并基准检验少发射体激光理论中的二阶累积量闭合（光子数半定量，反转以上互发射体相干符号错误）。好腔区二、三发射体呈近泊松统计和强线窄化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少发射体激光与腔量子电动力学中，N个全同非相干泵浦二能级发射体与损耗腔模耦合的问题维度随N快速增长，传统方法难以在中等N下精确求得稳态与发射谱；同时少发射体激光理论常用的二阶累积量闭合缺少严格检验。作者希望构建一个能高效、精确处理中等N的谱方法，并借此推导严格解析关系、检验近似理论的适用范围。

Method: 将腔模的Briegel–Englert阻尼基（对损耗腔模Liouvillian对角化）与N个全同非相干泵浦二能级发射体的置换不变Liouville基结合；所得Liouvillian在单一径向场指标上呈精确块三对角结构，原子块维数为C(N+3,3)。稳态由矩阵连分数给出，其渐近闭合为线性矩阵方程，证明在耗散区A>0唯一可解；在相干一扇区用同一构造得到发射谱。方法通过与直接对角化、弱泵浦微扰论、闭系统谱对比进行验证。

Result: 方法在少量子区对N=16可在数十秒内求解，大光子数下存在量化精度极限。应用得到：精确的双原子光子平衡恒等式，将腔诱导的配对相干与单重态–三重态布居差联系起来；零温腔下连接关联的精确泵浦阶层级及最小集体自旋扇区的宇称性质；对少发射体激光理论的二阶累积量闭合进行基准检验，发现其对光子数半定量，但在反转以上预测的互发射体相干符号错误。好腔区精确解显示二、三发射体呈近泊松统计和强线窄化。

Conclusion: 该阻尼基–置换不变Liouville基的块三对角/矩阵连分数框架为少发射体腔QED与激光问题提供了高效且可定量控制精度的精确求解途径，并可导出严格关系（光子平衡恒等式、泵浦阶层级、宇称性质），同时揭示二阶累积量闭合在互发射体相干符号上的失效及好腔区近泊松统计与线窄化特征。

Abstract: We combine the Briegel--Englert damping basis of a lossy cavity mode with the permutation-invariant Liouville basis of \(N\) identical, incoherently pumped two-level emitters. The resulting Liouvillian is exactly block tridiagonal in a single radial field index, with atomic blocks of dimension \(\binom{N+3}{3}\). The stationary state follows from a matrix continued fraction whose asymptotic closure is a linear matrix equation that we prove to be uniquely solvable throughout the dissipative regime \(A>0\), and the same construction in the coherence-one sector yields the emission spectrum. The method is validated against direct diagonalization, weak-pump perturbation theory, and closed-system spectra, reaches \(N=16\) in tens of seconds in the few-quanta regime, and has a quantified precision limit at large photon number. We use it to derive an exact two-atom photon-balance identity that isolates the cavity-induced pair coherence and relates it to a singlet--triplet population difference; to establish an exact pump-order hierarchy of connected correlations for a zero-temperature cavity and a parity property of the minimal collective-spin sector; and to benchmark the second-order cumulant closure used in few-emitter laser theory, which we find to be semi-quantitative for the photon number but to predict the wrong sign of the inter-emitter coherence above inversion. In a good-cavity regime the exact solution shows near-Poissonian statistics and strong line narrowing for two and three emitters.

</details>


### [155] [Superconducting levitation and control of a high-reflectivity micromirror](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17787)
*Philipp Koller,Jannek J. Hansen,David Walcher,Tomáš Plichta,Rémi Claessen,Martin Žemlička,Philip Schmidt,Rhys G. Povey,Stefan Minniberger,Stefan Putz,Markus Aspelmeyer,Michael Trupke*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用超导磁悬浮悬挂总质量约30微克光学微镜的方法，并实现低温下的稳定悬浮与高灵敏度位移测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现自由悬挂、低耗散且振荡频率可调的光学微镜，为微克量级的精密传感与量子腔光力学提供平台。

Method: 在硅膜上制备微镜，镀高反射介质叠层并连接超导微球；在低温下用磁四极场稳定悬浮，对横向运动施加磁反馈以稳定位置，并用光学干涉测量轴向位移。

Result: 悬浮体在轴向捕获频率167 Hz附近达到约100 pm/√Hz的灵敏度。

Conclusion: 该方案可实现低耗散、频率可调的自由悬挂微镜，适用于微克量级精密传感与量子腔光力学研究。

Abstract: We introduce a method to suspend an optical micromirror, with a total mass of \SI{30}{\micro\gram}, using superconducting magnetic levitation. The micromirror is formed on a silicon membrane, coated with a high-reflectivity dielectric stack and attached to superconducting microspheres. The object is stably levitated inside a magnetic quadrupole field at cryogenic temperatures. Magnetic feedback on the transverse motion is used to stabilize the position of the levitator within the trapping field, allowing to measure the axial displacement of the levitator using optical interferometry. The system reaches a sensitivity of order \SI{100}{\pico\metre/\sqrt{\hertz}} near the axial trap frequency of \SI{167}{Hz}. This approach enables free-standing mirrors with minimal dissipation and tunable oscillation frequencies, offering a platform for precision sensing and quantum cavity optomechanics in the microgram regime.

</details>


### [156] [Measuring correlations in quantum and statistical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18412)
*V. I. Yukalov,E. P. Yukalova*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 综述讨论复合量子与统计系统中关联强度的度量问题，重点介绍关联指数方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合量子与统计系统中关联度量缺乏统一、普适的方法，特别是需要同时适用于量子与经典关联、静态与动态过程。

Method: 采用关联指数方法，针对关联算符、约化密度矩阵及含关联信息的其他算符定义关联指数，用以度量纠缠等量子关联和经典关联。

Result: 给出了多种量子态以及自旋与赝自旋系统的关联指数示例；通过计算受交变场作用的囚禁Bose-Einstein凝聚体的关联指数，展示了非平衡系统关联的量化。

Conclusion: 关联指数方法具有足够的一般性，可唯一地度量量子与统计系统、静态与动态过程中的关联强度。

Abstract: The survey discusses the problem of measuring correlations in composite quantum and statistical systems. After briefly recalling the standard methods of describing correlations, the emphasis is on the method of correlation indices. The latter method for quantifying the strength of correlations in composite physical systems is sufficiently general allowing for measuring in a unique way the correlation strength in both quantum and statistical systems, for static as well as for dynamic processes. The correlation indices, can be defined for correlation operators, reduced density matrices, and other operators containing information on correlations in the studied composite systems. The correlation indices measure all types of correlations, quantum correlations, such as entanglement, as well as classical correlations. They are applicable for characterizing static as well as dynamical processes. Examples are given of correlation indices for several quantum states and for spin and pseudospin systems. The quantification of correlations in a nonequilibrium system is exemplified by calculating the correlation index for a trapped Bose-Einstein condensate subject to the action of an alternating field.

</details>


### [157] [QiT: Quantum-Inspired Transformer for Visual Recognition Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17789)
*Badri N. Patro,Vijay Agneeswaran*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出QiT——一种将量子模型的结构性思想(角度编码、周期性相似度核、门控乘法交互)实现为可扩展经典Transformer操作的视觉骨干网络，在标准ViT复杂度下取得有竞争力的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子机器学习在表示层面颇具吸引力：角度编码态天然处于表达周期相似性与相互作用的希尔伯特空间中。但将其用于视觉识别仍面临量子比特数受限、线路模拟与测量开销高、噪声及NISQ设备上优化不稳定等困难。本文转而探究能否把量子模型中有用的结构思想迁移为可扩展的经典Transformer算子。

Method: QiT由三个组件构成：(i)角度启发编码，将图像token映射为可学习的三角函数希尔伯特空间特征，类比量子旋转态编码；(ii)在周期特征上做自注意力，诱导出近似量子保真度核的经典余弦核；(iii)门控乘法仿真，作为变分电路中交互项的可训练经典替代。全部组件均为可微张量运算，不声称量子计算或量子加速，并保持标准ViT的O(N²D)注意力复杂度。

Result: 在多个图像分类基准上，QiT与参数匹配的经典Transformer性能相当，同时避免了小型模拟量子Transformer所观察到的严重运行时间开销。QiT-B以45.7M参数和11.5 GFLOPs达到78.3%的ImageNet-1K top-1准确率。

Conclusion: QiT可作为可扩展的基线，用于隔离并评估视觉识别中受量子启发的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning offers a compelling representational perspective: angle-encoded states inhabit Hilbert spaces in which periodic similarities and interactions can be expressed naturally. Realizing this perspective for visual recognition remains difficult, however, because present quantum neural networks are constrained by limited qubit counts, costly circuit simulation and measurement, noise, and unstable optimization on noisy intermediate-scale quantum devices. We investigate whether useful structural ideas from quantum models can instead be realized as scalable classical Transformer operations. We introduce QiT, a Quantum-inspired Transformer for vision tasks with three components: (i) angle-inspired encoding that maps image tokens to learned trigonometric Hilbert-space features analogous to quantum rotation-based state encoding; (ii) self-attention over these periodic features, inducing a classical cosine kernel approximated to quantum fidelity kernels; and (iii) gated multiplicative emulation, a trainable classical surrogate for interaction terms found in variational circuits. All components are differentiable tensor operations, so QiT claims neither quantum computation nor quantum speedup and retains the $\mathcal{O}(N^2D)$ attention complexity of a standard Vision Transformer. Across image-classification benchmarks, QiT is competitive with a matched classical Transformer while avoiding the severe runtime cost observed for a small simulated quantum Transformer. QiT-B reaches 78.3\% ImageNet-1K top-1 accuracy with 45.7M parameters and 11.5 GFLOPs. These results position QiT as a scalable baseline for isolating and evaluating quantum-motivated inductive biases in visual recognition.

</details>


### [158] [Lindbladian quantum chaos beyond classical strange attractors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18464)
*Ángel L. Corps,Armando Relaño*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了两个耦合集体自旋的耗散系统的经典与量子混沌联系，发现经典稳定不动点吸引子存在时，瞬态动力学仍表现出混沌特征，并对应Liouvillian谱的随机矩阵统计转变，修正了Grobe-Haake-Sommers猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为量子混沌需对应经典系统的混沌渐近吸引子，本文质疑该假设，探究在经典仅有稳定不动点吸引子时是否仍存在量子混沌特征，从而厘清经典混沌与量子混沌（Liouvillian框架下）的对应关系。

Method: 以两个耦合集体自旋的耗散系统为模型，分析经典动力学（不动点吸引子与分岔）及量子Liouvillian谱统计，考察瞬态动力学中的混沌特征、谱的随机矩阵统计转变以及参与物理量期望值的Liouvillian模式数目变化。

Result: 尽管经典动力学只有稳定不动点吸引子，弛豫前的瞬态仍表现出显著的混沌特征；量子模型中瞬态混沌伴随Liouvillian谱相关部分向随机矩阵统计的转变，且贡献于可观测期望值的Liouvillian模式数目定性增加；该转变发生在稳定不动点消失、混沌吸引子出现之前的分岔之前。

Conclusion: Liouvillian量子混沌可与瞬态经典混沌相关联，而不必要求存在混沌渐近吸引子，这是对Grobe-Haake-Sommers猜想的精化。

Abstract: We investigate the connection between classical and quantum chaos in a dissipative system of two coupled collective spins. Although the classical dynamics possesses a stable fixed-point attractor, we find pronounced signatures of chaos in the transient dynamics preceding relaxation. In the quantum model, such transient chaos is accompanied by a transition to random-matrix statistics in the relevant part of the Liouvillian spectrum and by a qualitative increase in the number of Liouvillian modes contributing to expectation values of physical observables. The transition occurs before the bifurcation at which the stable fixed point disappears and a chaotic attractor emerges. Our results show that Liouvillian quantum chaos can be associated with transient classical chaos, rather than requiring a chaotic asymptotic attractor, and provide a refinement of the Grobe-Haake-Sommers conjecture.

</details>


### [159] [Depth analysis of the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm with a Grover mixer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17835)
*Bojko N. Bakalov,Dimitar Grantcharov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析带Grover混合器的量子近似优化算法（QAOA），在独立采样的代价与混合角下，证明损失方差及末次混合角导数的深度无关下界，并给出Grover型可达性界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Grover混合器QAOA中初始态承载代价满足格点条件时的优化景观性质，旨在为算法的可训练性与可达性提供理论刻画。

Method: 采用带Grover混合器、独立采样代价角与混合角的QAOA框架；对初始态所承载代价值施加格点条件，分析损失方差及对末次混合角导数的下界，并建立Grover型可达性界。

Result: 证明对任意深度损失方差存在深度无关下界，且对末次混合角导数具有相同下界；该估计依赖实例，固定局域性时对局部项一致有界的整数值局部目标函数（如MaxCut）为量子比特数的逆多项式；可达性界表明近似指定承载本征空间所需深度下界为初始概率平方根倒数的常数倍。

Conclusion: Grover混合器QAOA在格点条件下具有深度无关的方差与梯度下界，为MaxCut等问题的优化景观与收敛深度提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We study the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm with a Grover mixer and independently sampled cost and mixing angles. Under a lattice condition on the cost values carried by the initial state, we prove a depth-independent lower bound for the variance of the loss at every depth, together with the same bound for the derivative with respect to the final mixing angle. The estimate is instance-dependent and, for fixed locality, is inverse polynomial in the number of qubits for integer-valued local objective functions with uniformly bounded local terms, in particular for MaxCut. We also establish Grover-type reachability bounds showing that the depth required to approximate a prescribed carried eigenspace is bounded below by a constant multiple of the inverse square root of its initial probability.

</details>


### [160] [Quantifying and Bounding Spatiotemporal Correlations in Quantum Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17870)
*Guilherme Zambon,Diogo O. Soares-Pinto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过过程张量建立统一的操作性框架，量化一般量子过程中的时间、空间及总时空关联，并给出层级/插值关系、维度相关的上界及记忆维度见证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息处理中常用的局域马尔可夫模型难以刻画量子噪声的时空关联，需要统一的、操作性的关联量化框架及其可检验界。

Method: 基于过程张量，用与马尔可夫、空间局域、完全无关联过程的最优可区分度定义时间/空间/时空关联；在容许探测comb上优化以保证对保持相应自由集的超过程的单调性，并用Choi态泛函与受限探测给出可获取下界。

Result: 建立关联测度间的层级与插值关系；导出量子记忆传递的时间关联的维度相关上界（经典记忆更紧）及系统维度导致的普适上限；将认证下界转化为最小记忆维度、记忆维度约束下的非经典性、以及过程模型不一致性的见证；在含ZZ与XY相互作用的超导比特模型中演示对经典记忆、量子记忆与系统维度上限的饱和。

Conclusion: 该框架为刻画与处理量子器件中的时空关联误差提供了可操作的途径。

Abstract: Spatial and temporal correlations in quantum noise challenge the local Markovian models commonly used in quantum information processing. We develop a unified operational framework for quantifying temporal, spatial, and total spatiotemporal correlations in general quantum processes. Using process tensors, we define these quantities through optimal distinguishability from Markovian, spatially local, and fully uncorrelated processes. Optimization over admissible probing combs ensures monotonicity under every superprocess that preserves the corresponding free set, while Choi-state functionals and restricted probes provide accessible lower bounds. We establish hierarchy and interpolation relations among the correlation measures and derive dimension-dependent upper bounds on temporal correlations transmitted by quantum memories, with tighter bounds for classical memory, together with universal ceilings imposed by the system dimension. These bounds turn certified lower estimates into witnesses of minimum memory dimension, nonclassicality under a memory-dimension constraint, and inconsistencies in the assumed process model. We illustrate the results with effective superconducting-qubit models featuring \(ZZ\) and \(XY\) interactions, exhibiting saturation of classical-memory, quantum-memory, and system-dimension ceilings. These results pave the way toward practical approaches to characterizing and addressing spatiotemporally correlated errors in quantum devices.

</details>


### [161] [Universal entanglement embezzlement and divergent nonlocal magic from generic local chaotic quantum evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18691)
*Matias Karjula,Teemu Ojanen,Kim Pöyhönen,Tapio Ala-Nissila,Moein N. Ivaki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 局域幺正演化在达到热化前产生的中间态普遍具备多尺度纠缠谱结构，可实现通用纠缠窃取（embezzlement），并伴随随系统规模发散的非局域非稳定化性（nonstabilizerness/magic）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究纠缠窃取所需的特殊纠缠谱结构与非局域magic资源能否在自然、无需精细调节的量子动力学过程中自发产生，从而将催化纠缠提取与混沌演化、热化等物理过程联系起来。

Method: 从乘积态出发，施加局域幺正量子演化，考察其中间阶段的纠缠谱结构；分析其是否满足通用纠缠窃取所需的多尺度层次；并计算相应非局域非稳定化性的标度行为，与"通用窃取等价于发散非局域magic"的既有结论对照。

Result: 中间态普遍展现出通用纠缠窃取所需的多尺度纠缠谱结构，且出现在热化抹平谱层次的最大熵态之前；利用此类态可从催化多体态中提取纠缠而催化态渐近不变；同时伴随随系统尺寸发散的非局域非稳定化性，与理论等价性一致。

Conclusion: 混沌量子演化无需任何精细调节即可生成构成通用催化储库家族的中间态，揭示了纠缠窃取、非局域magic与热化前动力学之间的内在联系。

Abstract: We show that, starting from a product state, local unitary quantum evolutions generate intermediate states which exhibit a multiscale entanglement-spectrum structure required for universal entanglement embezzlement. This facilitates entanglement extraction from a catalyst many-body state while leaving it asymptotically unchanged. Remarkably, these atypical structures emerge generically at intermediate stages, well before reaching maximum entropy where thermalization has flattened out the spectral hierarchy. The resulting state is accompanied by nonlocal nonstabilizerness that diverges with the system size, consistent with a recently established equivalence between universal embezzlement and divergent nonlocal magic. Thus, without any fine tuning, a chaotic quantum evolution generates intermediate states which form a universal family of catalytic reservoirs.

</details>


### [162] [QEMScore: How Much Does the Measurement Add to Learned Quantum Error Mitigation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17896)
*Yue Zhao,Huayue Gu,Yushun Dong,Xiyang Hu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 QEMScore 评测框架，通过“容量匹配但不读测量”的对照模型与测量开销记账，检验学习型量子纠错缓解器的增益是否真正源于测量信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭精度表无法判断学习型量子误差缓解（QEM）模型的增益是否来自测量数据：给定电路结构的模型即使完全不读测量也能取得高分。因此需要一种能区分“测量贡献”与“模型容量/电路描述先验”的评测方法。

Method: QEMScore：每个模拟电路带有精确理想答案；学习型缓解器与一个容量匹配的对照模型同台评分，对照模型读取相同电路描述但从不读取测量；对每种方法的测量开销进行记账而非强制均等。在模拟电路上开展受控实验，并基于公开硬件数据重新分析两个已发表的学习型缓解器 Q-LEAR 与 QRAFT。

Result: 在 S0（同族内）条件下，跨两个自旋链族与三个随机种子：连续耦合参数即可识别目标；不读测量的对照模型达到缓解器相对于仿射基线增益的 87.7%–100.5%；事后拟合的耦合参数多项式在全部六次评测中均优于缓解器。六次评测中有五次，缓解器相较容量匹配对照额外去除 19.5%–74.5% 的误差，属学习器特异的差距而非测量需求。在描述符仅部分识别查询的公开硬件数据上结论不同：Q-LEAR 中描述符的柔性模型相对仿射拟合平均增益可忽略，而 Q-LEAR 与 QRAFT 中测量输入均带来预测增益。

Conclusion: 上述差异反映的是表征与协议特异的行为，而非单一的跨域差别；学习型缓解器的精度应与此类对照模型一并报告。

Abstract: How much does the noisy measurement add to learned quantum error mitigation? An accuracy table cannot say, because a model handed circuit structure can score well without reading the measurement at all. QEMScore adds the comparison that can. Each simulated circuit carries an exact ideal answer. The learned mitigator is scored beside a capacity-matched control, a model just as flexible that reads the same circuit description but never the measurement. Each method's measurement spend is accounted and not equalized. We run a controlled campaign on simulated circuits and reanalyze two published learned mitigators, Q-LEAR and QRAFT, from their released hardware data. Three findings stand out. First, under familiar within-family conditions (S0) evaluated across two spin-chain families and three seeds, continuous couplings identify the target, and the control that never reads the measurement matches 87.7 to 100.5 percent of the mitigator's gain over an affine fit to the circuit description. A plain polynomial in the coupling parameters, fitted after the campaign, beats the mitigator on all six evaluations, reflecting the selected learners' capacity. Second, for these selected learners, matching most of the gain is not matching the accuracy: on five of six evaluations the mitigator removes 19.5 to 74.5 percent of the error the capacity-matched control leaves, a learner-specific gap rather than a measurement requirement. Third, on released hardware data where descriptors only partially identify queries, the findings differ: flexible models of the descriptors show negligible mean gain over affine fits in Q-LEAR, and measurement inputs carry predictive gains in both Q-LEAR and QRAFT. These comparisons reflect representation- and protocol-specific behavior rather than an isolated cross-regime difference. A learned mitigator's accuracy should therefore be reported beside such controls.

</details>


### [163] [Thermal entanglement on a frustrated tetrahedron: Probing quantum resources through concurrence and stabilizer structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18721)
*Reza Pourkhodabakhshi,Francis Dominie,Deep Gajera,Stephanie H. Curnoe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文以四面体（烧绿石晶格的基本单元）上四个自旋-1/2为对象，在四参数交换哈密顿量下研究热纠缠与可分离性的区分：利用四面体对称性将热态分解限制为线性优化问题，给出多体 concurrence 上界及可分离分解，并刻画低温/升温下可分离区域的演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阻挫自旋系统中高度纠缠的本征态可形成可分离的热混合态，因此仅凭本征态纠缠无法判断有限温度下是否存在真正的热纠缠；需要一套可操作的方法来区分热纠缠与可分离热态，并理解其与对称性多重态劈裂和占据的关系。

Method: 针对四面体上四个自旋-1/2的一般四参数交换哈密顿量，利用四面体对称性把热态分解的搜索约化为线性优化，从而得到多体 concurrence 的上界以及使该上界为零的显式可分离分解；辅以 negativity 独立验证有限温度纠缠，并使用混合态 stabilizer Rényi 诊断探索 quantum magic。

Result: 低温 concurrence 图显示：在 all-in–all-out 极限附近存在扩展的可分离区域，而 spin-ice 点附近的纠缠抑制仅狭窄局域；升温时邻近多重态被重新占据使该区域变宽。优化可在本征态平均 concurrence 仍较大时找到完全可分离热态；negativity 在部分耦合区独立确认有限温度纠缠；stabilizer Rényi 诊断偏好不同耦合，并可随温度局部升高。

Conclusion: 热纠缠与对称性多重态的劈裂及热占据密切相关，该线性优化框架为在超越基态极限的量子资源研究中识别合适阻挫自旋构型提供了实用基础。

Abstract: In a frustrated spin system, highly entangled eigenstates can form a separable thermal mixture. We study this distinction for four spin-$1/2$ moments on a tetrahedron, the elementary unit of the pyrochlore lattice, with the general four-parameter exchange Hamiltonian. Tetrahedral symmetry allows a restricted search over thermal-state decompositions to be carried out by linear optimization, yielding an upper bound on multipartite concurrence and explicit separable decompositions where this bound vanishes. At low temperature, the concurrence maps show extended separable regions near the all-in--all-out limit, whereas suppression near the spin-ice point is narrowly localized. Heating broadens the latter region as nearby multiplets are repopulated. The optimization identifies fully separable thermal states even when the eigenstate-averaged concurrence remains large. Negativity independently confirms finite-temperature entanglement in selected coupling regions. A mixed-state stabilizer Rényi diagnostic, used to explore quantum magic, favours different couplings and can increase locally with temperature. These results connect thermal entanglement to the splitting and population of symmetry multiplets, and provide a practical basis for identifying frustrated spin configurations for studies of quantum resources beyond the ground-state limit.

</details>


### [164] [Classical, quantum, and general probabilistic state-discrimination profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17920)
*Mihály Weiner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文引入"判别轮廓"F(H)（最小未归一化判别误差）来刻画概率理论中的可分辨性，精确刻画了通用概率理论(GPT)中可实现的轮廓。对n=3显式给出经典与GPT多胞形，建立β参数层级并证明量子界β_Q≤2/3；对任意n证明经典"面"的GPT违反必可在≤3维量子实现，并给出四态量子非子模及先验依赖反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望用一个统一的、可操作的对象（判别轮廓）来比较经典、量子与GPT三类概率理论的信息处理能力，模仿Bell理论中local–quantum–no-signalling层级，从而在"判别"而非"关联"的框架下建立类似的层级结构，并探究量子集合在其中的确切位置。

Method: 定义判别轮廓F(H)为子族H上的最优未归一化最小判别误差；证明固定n时GPT区域G_n是有界有理多胞形，得到C_n⊆Q_n⊆G_n层级。对n=3显式求解经典与GPT多胞形，用参数β=F(123)-F(12)-F(13)-F(23)刻画经典区域；对qubit三元组用trine态计算β_tr并推导维度无关的量子上界β_Q≤2/3；对任意n分析经典面的量子可实现性，并构造四态qubit与四维量子三元组的反例。

Result: n=3时经典区域由β≤0刻画，GPT最大值为1；qubit三元组中trine达到β_tr=3√3/2-2，并证明维度无关界β_Q≤2/3（略有进一步改进），故0=β_C<3√3/2-2≤β_Q≤2/3<1=β_GPT。猜想β_Q=β_tr，并对任意纯态三元组予以证明并给出支持证据。对任意n，任何允许GPT违反的经典面在维度≤3的量子理论中已有违反。四态qubit例子表明n≥4时量子成功轮廓未必子模；四维量子三元组显示等先验下离散轮廓经典、但不平等先验下变为非经典。

Conclusion: 判别轮廓为比较经典、量子与GPT提供了一套多胞形层级框架；量子区域严格介于经典与GPT之间，且β_Q被夹在3√3/2-2与2/3之间。作者猜想β_Q=β_tr并证明纯态情形；同时指出量子轮廓的非子模性与先验依赖性等新现象，为后续精确确定量子边界提供了方向。

Abstract: For a collection of states indexed by $[n]={1,\ldots,n}$, let $F(H)$ denote the optimal unnormalized minimum discrimination error for each nonempty subfamily $H\subseteq[n]$. We call $F$ the discrimination profile and characterize exactly which profiles can arise in a general probabilistic theory (GPT). For fixed $n$, the GPT region $\mathfrak{G}_n$ is a bounded rational polytope, giving a hierarchy $\mathfrak{C}_n\subseteq\mathfrak{Q}_n\subseteq\mathfrak{G}_n$ of classical, quantum, and GPT profiles, analogous to the local--quantum--no-signalling hierarchy in Bell theory.
  For $n=3$ we determine the classical and GPT polytopes explicitly. Within $\mathfrak{G}_3$, the classical region is characterized by $β:=F(123)-F(12)-F(13)-F(23)\le0$, while the GPT maximum is $1$. For qubit triples, the maximal value is attained by the trine, $β_{\mathrm{tr}}=3\sqrt3/2-2$, and we prove the dimension-independent quantum bound $β_{\mathrm Q}\le2/3$, with a slight further improvement. Hence $0=β_{\mathrm C}<3\sqrt3/2-2\leβ_{\mathrm Q}\le2/3<1=β_{\mathrm{GPT}}$. We conjecture $β_{\mathrm Q}=β_{\mathrm{tr}}$ and prove this for arbitrary pure-state triples, together with further supporting evidence.
  For arbitrary $n$, every classical facet admitting a GPT violation already admits a quantum violation in dimension at most $3$. A four-state qubit example shows that quantum success profiles need not be submodular, unlike for $n=3$. Finally, we exhibit a four-dimensional quantum triple whose discrete profile is classical for all subfamilies but becomes nonclassical when unequal priors are allowed.

</details>


### [165] [Liouvillian exceptional points in the emergence of quantum synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18867)
*Carlos Ortega Taberner,Rosalind E. Cotton,Paul R. Eastham*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究耦合激光器/极化激元凝聚体的量子理论中，同步相变由Liouvillian慢模态的例外点级联控制，并受广义PT对称性保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典/半经典理论中，自持振荡器（如激光器、极化激元凝聚体）的锁相/同步转变对应相空间吸引子拓扑的变化，发生在广义例外点处；但该转变在量子理论中的对应描述尚不清晰，需要探究量子Liouvillian如何刻画这些稳态机制。

Method: 构建耦合激光器或凝聚体的量子理论，分析其Liouvillian慢模态的谱与特征矩阵在不同耦合强度下的形式；追踪不同稳态区之间的谱流（spectral flow），识别由广义PT对称性保护的例外点级联。

Result: 经典极限下不同的稳态区对应慢模态谱与特征矩阵的截然不同形式；弱耦合时振荡器频率不同、相位无关联，强耦合时发生同步；同步相变由慢模态中例外点的级联所控制，并受广义PT对称性保护。

Conclusion: 量子Liouvillian的奇点控制着不同的动力学机制，并给出同步相变的实验可观测特征，这些特征在量子效应主导的少粒子区域仍然明确定义。

Abstract: The coupling between self-sustained oscillators, such as lasers or polariton condensates, leads to different steady-states in different regimes. Weak coupling allows the oscillators to behave independently, with different frequencies and uncorrelated phases, while stronger coupling can produce synchronization. In classical or semiclassical theories transitions between these steady-states correspond to changes in the topology of the phase space attractors and occur at generalized exceptional points. We investigate the corresponding changes in a quantum theory of coupled lasers or condensates. We show how the different steady-state regimes of the classical limit give rise to distinct forms for the spectra and eigenmatrices of the slow modes of the Liouvillian. By following the spectral flow between the different regimes we show that the synchronization transition is controlled by cascades of exceptional points in these slow modes, protected by a generalized PT symmetry. Our results show how singularities of the quantum Liouvillian control the different dynamical regimes, and give rise to experimental signatures of the synchronization transition which remain well-defined in the few-particle regime dominated by quantum effects.

</details>


### [166] [The Fourier Uncertainty Principle Through the Lens of Infinite Banded Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17970)
*Cameron L. Williams,Lara C. Ortelli,Gael Silva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过无限带状矩阵，以最小化不确定性乘积的变分方法研究傅里叶不确定性原理，基于移位量子谐振子哈密顿量 H_{a,b} 的谱给出三种不同推导途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统傅里叶不确定性原理的分析严重依赖其背后的 Heisenberg–Weyl 结构，因而难以推广到该框架之外的其他积分变换与共轭算符。作者希望寻找更具普适性的替代方法。

Method: 在 Hermite–Gauss 基下，移位量子谐振子哈密顿量 H_{a,b} 具有无限三对角（带状）形式，成为分析核心。作者提出三种方法：(1) 解析法直接求解其谱；(2) 将 H_{a,b} 分解为无限多个 2×2 矩阵之和；(3) 通过与该量子谐振子的酉等价性，用代数方法确定谱。

Result: 三种方法均成功刻画了 H_{a,b} 的谱，从而给出傅里叶不确定性原理的多种变分推导；这些推导不依赖 Heisenberg–Weyl 结构。

Conclusion: 所提途径为不确定性原理提供了更具推广性的分析框架，可应用于其他保持三对角形式的积分变换及其正则共轭算符。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the Fourier uncertainty principle through infinite banded matrices by way of a variational approach to minimizing the uncertainty product. Traditional methods for analyzing the Fourier uncertainty principle rely heavily on the Heisenberg--Weyl structure that underlie the Fourier transform and therefore do not generalize well beyond this setting. We provide three distinct approaches to developing the uncertainty principle based on the spectrum of a shifted quantum harmonic oscillator Hamiltonian, $H_{a,b}$. In the Hermite--Gauss basis, $H_{a,b}$ has an infinite, tridiagonal form which is central to the analysis. One approach exactly solves for the spectrum through analytic methods, another decomposes $H_{a,b}$ into an infinite sum of $2\times 2$ matrices, and the third approach is an algebraic approach to establishing the spectrum via a unitary equivalence with the quantum harmonic oscillator. The approaches taken herein provide alternate avenues that are more broadly applicable to other canonically conjugate operators for other integral transforms which maintain a tridiagonal form.

</details>


### [167] [A Gauge Sign Rule for Quantum Rotor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18969)
*Swagata Acharya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子转子网络中的符号问题，证明了一个转子版的Marshall符号规则：电荷基哈密顿量无符号当且仅当所有独立回路的阻挫磁通在模$2\pi$下为零，并揭示符号代价是磁通的规范不变函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号问题（非正路径积分权重）使量子系统难以经典模拟，广泛出现于费米子反对称性、几何阻挫、实时演化、拓扑角和有限密度等看似无关的问题中。作者希望针对紧致连续变量（量子转子网络）统一刻画符号问题的根源。

Method: 将系统建模为量子转子网络，哈密顿量为$\hat H=\sum_i 4E_C(\hat n_i-n_{g,i})^2-\sum_{\langle ij\rangle} E_{ij}\cos(\hatφ_i-\hatφ_j-θ_{ij})$；通过规范不变的回路磁通分析阻挫，证明符号规则，并结合精确对角化与密度矩阵重整化（DMRG）数值验证。

Result: 证明单个规范不变量（相互作用图独立回路上的阻挫磁通）控制符号障碍；电荷基哈密顿量无符号当且仅当每个回路磁通模$2\pi$为零。数值显示符号代价是磁通的规范不变函数：单回路时零通量处消失、$\pi$处峰值、随回路周长指数小；多回路求和后呈广延性，随系统尺寸增长。

Conclusion: 同一回路磁通统一决定了三个通常分开处理的问题的符号：Mott转变（经slave-rotor映射）、紧致$U(1)$格点规范理论和阻挫连续优化；超导转子阵列可原生实现该模型及其符号。

Abstract: The sign problem is the non-positive path-integral weight that makes a quantum system hard to simulate classically. It shows up in problems that look unrelated, from fermion antisymmetry and geometric frustration to real-time evolution, topological angles, and finite density. We study it here for compact continuous variables, modeled as networks of quantum rotors, $\hat H=\sum_i 4E_C(\hat n_i-n_{g,i})^2-\sum_{\langle ij\rangle} E_{ij}\cos(\hatφ_i-\hatφ_j-θ_{ij})$. For these, a single gauge-invariant quantity controls the obstruction, the frustration flux through the independent loops of the interaction graph. We prove a sign rule, the rotor analogue of Marshall's. The charge-basis Hamiltonian is sign-free if and only if every loop flux vanishes modulo $2π$. Exact diagonalization and density-matrix renormalization then show that the sign cost is a gauge-invariant function of the flux. For a single loop it vanishes at zero flux, peaks at $π$, and is exponentially small in the loop's perimeter. Summed over many loops it is extensive, growing with system size. The same loop flux generates the sign of three problems usually treated apart, namely the Mott transition (through the slave-rotor mapping), compact $U(1)$ lattice gauge theory, and frustrated continuous optimization. A superconducting rotor array realizes both the model and its sign natively.

</details>


### [168] [Unbounded Holevo additivity gaps in finite dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18222)
*Jinzhao Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在有限维中构造了无界的两用 Holevo 可加性差距：对充分大的固定整数 $K$ 及充分大的 $n$，构造输出维 $K^n$、输入维 $\exp(\Theta_K(n^2))$ 的信道 $T_n$，使得 $\chi(T_n^{\otimes 2})-2\chi(T_n)$ 线性于输出量子比特数，并给出显式的二次输入量子比特代价与阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Holevo 量的（超）可加性刻画了量子信道经典容量是否可由单用 Holevo 量给出。此前已知存在可加性差距，但缺乏有限维中无界、且带显式维度代价的构造，本文旨在给出这类显式例子。

Method: 基于 Collins 自由概率证明中使用的互补混合酉信道的结构化张量积构造信道族 $T_n$；通过结合 Collins–Youn 的乘积群 Haagerup 不等式与 Bordenave–Collins 的定量强收敛估计，建立最小输出熵差距。

Result: 对充分大的固定 $K$ 与充分大的 $n$，得到 $\chi(T_n^{\otimes2})-2\chi(T_n)\ge n[\log_2K/K-2\log_2(1+9/K)]-O(1/n)$；差距线性于输出量子比特数，输入量子比特代价为二次，并给出明确的 $n$ 阈值。此外还构造出单用 Holevo 量趋于零、而两用 Holevo 信息（从而经典容量）发散的渠道。

Conclusion: 结果表明有限维中存在无界的多用量子信道经典容量优势，且可由显式维度规模与定量估计构造，为 Holevo 可加性问题提供了新的显式反例框架。

Abstract: We establish unbounded two-use Holevo additivity gaps in finite dimensions. For each sufficiently large fixed integer $K$ and all sufficiently large $n$, we construct channels $T_n$ with output dimension $K^n$, input dimension $\exp(Θ_K(n^2))$, and \[
  χ(T_n^{\otimes2})-2χ(T_n)
  \ge n\left[\frac{\log_2K}{K}-2\log_2(1+9/K)\right]-O(1/n). \] The gap is linear in output qubits, with an explicit quadratic input-qubit cost and an explicit threshold on $n$. We also obtain channels whose single-use Holevo quantity tends to zero while their two-use Holevo information, and hence classical capacity, diverges. The channels arise from structured tensor products of the complementary mixed-unitary channels used in Collins's free-probabilistic proof. We establish minimum-output-entropy gaps by combining Collins--Youn's product-group Haagerup inequality with Bordenave--Collins's quantitative strong-convergence estimates

</details>


### [169] [Qurrium: A Python package for randomized measurement-based estimation of quantum state properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17979)
*Huai-Chun Chang,Teik-Hui Lee,Arthur Strauss,Yu-Cheng Lin,Hsiu-Chuan Hsu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Qurrium：基于Qiskit的Python包，实现随机测量协议（经典阴影），用于估计纯度、二阶Rényi熵、Pauli可观测量期望值与态重叠，支持端到端与独立估计两种工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态性质估计在量子多体物理研究与量子硬件基准测试等应用中至关重要，需要一个易于使用且与主流量子软件框架兼容的随机测量工具。

Method: 构建基于Qiskit的Python包Qurrium，实现经典阴影协议等随机测量协议；提供端到端工作流（集成线路制备、模拟、测量与分析）和独立估计工作流（接受符合Qurrium数据格式的任意硬件测量数据）。

Result: 以簇态和Ising时间演化态为例演示两种工作流；并使用中心研究院开发的超导量子处理器采集的数据，通过独立估计工作流完成分析。

Conclusion: Qurrium建立在Qiskit之上，便于已有Qiskit研究社区直接使用；源代码与示例代码均已开源。

Abstract: Estimating quantum state properties is essential across a wide range of applications, from studying quantum many-body physics to benchmarking quantum hardware. We present Qurrium, a Python package built on Qiskit that implements randomized measurement protocols for estimating purity, second-order Rényi entropy, expectation values of Pauli observables, and state overlap. In this paper, we focus on the classical shadow protocol and demonstrate two workflows in Qurrium. One is the end-to-end workflow that integrates quantum circuit preparation, simulation, measurement, and analysis. The other is the standalone estimation workflow that accepts pre-collected measurement data from any hardware platform in Qurrium's data format. We demonstrate both workflows through examples of a cluster state and an Ising time-evolved state. Furthermore, we report results obtained from a superconducting quantum processor developed by Academia Sinica and analyzed using the standalone estimation workflow. Qurrium is built on Qiskit, the dominant framework in quantum computing software, making it directly accessible to the large community of researchers already working with Qiskit. The source code is openly available at https://github.com/qurrium/qurrium and the example code is provided at https://github.com/qurrium/classical-shadow-examples.

</details>


### [170] [Fourier Analysis of Parametrized Interactive Quantum Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17991)
*Fábio Novaes,Fernando M. de Paula Neto,João V. M. Cardoso*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了单目标量子比特的参数化交互量子分类器（IQC）所生成约化量子通道的闭式解，揭示了哈密顿量参数如何控制分类器输出的常数、正弦与余弦成分，从而建立傅里叶解释，并据此提出可实现非可分傅里叶结构的广义哈密顿量编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作提出了多种IQC哈密顿量参数化方案并在经验上显示可提升分类性能，但这些参数在分类器中的作用机制尚不清楚，缺乏理论刻画。

Method: 针对单目标量子比特的参数化IQC，推导约化量子通道的解析（闭式）表达式；基于该解析解分析哈密顿量参数对分类器输出常数项、正弦项和余弦项的调控，建立特征映射的傅里叶解释；进而提出广义哈密顿量编码族，包括傅里叶分量依赖输入特征线性组合的矩阵参数化环境哈密顿量，以实现非可分傅里叶结构；并采用基于保真度的可表达性度量刻画所生成的状态集合。

Result: 在合成数据集与真实数据集上的数值实验表明，所提模型在若干非线性基准上可提升分类性能；广义矩阵编码在所评估基准中取得最强综合表现，而更简单的四参数扩展常以显著更少的可训练参数达到相当的性能；全局可表达性并不能直接预测分类性能。

Conclusion: 本文给出了交互量子分类器中参数化哈密顿量的解析刻画，并确立傅里叶分析作为理解与设计开放系统启发式量子学习模型的有效框架。

Abstract: Interactive Quantum Classifiers (IQCs) constitute a family of quantum machine learning models inspired by open quantum systems, in which the interaction between a target qubit and an environment is described by a Hamiltonian. Previous works introduced alternative Hamiltonian parameterizations and showed empirically that they can improve classification performance, but the role of these parameters in the resulting classifier remains poorly understood. In this work, we derive a closed-form expression for the reduced quantum channel generated by a parametrized IQC with a single target qubit. The analytical solution explicitly reveals how the Hamiltonian parameters control the constant, sine, and cosine components of the classifier output, establishing a Fourier interpretation of the induced feature map. This analysis motivates a generalized family of Hamiltonian encodings, including matrix-parameterized environmental Hamiltonians whose Fourier components depend on linear combinations of input features, thereby enabling non-separable Fourier structures. Numerical experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show that the proposed models can improve classification performance on several nonlinear benchmarks. The generalized matrix encoding achieves the strongest aggregate performance in the evaluated benchmark, while a simpler four-parameter extension often attains comparable performance with substantially fewer trainable parameters. We additionally characterize the generated state ensembles using the standard fidelity-based expressibility measure, finding that global expressibility does not directly predict classification performance. Our results provide an analytical characterization of parametrized Hamiltonians in Interactive Quantum Classifiers and establish Fourier analysis as a useful framework for understanding and designing open-system-inspired quantum learning models.

</details>


### [171] [Spectral Optimization for Absolutely PPT States: Purity, Entropy, and Volume Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18568)
*Anh T. Tran*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了绝对正部分转置（APPT）态的最大纯度与最小von Neumann熵，并给出了其谱集的相对体积估计。通过Hildebrand判据得到显式外谱多胞体并分类其顶点，进而导出对所有mn≥6的APPT态纯度上界4/(3mn)，改进了绝对可分态的2/(mn)上界，且在2⊗n系统上取到；对3⊗n系统该界严格并反驳了Dũng–Khôi qutrit–qudit猜想。同时给出任意维度熵下界、2⊗n系统精确最小熵，以及量子比特–qudit相对谱体积a_n的双边估计，得到a_n=Θ(√n(4/27)^n)与指数衰减率ln(27/4)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝对可分态是纠缠理论中的重要类，其纯度与熵的极值刻画以及谱集体积是核心问题。已有绝对可分态纯度上界2/(mn)，但APPT态这一更大类的相应极值、精确性与体积渐近尚不清楚，且Dũng–Khôi qutrit–qudit猜想有待检验。

Method: 利用Hildebrand判据构造显式外谱多胞体并分类其顶点；在外多胞体上优化纯度，结合Song–Chen关于2⊗3系统的结果；利用多胞体导出熵下界，并结合Song–Chen极点分类得到2⊗n系统的精确最小熵；通过内多胞体与外谱多胞体的相对体积精确公式，给出量子比特–qudit相对谱体积a_n的两边夹逼。

Result: 对每个mn≥6，APPT态纯度上界为4/(3mn)，渐近最优，优于绝对可分态的2/(mn)；该界对所有APPT态成立且在2⊗n（n≥3）取到，但对3⊗n（n≥3）唯一外多胞体极大点非APPT，故界严格并反驳Dũng–Khôi猜想。导出任意二分维度的显式熵下界及2⊗n系统精确最小熵。相对体积夹逼比值小于4且趋于3，从而a_n=Θ(√n(4/27)^n)，APPT谱集（等价绝对可分谱集）相对体积的精确指数衰减率为ln(27/4)。

Conclusion: 外谱多胞体为APPT态的纯度与熵极值提供了统一且显式的框架，所得纯度上界对2⊗n系统紧、对3⊗n系统严格并否定Dũng–Khôi猜想；同时确定了量子比特–qudit相对谱体积的指数衰减率ln(27/4)。

Abstract: We study maximum purity and minimum von Neumann entropy of absolutely positive partial transpose (APPT) states, together with the relative volume of their spectral sets. A consequence of Hildebrand's criterion gives an explicit outer spectral polytope, whose vertices we classify. Optimizing purity over this polytope, together with the Song--Chen result for the $2\otimes3$ system, yields, for every $mn\ge6$, an explicit upper bound on the purity of APPT states that is asymptotic to $4/(3mn)$. This improves the previous $2/(mn)$ upper bound for absolutely separable states. The new bound applies to every APPT state, a class containing all absolutely separable states, and is sharp for every $2\otimes n$ system with $n\ge3$. For every $3\otimes n$ system with $n\ge3$, however, the unique outer-polytope maximizer is not APPT, so the bound is strict and disproves the Dũng--Khôi qutrit--qudit conjecture. The polytope also gives an explicit entropy lower bound in arbitrary bipartite dimensions and, together with the Song--Chen extreme-point classification, the exact minimum entropy for every $2\otimes n$ system. Finally, exact formulas for the relative volumes of an inner polytope and the outer spectral polytope give explicit two-sided bounds on the qubit--qudit relative spectral volume $a_n$ whose ratio is less than $4$ and tends to $3$. Consequently, $a_n=Θ\!\left(\sqrt n(4/27)^n\right)$, and the relative volume of the qubit--qudit APPT spectral set (equivalently, the absolutely separable spectral set) has exact exponential decay rate $\ln(27/4)$.

</details>


### [172] [Algorithmic Design of Heralded Linear Optical Circuits for Multipartite Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18002)
*Jaehee Kim,Hon Wai Lau,William J. Munro,Joonsuk Huh,Seungbeom Chin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将预兆式光子电路设计建模为算法图搜索问题，基于线性量子图（LQG）图像大幅缩减搜索空间，实现多体纠缠资源态电路的自动构建与优化，并设计了多类资源态的高效方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预兆式多体纠缠是量子信息任务的关键资源，但设计能生成特定目标态的线性光学电路通常很困难，主要原因是所需光学结构复杂。因此需要一个系统化、可自动化的电路设计方法。

Method: 将预兆式光子电路的设计表述为算法图搜索问题；利用线性量子图（LQG）图像把电路结构重建为图，并自动识别合适的图，从而大幅缩减搜索空间，实现电路的自动构建与优化。

Result: 为多种有用的多体资源态设计了高效方案，包括超图魔术态、量子纠错码、一般三量子比特态以及长度-1毛虫图态。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了系统性发现预兆式资源态的算法框架，为日益复杂的多体纠缠资源的自动化设计奠定了基础。

Abstract: Heralded multipartite entanglement is a key resource for various quantum information tasks. However, designing linear optical circuits that generate specific target states is generally challenging due to the complexity of the required optical structures. Here we formulate the design of heralded photonic circuits as an algorithmic graph-search problem. Our framework enables the automated construction and optimization of heralded photonic circuits by substantially reducing the search space using the linear quantum graph (LQG) picture. Our strategy reconstructs circuit structures as graphs in the picture and identifies suitable graphs automatically. As a result, we design efficient schemes for a broad range of useful multipartite resource states, including hypergraph magic states, quantum error correcting codes, general three-qubit states and length-1 caterpillar graph states. Our work establishes an algorithmic framework for the systematic discovery of heralded resource states, laying the foundation for the automated design of increasingly complex multipartite entangled resources.

</details>


### [173] [Sublattice-selective control of spin reversal in metasurface-coupled Kagome interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18012)
*Ximo Wang,Qiwei Han,Zhenqi Bai,Ruyue Guo,Min Feng,Yichi Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究Kagome晶格中光子界面态的相干操控，提出1:1:-2亚晶格Raman控制图案以消除自旋-能谷耦合的抵消，实现高保真模式转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子界面态的相干操控需要控制场与其内部模式结构匹配，但均匀Raman控制在耦合对映自旋、同能谷界面模式时存在天然限制，需要找到能突破该限制的控制方案。

Method: 以非线性非局域超表面附近的光子介导交换为动机，构建双组分Kagome晶格模型，用Dirac旋量分析均匀Raman控制的耦合性质，设计1:1:-2亚晶格控制图案，并通过全区域拓扑、完整晶格传播以及三维铌酸锂超胞进行验证。

Result: Dirac旋量分析表明均匀Raman控制在最低阶无法耦合对映自旋、同能谷界面模式；1:1:-2图案消除该抵消并在固定RMS幅度下最大化投影耦合。720维计算在RMS驱动0.04t下给出目标模式保真度0.999937、泄漏6.27×10⁻⁵。三维铌酸锂超胞以0.373%相对误差复现该复杂寻址图案，并提供非局域交换、衰减和电光频率转换矩阵。

Conclusion: 模式分辨的控制原理为电磁设计提供了定量目标，但完整的自旋相关器件实现仍需进一步标定。

Abstract: Coherent manipulation of photonic interface states requires a control field that matches their internal mode structure. We study this requirement in a two-component Kagome lattice motivated by photon-mediated exchange near a nonlinear nonlocal metasurface. The Dirac spinors show why uniform Raman control cannot couple opposite-spin, same-valley interface modes at leading order, even when their spatial envelopes coincide. A $1:1:-2$ sublattice pattern removes this cancellation and maximizes the projected coupling at fixed root-mean-square amplitude within the diagonal-control class. We test this control scheme through full-zone topology and complete lattice propagation. For a smooth, gapped interface, a $720$-dimensional calculation gives target-mode fidelity $0.999937$ with leakage $6.27\times10^{-5}$ at RMS drive $0.04t$. Trace-preserving dynamics gives the separate survival condition needed for successful conversion. An exploratory three-dimensional lithium-niobate supercell reproduces the complex addressing pattern with $0.373\%$ relative error and provides nonlocal exchange, decay and electro-optic frequency-conversion matrices. The mode-resolved control principle thus gives quantitative electromagnetic design targets; the full spin-dependent device realization still requires further calibration.

</details>


### [174] [Feasibility Ordering of Entanglement-Source Placement for Qubit Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18803)
*Samuel Marquez Gonzalez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了一个关于量子纠缠源放置位置的猜想：对于任意量子比特信道，若顺序复合能保持纠缠，则并行作用不能完全破坏纠缠，由此证明中点放置在中继通信中是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在由两个含噪量子比特信道构成的通信线路中，纠缠源的放置位置（中点放置 vs 端点放置）影响纠缠分布效果。近期工作从解析与数值角度提出猜想：中点放置至少与端点放置一样有利，但该猜想尚未得到对任意量子比特信道的严格证明，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 采用近期工作提出的转置因子分解判据（transpose-factorization criterion）进行证明。首先利用量子Sinkhorn缩放将任意严格正的量子比特信道转化为单位（unital）代表；再借助单位量子比特信道的特殊正规形式，构造出转置映射经原信道的所需因子分解；最后通过退极化正则化与纠缠破坏信道集合的闭性，将结论推广到任意信道。

Result: 证明了对任意量子比特信道，若顺序复合 Λ₂∘Λ₁ 能保持纠缠（即 Λ₂∘Λ₁ ∉ EB），则并行作用 Λ₁⊗Λ₂ ∉ EA，即不会湮灭全部纠缠。等价地，Λ₁⊗Λ₂ ∈ EA 蕴含 Λ₂∘Λ₁ ∈ EB；交换信道顺序后相反复合次序同样成立。

Conclusion: 该结果在可行性（feasibility）意义下严格证明了近期提出的猜想：对于所有量子比特信道，中点放置均为最优。这为量子通信线路中纠缠源的最优配置提供了完整的理论依据。

Abstract: This work studies the placement of an entanglement source along a communication line formed by two noisy qubit channels. Recent work argued, on analytical and numerical grounds, that midpoint placement should be at least as favorable as endpoint placement. Here it is shown that, for arbitrary qubit channels, if a sequential composition can preserve entanglement, then the corresponding parallel action cannot annihilate all entanglement. The proof uses the transpose-factorization criterion introduced in that recent work. Quantum Sinkhorn scaling converts every strictly positive qubit channel into a unital representative, and the special normal form of unital qubit channels then yields the required factorization of the transposed map through the original channel. Depolarizing regularization and the closedness of the set of entanglement-breaking channels extend the result to arbitrary channels. Consequently, $Λ_1 \otimes Λ_2 \in \mathrm{EA}$ implies $Λ_2 \circ Λ_1 \in \mathrm{EB}$, and, by exchanging the two channels, the same holds for the opposite composition order. This proves the recent conjecture that midpoint placement is optimal for all qubit channels, in the feasibility sense in which that optimality was originally defined.

</details>


### [175] [Remote Adiabatic Controlled-Z Gate between Distant Superconducting Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18081)
*Si-Yu Xu,Yi-Rong Jin,Wen-Gang Zhang,Hai-Feng Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出并数值研究了一种基于多模同轴电缆连接的远程绝热控制-Z门，无需额外可调耦合器，30 cm电缆下门保真度误差低至4.71×10⁻⁷，为跨芯片可扩展超导量子处理器提供方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化量子计算需要空间分离量子比特间的直接纠缠门，以跨处理器模块执行非局域量子电路，从而缓解单芯片扩展限制并扩展系统有效连通性。

Method: 在两个频率可调超导量子比特之间，通过多模同轴电缆实现远程绝热控制-Z门；利用电缆介导的相互作用，无需额外可调耦合器，保持简单电路结构。针对30 cm电缆，仿真纳入十个驻波模式进行优化，并考察量子比特频率涨落与毫米级电缆长度变化的影响，同时计算50 cm和100 cm电缆情形。

Result: 30 cm电缆、无退相干条件下，优化后门保真度误差为4.71×10⁻⁷，门时长为376.7 ns；门在量子比特频率涨落和毫米级电缆长度变化下仍保持高保真度；50 cm和100 cm电缆的计算表明其适用于更大低温系统。

Conclusion: 该方案为将高保真量子操作扩展到单个芯片之外、迈向可扩展互连超导量子处理器提供了有前景的途径。

Abstract: Direct entangling gates between spatially separated qubits are a key capability for modular quantum computing. They enable nonlocal quantum circuits to be executed across different processor modules, thereby alleviating single-chip scaling constraints and expanding the effective connectivity of the system. Here, we propose and numerically investigate a remote adiabatic controlled-Z gate between two frequency-tunable superconducting qubits connected by a multi-mode coaxial cable. The cable-mediated interaction enables the gate to be implemented without additional tunable couplers, preserving a simple circuit architecture. For a 30-cm cable, simulations incorporating ten standing-wave modes yield an optimized gate infidelity of $4.71\times10^{-7}$ with a duration of 376.7 ns in the absence of decoherence. The gate maintains high fidelity under qubit-frequency fluctuations and millimeter-scale cable-length variations. Calculations for 50- and 100-cm cable situations demonstrate its potential for even larger cryogenic systems. These results provide a promising approach to extending high-fidelity quantum operations beyond individual chips and toward scalable, interconnected superconducting quantum processors.

</details>


### [176] [Quantum Behaviors Are Not Semialgebraic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18865)
*Minbo Gao,Zhengfeng Ji,Chenghua Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文否定地回答了Tsirelson于1993年提出的问题：量子关联集合是否可半代数描述。作者证明，在每方四个二值测量的Bell场景下，有限维量子行为集合、其闭包以及交换算子集合均不是半代数的，甚至局部也不存在有限的实解析描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tsirelson在1993年提出一个长期未解的问题：Bell场景中由局域测量得到的量子行为集合（quantum behaviors）是否可以用有限个多项式方程与不等式的布尔组合来描述（即是否为半代数集）。该问题关系到量子关联集合的结构复杂性，以及是否存在精确的有限半定表示，因此具有基础性意义。

Method: 论文通过构造性的数学证明来回答该问题。具体地，考虑每方各四个二值测量的Bell场景，对有限维量子行为集合、其拓扑闭包以及交换算子（commuting-operator）集合分别进行分析，并证明它们在某个特定经典行为附近无法被有限实解析函数刻画。

Result: 主要结果是否定的：在每方四个二值测量的场景中，有限维量子行为集合、其闭包以及交换算子集合均不是半代数的（nonsemialgebraic）。更强的结论是，这些集合在某个特定经典行为附近甚至不存在局部的有限实解析描述。

Conclusion: 这些结果排除了精确的有限半定表示（finite semidefinite representations）的可能性，并表明Navascués-Pironio-Acín（NPA）层级不存在任何有限层级能够精确刻画这些量子行为集合。这一工作最终否定了Tsirelson关于量子行为集合半代数性的猜想。

Abstract: The conditional probabilities achievable by local measurements on a shared quantum state in a Bell scenario form the set of quantum behaviors, whose structure was studied by Tsirelson. In 1993, Tsirelson asked whether this set is semialgebraic, that is, describable by finite Boolean combinations of polynomial equations and inequalities. This question has remained open. We answer it in the negative: with four binary measurements per party, the set of finite-dimensional quantum behaviors, its closure, and the commuting-operator set are all nonsemialgebraic. More strongly, none admits a finite real-analytic description even locally near a particular classical behavior. These results rule out exact finite semidefinite representations and show that no finite level of the Navascués-Pironio-Acín hierarchy characterizes these sets exactly.

</details>


### [177] [Redesigning the linear--quadratic--Gaussian cost function for feedback cooling of a quantum harmonic oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18195)
*Shuma Sugiura,Koki Shiraishi,Masahito Ueda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文针对连续监测量子谐振子的反馈冷却，重新设计LQG代价函数以显式包含反馈引起的势阱平移，从而直接优化相对平移后势阱的振子能量，并推导出最优反馈律与稳态声子数解析表达式，证明其冷却性能优于LPF反馈，且优势随探测效率趋近1而显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LQG控制仅针对预设代价函数最优，而代价函数的选择决定控制的物理目标。在通过平移俘获势阱最小值实现连续监测量子谐振子反馈冷却时，物理上合理的冷却目标应是最小化振子相对反馈平移后势阱的能量；但传统LQG从固定原点评估能量，无法直接优化该量，因此需要重新设计代价函数。

Method: 引入显式考虑反馈所致势阱平移的重新设计代价函数，并据此推导对应的最优反馈律，同时获得最小可达稳态声子占据数的解析表达式。

Result: 重新设计的LQG控制相比针对同一冷却目标设计的低通滤波（LPF）反馈可获得更低的占据数。在目前实验可达到的探测效率下，这一改善较小，表明LPF反馈已接近最优；但随着探测效率趋近1，优势变得显著，在有限测量强度下更有助于达到运动基态。

Conclusion: 传统与重新设计的代价函数代表不同的控制目标，而非同一优化问题的不同实现；重新设计LQG在高效探测区对量子谐振子反馈冷却具有明显优势。

Abstract: Linear--quadratic--Gaussian (LQG) control is optimal only with respect to a prescribed cost function, the choice of which dictates the physical objective of the control. We consider feedback cooling of a continuously monitored quantum harmonic oscillator by shifting the minimum of its trapping potential. In this setting, the physically relevant cooling objective can be defined as minimizing the oscillator's energy relative to the feedback-shifted potential. In contrast, conventional LQG control evaluates the energy from a fixed origin and thus fails to directly optimize this quantity. To address this problem, we introduce a redesigned cost function that explicitly accounts for the feedback-induced shift of the potential. We then derive the corresponding optimal feedback law and obtain an analytic expression for the minimum achievable steady-state phonon occupation number. The redesigned LQG control achieves a lower occupation number than low-pass-filter (LPF) feedback formulated for the same cooling objective. While this improvement is minor at detection efficiencies currently attainable in experiments---indicating that LPF feedback already delivers near-optimal cooling performance---the advantage becomes pronounced as the detection efficiency approaches unity. In this regime, the redesigned LQG control provides an increasing advantage for reaching the motional ground state at a finite measurement strength. We clarify that the conventional and redesigned cost functions represent distinct control objectives rather than different implementations of the same optimization problem.

</details>


### [178] [Optimal Temporal Hiding in Correlated Quantum Reference-Frame Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18948)
*Maxim V. Churilov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文将有限群值时间参考系建模为关联随机酉过程，揭示其完整轨迹规律可作为精确操作坐标，并把时间隐藏问题转化为q元编码问题，给出保密载荷与可见深度的编码界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间参考系常被视为独立信道的列表，忽略了信道间的关联性；作者希望刻画这种关联结构能否在操作层面被完全分辨，并量化"时间隐私"（trajectory law不可见部分）与相干时间隐私的极限。

Method: 用有限群G上的随机酉过程描述时间参考系；在载体包含G所有不可约表示时，用单辅助输入策略分辨所有群元素；将最优输出端因果后处理归约为G^n上的经典卷积；把时间隐藏转化为q元编码，借助对偶码最小距离、嵌套码相对距离、广义Reed-Solomon码以及量子擦除纠错进行分析。

Result: 策略半距离等于总变差距离d_TV(μ,ν)；均匀陪集律在少于d(C^⊥)个槽上相同而全局完美可分；得到(1-ε)log M ≤ (n-t)log q+η+h_2(ε)；嵌套码满足R+t+d_rel(D,C)-1≤n与R+t+2e+f≤n，嵌套广义Reed-Solomon码在存在范围内达到界；相干隐藏等价于量子擦除纠错，满足log_q K+2t≤n。

Conclusion: 结果区分了经典轨迹隐私与相干时间隐私，并量化了在受限过程层析下可鲁棒隐藏的载荷，为时间参考系的隐私与纠错提供统一编码框架。

Abstract: A finite group-valued temporal reference frame defines a correlated random-unitary process rather than a list of independent channels. We identify a regime in which its complete trajectory law is an exact operational coordinate. If the carrier contains every irreducible representation of the finite group $G$, one ancilla-assisted input resolves all group elements, and for arbitrary laws $μ,ν$ on $G^n$ the strategy half-distance equals $d_{\mathrm{TV}}(μ,ν)$. Optimal output-side causal post-processing likewise reduces to classical convolution on $G^n$. This converts temporal hiding into a $q$-ary coding problem. Uniform coset laws of an $[n,k]_q$ code are identical on every set of fewer than $d(C^\perp)$ slots and perfectly distinguishable globally. For $M$ sectors with leakage at most $η$ from $t$ selected slots and global decoding error $ε$, we obtain $(1-ε)\log M \leq (n-t)\log q+η+h_2(ε)$. For nested codes $C\subset D$, the payload $R=\dim D-\dim C$, invisible depth $t=d_{\mathrm{rel}}(C^\perp,D^\perp)-1$, and sector distance $d_{\mathrm{rel}}(D,C)$ obey $R+t+d_{\mathrm{rel}}(D,C)-1\leq n$ and $R+t+2e+f\leq n$, where $e$ and $f$ are adversarial errors and known erasures. Nested generalized Reed-Solomon codes attain the bounds in their existence range. We also give the exact projection-rank leakage profile and show that hiding arbitrary coherent superpositions from $t$ slots is precisely quantum erasure correction, yielding $\log_q K+2t\leq n$. The results separate classical trajectory privacy from coherent temporal privacy and quantify the robust payload hidden from reduced process tomography.

</details>


### [179] [The Expressive Power of Constrained QAOA: What You Might Have MISsed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18209)
*Boris Tsvelikhovskiy,Bao Bach,Ilya Safro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在最大独立集（MIS）约束量子优化中比较了标准受控比特翻转混合器与Flip-or-Stay混合器，通过动力学李代数分析证明后者严格包含前者，并构造出真空态轨道维度严格不同的无限图族，建立了两类架构的表达能力定性分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保持可行性的混合器哈密顿量相比惩罚编码更具优势，但在QAOA中强制约束如何改变可达状态空间尚不清楚；本文旨在厘清两类混合器在MIS问题上的状态空间可达性与表达能力差异。

Method: 作者考察MIS问题的可行子空间W_F，将Flip-or-Stay混合器限制到该子空间，指出其正对应独立集重构图的平移负拉普拉斯算子；随后分别计算两类混合器下独立参数化局部控制生成的李代数、标准MIS-QAOA的动力学李代数及其嵌入关系，并构造无限图族比较真空态动力学群轨道维度；最后推导自由架构在酉设计极限下的损失-方差精确公式。

Result: 对每个至少两个顶点的连通输入图，证明两种混合器的独立参数化局部控制均可在可行子空间上生成完整幺正李代数；标准QAOA中，传统混合器对应的动力学李代数嵌入Flip-or-Stay对应代数；构造出真空态轨道维度严格不同的无限图族，确立表达能力定性分离；两种混合器下标准QAOA从空集初始化均可在有限深度制备完全支撑在最大独立集上的态；自由架构下给出以可行独立集数目及其基数方差表示的精确损失-方差公式。

Conclusion: Flip-or-Stay混合器通过改变对角结构扩大了可达量子态集合，并在动力学李代数与真空态轨道维度上严格强于传统混合器，为约束量子优化中混合器设计提供了理论依据；同时损失-方差公式将表达能力与实际优化性能指标联系起来。

Abstract: Feasibility-preserving mixer Hamiltonians offer an attractive alternative to penalty encodings for constrained quantum optimization, yet how enforcing constraints alters the state space reachable by the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) remains largely unexplored. We address this question within the context of the Maximum Independent Set (MIS) problem. Alongside the standard controlled bit-flip mixer, we analyze the alternative $\mathit{Flip\text{-}or\text{-}Stay}$ mixer, whose restriction to the feasible subspace $W_{\mathcal{F}}$ corresponds precisely to the shifted negative Laplacian of the independent-set reconfiguration graph. Although both mixers induce identical transitions between independent sets, the diagonal modification changes the spectral structure and can substantially enlarge the set of reachable quantum states.
  For every connected input graph with at least two vertices, we prove that independently parameterized local controls associated with either mixer generate the full unitary Lie algebra on the feasible subspace. Standard QAOA exhibits a more nuanced algebraic structure. We prove that the dynamical Lie algebra associated with MIS-QAOA employing the conventional mixer embeds into its Flip-or-Stay counterpart. Furthermore, we construct an infinite family of graphs for which the corresponding vacuum-state dynamical group orbits exhibit strictly distinct dimensions, establishing a qualitative expressivity separation between the two architectures. For either mixer, we show that standard QAOA initialized in the empty set can prepare a state supported entirely on maximum independent sets at finite depth. For the free architectures, we also derive an exact loss-variance formula in the unitary-design limit, expressed through the number of feasible independent sets and the variance of their cardinalities.

</details>


### [180] [Proof of Heisenberg's Error-Disturbance Relation for Individual Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18211)
*Seiji Kosugi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Heisenberg原始设想下，通过可观测量幺正变换方程严格推导了误差-扰动关系，证明误差ε_X(x̂_t)与扰动η_X(p̂_0)的不确定性关系在单次测量层面（由具体读数X表征）严格成立，并验证了传统以无条件态平均定义的误差-扰动关系亦成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Heisenberg最初设想误差-扰动关系的前提是测量后状态必须与Kennard-Robertson不确定性关系保持一致，这要求测量误差须由后验可观测量x̂_t而非先验可观测量x̂_0来表述，因为后验测量误差决定电子测量后的不确定性。本文旨在在arXiv:1504.03779初步概念基础上，为单次测量结果中的基本权衡提供更精细的理论基础。

Method: 从可观测量的幺正变换方程出发，对误差-扰动原理进行严格推导。将后验探针可观测量X̂_t的每一次读数X视为完成一次测量事件，产生特定的条件对象态，并要求Kennard-Robertson不确定性关系对这些态成立；据此在单次测量层面定义并分析误差ε_X(x̂_t)与扰动η_X(p̂_0)。

Result: 证明了误差ε_X(x̂_t)与扰动η_X(p̂_0)之间的不确定性关系在单次测量层面严格成立，且由具体读数X表征；同时验证了以无条件态平均评估误差的传统误差-扰动关系同样成立。

Conclusion: 本文为理解单次测量结果中的基本权衡提供了更精细的理论基础，将误差-扰动关系从系综平均层面推广并严格建立在单次测量层面。

Abstract: Heisenberg originally envisioned the error-disturbance relation under the premise that the state after measurement must remain consistent with the Kennard-Robertson uncertainty relation. This implies that the measurement error must be formulated via a posterior observable $\hat{x}_t$, rather than a prior observable $\hat{x}_0$, because the posterior measurement error determines the post-measurement uncertainty of the electron. Building upon the preliminary conceptual foundation reported in arXiv:1504.03779, this paper presents a rigorous derivation of the error-disturbance principle formulated from the unitary transformation equations of observables. Each readout $X$ of a posterior probe observable $\hat{X}_t$ completes a single measurement event, producing a specific conditional object state. The Kennard-Robertson uncertainty relation must hold for these states. Specifically, we show that the uncertainty relation between the error $ε_{X}(\hat{x}_t)$ and the disturbance $η_{X}(\hat{p}_0)$ holds strictly at the level of individual measurements, characterized by the specific readout $X$. We also verify that conventional error-disturbance relations, where the errors are evaluated by averaging over the unconditioned state, hold true. Our results provide a refined theoretical basis for understanding the fundamental trade-off in individual measurement outcomes.

</details>


### [181] [Code-Visible Liouvillian Modes and Coherent Logical Errors in Quantum Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18978)
*Marina Gordeychuk,Oleg Kiselev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将线性系统理论中的可观测性与最小实现概念应用于量子纠错信道的Lindblad动力学，定义了"码可见空间"K_code，给出逻辑信道的最小实现，并为相干噪声的谱特征提供了分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干哈密顿漂移和系统性误差在量子纠错中的传播方式与随机Pauli噪声不同：它们会产生逻辑信道的相位成分，而该成分在完整Pauli twirling下会丢失。因此需要一套统一语言来刻画噪声（尤其是相干噪声）如何透过编码、恢复与解码方案表现为逻辑信道。

Method: 将线性系统理论中的可观测性与最小实现思想应用于逻辑信道 ω↦Φ_t(ω)=D e^{tL}E(ω)，其中E为编码、D为恢复与解码映射。定义码可见空间K_code为可解码逻辑可观测量关于L†的Krylov闭包，并对其做可观测量因子分解，从而得到逻辑信道的最小实现。在该框架下考察Knill-Laflamme条件、稳定子码上的Pauli噪声以及恢复映射的作用。

Result: 在该形式下：Knill-Laflamme条件表现为短时一致性检查；稳定子码上的Pauli噪声约化为综合症-逻辑类上的链式过程；恢复映射的选择相当于对可见模式施加谱滤波。对量子存储，该语言给出了用纠错循环逻辑信道的主导可见特征值来估计存储时间的方法。并特别关注相干噪声的谱特征及其对解码器的依赖性。

Conclusion: 该工作为分析相干噪声与系统误差在量子纠错中的传播提供了方法学框架，将纠错码性能与逻辑信道的谱结构联系起来，并表明相干噪声的谱特征与解码器选择密切相关，可用于估计量子存储时间。

Abstract: Coherent Hamiltonian drifts and systematic errors can pass through quantum error correction differently than stochastic Pauli noise: they generate phase components of the logical channel that are lost under full Pauli twirling. We consider the Lindbladian dynamics of \(n\) physical qubits propagated through an effective logical channel of a fixed encoding, recovery, and decoding scheme. The work is methodological in character: standard ideas of observability and minimal realization from linear systems theory are applied to the channel \[
  ω
  \mapsto
  Φ_t(ω)
  =
  \cD e^{t\cL}\cE(ω), \] where \(\cE\) encodes the logical state and \(\cD\) is the chosen recovery map with final decoding. The minimal code-visible space \(\cK_{\rm code}\) is defined as the Krylov closure of the decodable logical observables with respect to \(\cL^\dagger\); its observable factorization yields a minimal realization of the logical channel. In this form, the Knill--Laflamme conditions serve as a short-time consistency check, Pauli noise on stabilizer codes reduces to a chain on syndrome-logical classes, and the choice of recovery map acts as a spectral filter on the visible modes. For quantum memory, the same language yields an estimate of the storage time in terms of the dominant visible eigenvalues of the logical channel of the correction cycle. Particular attention is paid to the spectral signatures of coherent noise and to how these signatures depend on the chosen decoder.

</details>


### [182] [Information capacity of quantum statistics: Fock-state tests of a discrete binary-sequence model on cloud photonic quantum processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10216)
*Chiran Wijesundara,Octavia T. Volpe,Dejan Stojkovic,Herbert Fotso,Tim Thomay*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 商用云光子量子处理器已达到足够精度，可对"量子力学作为离散理论的统计极限"这一假设中的信息容量给出下界：分析排除了n≤100，二光子态寄存器容量若有限则超过10²，并开辟了当前硬件触及10⁻³（n~10³）阈值的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文的核心前提是量子力学可能只是某个更基本离散理论的统计极限；此类理论赋予物理系统有限的信息容量，且系统偏离量子统计的程度由其对该容量的使用比例决定。作者希望检验这一设想是否可被现有商用光子量子硬件定量约束。

Method: 采用Powers等人的二元序列模型作为具体检验理论：结果概率源于对长度n的离散序列计数，n→∞时恢复量子力学，n即制备态背后寄存器的信息容量。在可编程干涉仪上测量光子Fock态|1⟩、|1,1⟩、预报|2⟩及级联分束器对，并原位确定主导系统误差；使用由"旋转可合成"要求唯一确定的合成一致参数化，并采用参数自助法校准的随机效应似然分析。

Result: 合成一致参数化在偏差1.24/n下恢复量子力学；随机效应似然分析排除所有n≤100，即承载二光子态的寄存器信息容量若有限则超过10²。级联分束器直接检验合成律：数据显示分裂不变性，以8σ排除朴素计数合成，并证实干涉符号规则。与量子分配律的曲线平均偏差大于2.3×10⁻²被以95%置信水平排除，原发表的线性参数化被彻底排除。

Conclusion: 由于编译偏移按电路冻结因而可校准，当前硬件可触及10⁻³（n~10³）精度下限：云光子处理器成为量子基础研究的定量仪器，信息容量成为可由实验界定下限的物理量。

Abstract: Our central premise is that quantum mechanics may be the statistical limit of a more fundamental discrete theory: any such theory equips a physical system with a finite information capacity, and its departure from quantum statistics is controlled by how much of that capacity the system uses. We show that commercial cloud photonic quantum processors have reached the precision required to bound this capacity from below, using the binary-sequence model of Powers et al. as the concrete test theory: outcome probabilities arise from counting discrete sequences of length $n$, quantum mechanics is recovered as $n \to \infty$, and $n$ measures the information capacity of the register behind a prepared state. Photon Fock states $|1\rangle$, $|1,1\rangle$, heralded $|2\rangle$, and cascaded beam-splitter pairs are measured on programmable interferometers with dominant systematics determined in situ. The model's composition-consistent parametrization, singled out by requiring that rotations compose, recovers quantum mechanics with deviations $1.24/n$; a random-effects likelihood analysis calibrated by parametric bootstrap excludes all $n \le 100$: the information capacity of the register carrying the two-photon state, if finite, exceeds $10^2$. Cascaded beam splitters test the composition law directly: the data are split-invariant, excluding naive count composition at $8σ$ and confirming the interference-sign rule. Model-independently, curve-averaged deviations from the quantum partition law larger than $2.3\times10^{-2}$ are excluded at 95% CL, and the originally published linear parametrization is excluded outright. Because the compilation offset is frozen per circuit it is calibratable, opening the $10^{-3}$ floor ($n \sim 10^3$) to current hardware: cloud photonic processors are quantitative instruments for quantum foundations, and information capacity an experimentally boundable quantity.

</details>


### [183] [Spectral-Gap Bounds and Timescales for Purity Loss in Hamiltonian--Pointer Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18244)
*Orhan Amirov,Necati Çelik*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了系统经冯·诺依曼型相互作用耦合连续变量指针后的纯度动力学，给出精确纯度解析表达式、双边纯度界、短时行为、渐近时间尺度及N能级示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究连续变量指针测量下系统纯度的动力学机制，厘清谱间距、能量方差、耦合强度与指针宽度在纯度损失中的不同作用。

Method: 采用冯·诺依曼型哈密顿量 H_int=gH⊗p，以初始高斯指针态产生能量依赖的条件平移，利用谱间距显式确定平移间的相互交叠；对指针求迹得到约化密度算符并导出含时纯度的精确解析式。

Result: 建立由已布居谱支撑上最小与最大非零能隙控制的双边纯度界，二能级情形下精确成立；短时纯度下降由初态哈密顿量方差支配，满足 P''(0)=-(g^2/σ^2)Var_ψS(H)；导出纯度在给定容差内趋近渐近值的时间尺度 t_ε∝σ/(|g|Δ_min)；等权等间距N能级系统的渐近纯度为1/N。

Conclusion: 纯度损失可由哈密顿量条件化的谱特征刻画，谱间距、能量方差、耦合强度与指针宽度扮演不同角色，结果为连续变量指针下退相干与纯度演化提供解析框架。

Abstract: We investigate the purity dynamics of a quantum system coupled to a continuous-variable pointer through a von Neumann-type interaction Hamiltonian of the form $\hat H_{\mathrm{int}}=g\,\hat H\otimes\hat p$. For an initially Gaussian pointer state, the interaction generates energy-dependent conditional translations whose mutual overlaps are determined explicitly by the populated spectral separations of the system Hamiltonian. After tracing out the pointer degrees of freedom, we obtain the reduced density operator and derive an exact analytical expression for the time-dependent purity.
  Using this expression, we establish two-sided purity bounds governed by the minimum and maximum nonzero energy gaps on the populated spectral support. These bounds provide a state-dependent spectral characterization of the loss of purity and become exact for two-level systems. We further show that the short-time decrease of purity is controlled by the Hamiltonian variance of the initial state, with $\mathcal P''(0)=-(g^2/σ^2) \operatorname{Var}_{ψ_S}(\hat H)$. In addition, an explicit sufficient timescale is derived for the purity to approach its asymptotic value within a prescribed tolerance, revealing the scaling $t_{\varepsilon}\proptoσ/(|g|Δ_{\min})$. Finally, the general results are illustrated for an equally weighted $N$-level system with an equally spaced spectrum, for which the asymptotic purity is $1/N$. The analysis clarifies the distinct roles of spectral separation, energy variance, coupling strength, and pointer width in Hamiltonian-conditioned purity loss.

</details>


### [184] [New directions in dynamical expectation estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18246)
*Panjin Kim,Kyung Chul Jeong,Yun-Tak Oh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出联合状态与可观测量的近似方法，通过耦合损失函数的扫描算法，将动力学期望值误差降低2-3个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算动力学期望值通常对状态或可观测量分别优化近似，忽略了二者误差在最终表达式中的联合效应。

Method: 采用扫描算法，为前向状态与后向可观测量更新设计耦合损失函数，使每个近似同时受状态和可观测量的引导，并给出精确误差关系作为分析依据。

Result: 在30量子比特随机电路测试中，相同键维下误差比变分状态压缩小2至3个数量级。

Conclusion: 结果支持进一步探索面向动力学期望值估计的状态与可观测量联合近似。

Abstract: Computing dynamical expectation values typically relies on approximations optimized for the state or observable separately, without accounting for how their errors combine in the final expression.This work explores an alternative approach in which each approximation is guided by both the state and the observable, accounting for how they jointly determine the target expectation value. This idea is implemented through a sweep algorithm with coupled loss functions for forward state and backward observable updates. Exact error relations provide an analytical rationale for how the proposed losses can improve the accuracy. Numerical tests on 30-qubit random circuits show errors two to three orders of magnitude smaller than those of variational state compression at equal bond dimensions. These results motivate further exploration of joint state and observable approximation for dynamical expectation value estimation.

</details>


### [185] [Quantum Probability Current Guided Reduction of Coupling Control Degrees of Freedom for Excitation Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18250)
*Liuheng Cao,Lin Zhang,Junde Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于控制诱导的量子概率流梯度变化（经图Hodge分解求得）的边排序策略，用于筛选开放量子网络中需施加相干控制的边；在FMO七站点模型上以更少控制边实现接近全控制的输运增强，并降低控制代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时变相干控制可增强开放量子网络中的激发输运，但独立控制每条位点间耦合会带来高维控制空间，导致优化困难。为此需要一种能够筛选关键耦合边的降维策略。

Method: 引入边排序策略：基于控制引起的、时间积分量子概率流梯度分量的变化量进行排序，该量通过图Hodge分解获得。将该策略应用于七站点FMO模型，并选取不同规模的边集进行对比；同时开展退相干扫描，以及与随机边集、随机网络的对照实验。

Result: 在FMO模型上，六边集保留了全控制的99.83%增强效果；四边集保留97.60%，同时相较全控制将脉冲注量（作为控制代价的代理指标）降低41.55%。退相干扫描及与随机边集、随机网络的比较为该排序策略的相关性与潜在普适性提供了数值支持。

Conclusion: 由量子概率流引导的边选择能够在保持高输运性能的同时大幅缩减控制空间，并降低控制代价，表明该策略可有效缓解开放量子网络相干控制的高维优化难题。

Abstract: Time-dependent coherent control can enhance excitation transport in open quantum networks, but independently controlling every inter-site coupling creates a control space of high dimension and leads to difficult optimization problems. We introduce an edge-ranking strategy based on the control-induced change in the gradient component of the time-integrated quantum probability current, which is obtained via a graph Hodge decomposition. When our strategy is applied to the seven-site Fenna-Matthews-Olson (FMO) model, the six-edge set retains $99.83\%$ of the enhancement achieved by full control, and the four-edge set retains $97.60\%$ while reducing the pulse fluence---used here as a proxy for control effort---by $41.55\%$ relative to full control. Dephasing scans and comparisons with random edge sets and random networks provide numerical support for the relevance and potential broader utility of the ranking. These results show that edge selection guided by the quantum probability current can substantially reduce the control space while preserving high transport performance with lower control effort.

</details>


### [186] [Bath dimension and initial entropy for closed repeated use of a quantum channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18267)
*Seth Douglas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文刻画了在封闭器件中重复使用固定有限维量子信道时所需的"浴"资源，给出了可达区域的精确刻画：s≥0, r+s≥h, r−s≥κ，最小维度速率为(h+κ)/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在无重置、无丢弃、无新辅助态、无未计数控制器的封闭器件中，反复使用同一固定有限维量子信道时，需要多少"浴"资源。目标是刻画浴维度与初始熵之间的可达区域，并应对具有量子存储和参考的任意自适应用户，使近似误差趋于零。

Method: 设浴维度速率 r=lim log₂(R_T)/T，初始熵速率 s=lim S(ω_T)/T。证明手段结合了熵逆定理（entropy converses）、与浴维度无关的支撑修复（support repair），以及仅编码器全量子 Slepian–Wolf 回收的封闭自适应实现。关键量 κ 为平滑独立参考扩展成本，其零误差极限先于对满秩输入取上确界；固定输入形式是零泄漏量子隐私漏斗（privacy funnel）的仿射变换。

Result: 可达区域恰为 s≥0, r+s≥h, r−s≥κ，其中 h 为最大熵交换。最小维度速率为 (h+κ)/2。所有种子、时钟、工作空间和保留残余均被计数。给出退相位、纯替换以及满足 0<κ<h 的量子比特信道等实例。

Conclusion: 论文精确给出了封闭量子器件中重复使用信道所需的浴资源可达区域与最小维度速率，并指出未声称 κ 的可计算性或高效电路综合。

Abstract: We characterize the bath resources needed to supply repeated uses of a fixed finite-dimensional quantum channel in a closed device. For each horizon $T$, one bath, one initial state and one repeated unitary are fixed before the user. Each output is returned before the next input arrives; no reset, discard, fresh ancilla or uncounted controller is available. Approximation error must vanish against arbitrary adaptive users with quantum memory and references. Writing $r=\lim \log_2(R_T)/T$ for the bath dimension rate and $s=\lim S(ω_T)/T$ for the actual initial entropy rate, we prove that the achievable region is exactly $s\ge 0$, $r+s\ge h$ and $r-s\geκ$. Here $h$ is maximum entropy exchange and $κ$ is a smoothed independent-reference extension cost, with the zero-error limit taken before the supremum over full-rank inputs. Its exact fixed-input form is an affine transform of the zero-leakage quantum privacy funnel. The minimum dimension rate is $(h+κ)/2$. The proof combines entropy converses, a bath-dimension-independent support repair, and a closed adaptive implementation of encoder-only fully quantum Slepian--Wolf recycling.
