All seeds, clocks, workspace and retained residues are counted. Worked examples include dephasing, pure replacement and a qubit channel with $0<κ<h$. No computability of $κ$ or efficient circuit synthesis is claimed.

</details>


### [187] [Optical Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18347)
*Hamza Hasnaoui,Leonardo Limongi,Taira Giordani,Beatrice Polacchi,Alberto Quaranta,Martino Bernard,Fabio Sciarrino,Mirko Lobino*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了光学量子计算的主要实现方案，按编码方式与计算方式（门模型、簇态）分类，并用表格总结实验结果、优势与挑战，同时专章介绍玻色采样、变分量子本征求解器与量子机器学习等非通用量子计算应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学量子计算领域包含多种协议与实验，使用光的量子特性以取得相对经典计算机的计算优势，但方案分散、编码与计算方式各异，需要一个系统的梳理以便读者全面了解该领域。

Method: 采用综述方法，按编码类型（如偏振、路径、连续变量等）与计算执行方式（基于量子门或基于簇态）对主要实现协议进行分类描述，逐一分析其优势与挑战，并以表格汇总已有实验结果；另设专节讨论非通用量子计算应用。

Result: 提供了各主要光学量子计算协议的优缺点与实验进展概览，并以表格形式加以总结；同时涵盖玻色采样、变分量子本征求解器和量子机器学习等应用。

Conclusion: 该综述旨在为读者提供最全面的概览，展示光子及其量子特性在计算中发挥关键作用的各类应用及其现状。

Abstract: Under the label of optical quantum computing, there are a variety of protocols and experiments that use the quantum properties of light to achieve a computational advantage over classical computing machines. In this review, we describe some of the main implementations, which differ in the type of encoding and in how the computation is performed, whether using gates or cluster states. For each protocol, we describe advantages and challenges with an overview of the experimental results obtained, summarized in tables. Because of the great relevance achieved in this field, there is a section dedicated to non-universal quantum computation with photons, where boson sampling, variational quantum eigensolvers, and quantum machine learning applications are described. The aim is to give the reader the broadest overview of the applications where photons and their quantum properties play a key role in computation.

</details>


### [188] [Signatures of Chaos in a Quasiperiodically Driven Quantum Impact Oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18350)
*Deepshikha Singh,Titir Mukherjee,Soumitro Banerjee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究准周期驱动撞击振子在擦边条件下的量子混沌动力学，证明准周期驱动能产生稳健的混沌特征，并揭示频率比的有理性主要影响全局扰乱的后续演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对周期驱动量子撞击振子的研究仅报告了奇异非混沌动力学（strange nonchaotic dynamics），缺乏对量子混沌产生机制的认识。作者希望探究准周期驱动是否能突破这一限制，在擦边条件下诱导量子混沌。

Method: 采用擦边条件下的准周期驱动撞击振子模型。经典层面使用分岔分析、Lyapunov指数、Fourier谱和0-1检验刻画从准周期运动到混沌的转变；量子层面采用Shannon熵时间序列、0-1检验、有限时间Lyapunov指数、非时序关联函数（OTOC）和保真度作为诊断工具，分别考察有理与无理驱动频率比（含黄金比驱动）。

Result: 经典系统在擦边处发生从准周期运动到混沌的急剧转变。量子系统中，Shannon熵时间序列呈现宽带谱，0-1检验值接近1，有限时间Lyapunov指数对有理和无理频率比均以正值为主。OTOC在早期呈近指数增长且速率几乎一致，黄金比驱动下OTOC饱和显著更快；保真度呈近似与扰动强度无关的指数衰减。

Conclusion: 准周期驱动结合擦边条件可作为产生量子混沌行为的有效机制。初始扰乱的发生对频率比的有理性基本不敏感，但全局扰乱的后续发展与驱动的无理性程度密切相关。

Abstract: We demonstrate the emergence of quantum-chaotic dynamics in a quasiperiodically driven impact oscillator near the grazing condition. While previous studies of the quantum impact oscillator under periodic driving reported strange nonchaotic dynamics, we show that quasiperiodic driving produces robust signatures of chaos. The corresponding classical system undergoes a sharp grazing-induced transition from quasiperiodic motion to chaos, as established by bifurcation analysis, Lyapunov exponents, Fourier spectra, and the 0-1 test. In the quantum system, Shannon-entropy time series exhibit broadband spectra, 0-1 test values close to unity, and predominantly positive finite-time Lyapunov exponents for both rational and irrational driving-frequency ratios. Independent quantum diagnostics reinforce this result: the out-of-time-order correlator (OTOC) displays early-time exponential growth with nearly identical rates, while the golden-ratio drive leads to a substantially faster saturation of the OTOC. Fidelity exhibits an exponential decay regime that is approximately independent of perturbation strength. These results establish quasiperiodic driving near grazing as a mechanism for generating quantum-chaotic behavior and reveal that, although the initial onset of scrambling is largely insensitive to frequency-ratio rationality, the subsequent development of global scrambling is strongly influenced by the degree of irrationality of the drive.

</details>


### [189] [Benchmarking indirect quantum control schemes via higher-order quantum operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18409)
*Simon Vedl,Varun Srivastava,Riddhi Ghosh,Alexei Gilchrist*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文用高阶量子操作（确定性超仪器）刻画有限步间接量子控制协议，将最优控制问题写成半定规划，给出可控操作序列的性能基准，并以两步量子比特纯化任务验证框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间接量子控制通过操纵与目标系统相互作用的辅助控制器来间接影响目标系统，但缺乏对"有限步控制操作所能达到的最优性能"的系统性刻画与操作基准，也难以公平比较不同受限控制类别的价值。

Method: 以高阶量子操作的数学语言形式化有限步间接控制协议，把允许的控制器操作表示为确定性超仪器（deterministic superinstruments），从而将不可控但固定的动力学与可控操作分离；针对选定品质因数，将最优控制问题表述为半定规划（SDP）并求解全局最优。

Result: SDP最优解给出有限步控制序列（含经典或量子前馈协议）可达最优性能的操作基准；据此可比较更受限且实验可行的控制类，如独立酉控制或无记忆量子信道；在两步量子比特纯化任务中，存在简单酉策略达到高阶基准的区间，也存在可证明次优的区间。

Conclusion: 该框架为间接量子控制方案中控制资源的价值提供了系统化的量化研究途径，能够区分简单策略何时最优、何时次优。

Abstract: Indirect quantum control aims to manipulate a target system through interventions on an auxiliary controller with which it interacts. We formulate finite-step indirect-control protocols using the language of higher-order quantum operations, representing admissible controller manipulations as deterministic superinstruments. This representation separates the uncontrolled but fixed dynamics from the controllable operations and allows the optimal control problem to be written as a semidefinite program for a chosen figure of merit. The resulting optimum gives an operational benchmark by providing the best performance achievable by a finite sequence of control operations, including protocols with classical or quantum feed-forward. More restricted and experimentally motivated control classes, such as independent unitary controls or memoryless quantum channels, can then be compared against this benchmark. We illustrate the framework with a two-step qubit-purification task, in which an initially mixed target qubit is steered toward a pure state through a fixed interaction with a controller qubit. The example shows regimes where simple unitary strategies saturate the higher-order benchmark, as well as regimes where they are provably suboptimal. This approach provides a systematic way to study the value of control resources in indirect quantum-control schemes.

</details>


### [190] [A Programmable Rydberg Quantum Bus for Nonlocal Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18447)
*X. Jin,F. Yang,Weibin Li,X. Q. Shao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用里德堡原子链作为相干量子总线，将局域连接的一维中性原子架构转化为有效非局域相互作用网络，实现准全对全耦合的可编程量子节点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展量子网络需要具备灵活内部连接的处理节点，但中性原子架构受限于里德堡相互作用强烈的空间依赖性，缺乏可控的非局域连接能力。

Method: 利用色散区虚激发介导空间分离的数据单元间的可控相互作用，采用格林函数连分式方法进行解析推导，并结合全局长程里德堡相互作用、原子位置涨落及有限里德堡态寿命的数值模拟进行验证。

Result: 该机制不局限于单激发动力学，支持多种功能：Floquet工程手性输运、机械振子的远程纠缠以及选择性抑制不需要的偶极交换的破坏性干涉；模拟表明在实验相关条件下介导动力学保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 里德堡链可作为可编程相干中介，扩展中性原子量子节点的内部连接性，向准全对全耦合迈进，为可扩展和可重构的量子网络架构提供硬件层面的实现路径。

Abstract: Scalable quantum networks require processing nodes with flexible internal connectivity, yet neutral-atom architectures remain constrained by the strong spatial dependence of native Rydberg interactions. Here we show that a Rydberg atom chain can act as a coherent quantum bus, converting a locally connected one-dimensional architecture into an effectively nonlocal interaction network. Virtual excitations in the dispersive regime mediate controllable interactions between spatially separated data units, which we derive analytically using a Green's-function continued-fraction method. The resulting mechanism is not restricted to single-excitation dynamics and supports several distinct functionalities, including Floquet-engineered chiral transport, remote entanglement of mechanical oscillators, and destructive interference for selectively suppressing unwanted dipole exchange. Simulations incorporating full long-range Rydberg interactions, atomic position fluctuations, and finite Rydberg-state lifetimes show that the mediated dynamics remain robust under experimentally relevant conditions. These results establish Rydberg chains as programmable coherent mediators for extending the internal connectivity of neutral-atom quantum nodes toward quasi-all-to-all coupling, providing a hardware-level route toward scalable and reconfigurable quantum-network architectures.

</details>


### [191] [Simplification Rules for Continuous-Time Quantum Walks on Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18463)
*Mostafa Atallah,Daniel Dilley,Jishnu Mahmud,Zain H Saleem,Rebekah Herrman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出标准单量子比特门的连续时间量子游走（CTQW）实现，并提出新的图重写规则，用于缩短动态图序列、保持所实现酉变换不变，作为电路模型与动态图框架间转换的转译原语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态图上的CTQW通过哈密顿量序列实现量子门，但朴素构造会产生冗长且有冗余的序列，需要类似门模型电路恒等式的简化手段。

Method: 给出标准单量子比特门在CTQW中的实现，引入新的图重写规则（图重写规则可在保持所实现酉变换不变的前提下缩短动态图序列），并通过具体的电路约简示例及转译原语应用说明其效果。

Result: 提出的图重写规则能够通过具体电路约简示例实现门序列的简化（缩短动态图序列），并说明其可作为电路模型与动态图框架之间转换的转译原语。

Conclusion: CTQW的图重写规则相当于门模型中的电路恒等式，能够消除动态图序列冗余，为两个框架间的转译提供基础原语。

Abstract: Continuous-time quantum walks (CTQWs) on dynamic graphs realize quantum gates as sequences of time-evolving graph Hamiltonians, but naive constructions produce long sequences with redundancy. Simplification rules, which are graph rewrite rules that shorten a dynamic graph sequence while preserving the unitary it implements, are the CTQW analogue of circuit identities in the gate model. In this work, we give CTQW realizations of the standard single-qubit gates and introduce new graph rewrite rules. We demonstrate the simplification rules through worked circuit reductions and outline their use as transpilation primitives for converting between the circuit model and the dynamic graph framework.

</details>


### [192] [Programmable Hong--Ou--Mandel interference in a giant-atom beam splitter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18530)
*Ruolin Chai,Lei Du,Guoqing Cai,Alejandro Vivas-Viaña,Anton Frisk Kockum,Yong Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将耦合到两个波导（各含两个耦合点）的巨原子（GA）用作可编程的HOM干涉仪，通过调节耦合相位差实现单光子分束和双光子干涉的连续调控，并可用于量子参数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hong-Ou-Mandel（HOM）效应是双光子量子干涉的标志性现象，但传统实现方式（分束器）功能固定，缺乏对分束比和干涉统计的可编程调控。作者希望利用波导量子电动力学中巨原子的独特散射特性，构建一个可连续调控的双光子干涉平台，并拓展其在量子信息、通信与传感中的应用。

Method: 作者考虑一个巨原子分别通过两个耦合点与两条波导耦合，利用耦合相位差调控其自干涉与方向性，从而控制散射响应。分析了从不同波导注入的两个高斯型单光子波包在该GA上的散射过程，计算四个输出端口的聚束与反聚束（符合计数）概率，并借助二元符合测量的经典Fisher信息量化耦合相位微小偏离的可估计灵敏度。

Result: 当GA作为单光子的有效50:50分束器时，随着两波包相对输入延迟变化，观察到明显的HOM凹陷（dip）。偏离该工作点时，连续调节耦合相位可调控双光子输出统计，使其在聚束（进入同一输出端口）与反聚束（分布于不同输出端口）之间转换。此外，可通过双光子输出统计估计耦合相位的微小偏差，并用经典Fisher信息量化其灵敏度。

Conclusion: 巨原子分束器为波导量子电动力学中的双光子干涉提供了可编程平台，在量子信息处理、量子通信和量子传感等领域具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: The Hong--Ou--Mandel (HOM) effect is a hallmark of two-photon quantum interference, in which two indistinguishable photons impinging on a balanced beam splitter bunch into the same output port. Here, we show that a giant atom (GA), coupled to two waveguides through two coupling points each, can function as a programmable HOM interferometer, enabling continuous control over single-photon beam splitting and two-photon interference. This programmability arises from coupling-phase differences in the GA, which tune its self-interference and directionality, and thereby its scattering response. To characterize the two-photon interference, we analyze the scattering of two Gaussian single-photon wave packets injected through different waveguides and evaluate the bunching and antibunching (coincidence) probabilities of the resulting four output ports. At the operating point where the GA acts as an effective 50:50 beam splitter for single photons, we observe a pronounced HOM dip as the relative input delay between the two wave packets is varied. Away from this point, adjusting the coupling phases continuously tunes the two-photon output statistics between bunching into the same output port and antibunching across distinct output ports. In addition, we explore an application to quantum parameter estimation, showing that small deviations of coupling phases can be estimated from the two-photon output statistics, with the achievable sensitivity quantified by the classical Fisher information associated with a binary coincidence measurement. Our giant-atom beam splitter thus provides a programmable platform for two-photon interference in waveguide quantum electrodynamics, with potential applications in quantum information processing, quantum communication, and quantum sensing.

</details>


### [193] [Giant-atom-mediated photon blockade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18547)
*C. Cui,W. Y. Hu,Y. Q. Ji,H. T. Cui,Yan-Hui Zhou,H. Z. Shen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出基于巨原子介导的双腔与三腔光子阻塞方案，利用巨原子空间延展导致的耦合相位与耦合强度调控，在多腔中同时实现非常规光子阻塞(UPB)、常规光子阻塞(CPB)与双光子阻塞(2PB)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子阻塞（输出为单光子）在点原子系统中研究充分，但在巨原子系统中鲜有探索；且点原子系统因存在单一路径腔而难以实现多腔同时非常规光子阻塞，因此需要借助巨原子的多腔耦合特性加以突破。

Method: 构建巨原子介导的双腔与三腔系统，驱动场施加于第一个腔；对比点原子情形（原子仅耦合最左腔），利用巨原子空间延展实现与多个腔耦合，引入相位与耦合强度以形成多路径破坏性干涉；并通过调节失谐量实现常规光子阻塞；最后分析点原子多腔系统中的双光子阻塞。

Result: 点原子系统中因总存在单一路径腔而无法实现多腔同时UPB；巨原子系统凭借多腔耦合引入的相位与耦合强度，通过多路径破坏性干涉可同时实现多腔UPB；调节失谐量后可观察到多腔同时CPB；并在点原子多腔系统中研究了同时2PB。

Conclusion: 巨原子的空间延展特性为多腔同时光子阻塞（UPB、CPB）提供可行机制，突破了点原子单路径限制，并拓展至双光子阻塞情形。

Abstract: Photon blockade is a phenomenon where the presence of system nonlinearity causes the output to consist of single photons, which has been extensively studied in point atom systems, but it is barely explored in giant atom ones. In this paper, we propose giant atom-mediated photon blockade scheme based on two cavities and three cavities systems with driving field applied to the first cavity. We show that simultaneous unconventional photon blockades (UPBs) can not occur in the point atom system (the atom coupling only to the leftmost cavity) due to there always existing a cavity to have a single path. In contrast, the spatially extended nature of giant atom enables coupling to multiple cavities and allows for the introduction of a phase and coupling strength. Consequently, simultaneous UPBs in multiple cavities can be obtained due to the multipath destructive interference. Moreover, by manipulating the detuning, we observe simultaneous conventional photon blockades (CPBs) in multiple cavities. Finally, we study simultaneous two-photon blockades (2PBs) in point atom multiple cavities system.

</details>


### [194] [Information Geometry of Four-Parameter Single-Qutrit States: From Quantum to Semiclassical Geometric Tensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18558)
*Xue-xiang Xu,Kai-xu Cai,Xue-feng Zhan,Hong-chun Yuan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文对一类纯四参数单qutrit态进行信息几何研究，推导CFIM、QGT与SCGT的闭式表达，揭示SCGT在投影极限下还原QGT、在平凡极限下消失，并用测量依赖的间隙量化其相对QGT的损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典与量子Fisher信息矩阵之间的差距区分了可通过测量获取的信息与量子态内禀信息，而在多参数情形下这一量子阻碍一般无法饱和。作者受近期提出的半经典几何张量（SCGT）启发，希望明确实际测量如何读取并部分退化量子态的几何内容。

Method: 针对一类纯四参数单qutrit态（FPSQSs），采用在无信息POVM与锐投影测量之间插值的单参数测量族进行探测，并推导CFIM、量子几何张量（QGT）与SCGT的闭式表达式。

Result: SCGT在投影极限下还原QGT，在平凡极限下消失；其实部分解为CFIM与相位扇区中额外的非负测量传递度规之和，虚部给出半经典Berry曲率；相对QGT的损失由测量依赖的间隙量化。

Conclusion: 该工作为半经典几何框架提供了精确可解的四参数平台，阐明了现实测量如何读出量子态的几何内容，以及测量如何部分地使其退化。

Abstract: The gap between the classical and quantum Fisher information matrices (CFIM and QFIM) separates what is operationally accessible through measurements from what is intrinsic to a quantum state. In the multiparameter setting, this quantum obstruction is generically not saturable. Motivated by the recently introduced semiclassical geometric tensor (SCGT), we perform an explicit information-geometric study for a class of pure four-parameter single-qutrit states (FPSQSs). These states are probed by a one-parameter family of measurements that interpolates between an uninformative POVM and a sharp projective measurement. We derive closed-form expressions for the CFIM, the quantum geometric tensor (QGT), and the SCGT. We show that the SCGT reproduces the QGT in the projective limit and vanishes in the trivial limit. The real part of the SCGT splits into two terms: the CFIM and an additional nonnegative measurement-transmitted metric in the phase sector. Its imaginary part provides a semiclassical Berry curvature. Its loss relative to the QGT is quantified by a measurement-dependent gap. Our results provide an exactly solvable four-parameter platform for the semiclassical geometric framework. They also clarify how realistic measurements read out the geometric content of quantum states, and how they partially degrade it.

</details>


### [195] [Extensibly Causally Separable Processes Admit Realizations as Quantum Circuits with Classical Control of Causal Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18559)
*Wenjie Wei,Shengshi Pang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了可扩展因果可分（ECS）过程与经典因果序控制量子电路（QC-CC）之间的等价性，解决了长期悬而未决的公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不定因果序在过程矩阵框架下由因果非可分性刻画，而ECS要求过程在任意输入辅助扩展下可递归分解。已知QC-CC能生成ECS过程，但"是否每个ECS过程都能如此实现"这一逆命题长期未决，论文旨在填补该缺口并给出ECS的精确操作解释。

Method: 通过一个广义隐形传态构造，将任意多分体ECS过程实现为QC-CC电路；同时论证平凡与非平凡全局过去/未来系统下ECS定义的一致性。

Result: 证明每个多分体ECS过程都可实现为QC-CC，从而确立ECS与QC-CC的等价；并建立不同全局过去/未来系统情境下ECS定义的一致性。

Conclusion: ECS获得了精确的操作解释，QC-CC被确认为该类过程对应的完备电路结构。

Abstract: Indefinite causal order extends the conventional circuit paradigm by allowing local quantum operations to be connected without a predetermined global order. Within the process-matrix framework, it is characterized by causal nonseparability, whereas extensible causal separability (ECS) requires that, under arbitrary input-ancilla extensions, a process admit a recursive decomposition into components, each compatible with a particular operation acting first. Quantum circuits with classical control of causal order (QC-CC), in which previous outcomes dynamically determine which operation acts next, are known to generate ECS processes, but whether every ECS process admits such a realization has remained a longstanding open problem. We settle it through a generalized teleportation construction that realizes every multipartite ECS process as a QC-CC. We further establish consistency between ECS definitions for trivial and nontrivial global past and future systems. These results provide ECS with an exact operational interpretation and identify QC-CC as the complete circuit structure underlying this class of processes.

</details>


### [196] [Variational Quantum Transformer Architecture for Synthetic Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18565)
*Julian Hager,Michael Kölle,Gerhard Stenzel,Tobias Rohe,Jonas Stein,Claudia Linnhoff-Popien*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向NISQ设备的紧凑量子Transformer架构，用于合成QNLP序列建模，保留自回归下一词接口但用量子变分模块替换注意力与前馈层，在语法生成任务上验证其可训练性与有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近term量子硬件（NISQ）资源受限，现有量子NLP模型难以兼容经典Transformer的序列建模范式；作者希望在量子约束下探索一种可端到端训练、保留自回归接口的Transformer启发式QNLP架构，而非追求量子优势。

Method: 提出紧凑NISQ兼容量子Transformer：保留经典Transformer的自回归下一词接口，但将注意力与ffn子层替换为变分量子编码块、连接电路、解码块和双量子比特直接测量读出；token上下文经角度编码进入小量子寄存器，由并行变分头和编码整合电路处理，再经解码辅助比特条件化，输出四词词表分布；在确定性与字典序语法生成任务上与紧凑经典Transformer基线对比多种架构变体。

Result: 量子模型可端到端训练并学到非平凡语法结构：个别运行实现完美确定性生成，最强变体在字典序任务上具有高有效率；但经典基线在准确性和稳定性上更优，量子模型对初始化敏感。

Conclusion: 论文贡献不在于宣称量子优势，而是在NISQ约束下给出一种具体的Transformer启发式QNLP序列建模架构与评估，验证了此类混合量子架构在语法生成上的可行性及当前局限。

Abstract: We propose a compact NISQ-compatible quantum transformer architecture for synthetic QNLP sequence modelling. The model preserves the autoregressive next-token interface of a classical transformer, but replaces attention and feed-forward sublayers with variational quantum encoder blocks, connector circuits, decoder blocks and a direct two-qubit measurement readout. Token contexts are angle-encoded into small quantum registers, processed by parallel variational heads and encoder integration circuits and conditioned through decoder ancillae to produce a distribution over a four-token vocabulary. We evaluate several architecture variants on deterministic and lexicographic grammar-generation tasks against a compact classical transformer baseline. The quantum models are trainable end-to-end and learn nontrivial grammar structure, including perfect deterministic generation in individual runs and high lexicographic validity in the strongest variant. The classical baseline remains more accurate and stable and the quantum models are sensitive to initialization. The contribution is therefore not a claim of quantum advantage, but a concrete architecture and evaluation of transformer-inspired QNLP sequence modelling under near-term quantum constraints.

</details>


### [197] [Catalytic Activation of Genuine Multipartite Entanglement and Nonlocality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18570)
*Eliot Donnadieu,Pavel Sekatski,Nicolas Brunner,Victor Barizien*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明在量子催化框架下，任何非分划可分的双可分态都可确定性转化为真正的多体纠缠（GME）态，并提出"和到积"催化协议，在随机网络纠缠态上实现了GME与真正多体Bell非局域性的催化激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真正的多体纠缠（GME）是多体态中最强形式的纠缠，通常难以直接制备或激活；而量子催化提供了一种在不消耗催化剂的前提下实现态转化的可能。作者希望探究能否借助催化将本不具备GME的双可分态转化为GME态，并扩展到多体情形与Bell非局域性激活。

Method: 作者在量子催化框架下，考虑仅使用局域操作、无需经典通信（即无经典通信的局域操作加催化）的转化过程。证明：任何非分划可分的双可分态均可被确定性地转化为GME态。为此构造了面向多体情形的催化协议——"和到积"（sum-to-product），该协议以预示（heralded）方式将若干态的混合转化为其张量积。随后将该协议应用于按构造即为双可分的随机网络纠缠态。

Result: 理论上证明了双可分态（非分划可分）在催化辅助下可确定性激活GME。所提出的"和到积"协议成功将混合态以预示方式转化为张量积形式。在随机网络纠缠态上，该协议实现了GME的催化激活，并进一步实现了真正多体Bell非局域性的催化激活。

Conclusion: 量子催化可以将不具有GME的双可分态确定性地转化为GME态，扩展了催化在纠缠理论中的适用范围；"和到积"协议为多体纠缠与真正多体Bell非局域性的激活提供了通用工具，并可用于网络纠缠态场景。

Abstract: We demonstrate the possibility to activate genuine multipartite entanglement (GME), the strongest form of entanglement for multipartite states, within the framework of quantum catalysis. Specifically, we show that any biseparable state (i.e. not GME) that is not partition separable can be deterministically transformed into a GME state via the help of a catalyst and local operations, without any classical communication. In turn, we construct a catalytic protocol tailored to the multipartite case. The protocol is termed "sum-to-product", as it transforms a mixture of states into their tensor product in a heralded manner. We apply this protocol to random network entangled states, which are biseparable by construction, and demonstrate catalytic activation of both GME and genuine multipartite Bell nonlocality.

</details>


### [198] [Filter-Free Indistinguishable Photon Generation from Continuous-Wave-Driven Integrated Microresonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18575)
*Ruiyang Chen,Sicheng Zeng,Yuan Chen,Sanli Huang,Zeying Zhong,Zhen Chen,Xue Bai,Yi-Han Luo,Junqiu Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作指出连续波驱动微腔光子对的HOM干涉可见度并非由联合谱振幅(JSA)单独决定，而是由检测端条件化的预示态(fored state)所支配；通过独立优化闲频光与信号光的检测时间窗口，可在无需光谱滤波或复杂源工程的情况下，同时实现接近1的预示光子不可分辨性与内在预示效率，并在高Q氮化硅微腔上获得0.992(8)与0.942(12)的HOM可见度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络需要兼具窄线宽、高效率与高不可分辨性的可扩展光子源。微腔光子对源是有力候选，但基于JSA的"仅源"描述预测：由独立的、连续波驱动的微腔所产生的光子之间，HOM干涉可见度接近零，这与实际观测相矛盾，说明现有理论描述不完整，需要重新厘清决定HOM干涉的物理因素。

Method: 将有限时间探测(finite-time detection)与腔增强自发四波混频(cavity-enhanced SFWM)相结合，刻画由闲频光子探测窗口所支配的、检测器条件化的预示态(detector-conditioned heralded state)；独立优化闲频与信号检测时间窗口，以同时调控预示光子不可分辨性与内在预示效率；在集成高Q氮化硅(Si3N4)微谐振器上开展HOM干涉实验，且不进行背景扣除。

Result: 在无背景扣除条件下，四重符合计数率分别为4.5(3) Hz与12.2(6) Hz时，测得HOM干涉可见度分别为0.992(8)与0.942(12)；预示光子不可分辨性与内在预示效率均可趋近于1，且无需光谱滤波或复杂源工程。

Conclusion: 微腔光子对的HOM干涉不由JSA单独决定，而由探测窗口条件化的预示态决定；仅通过优化探测时间窗口即可逼近单位可见度与单位内在预示效率。该结果确立了CW驱动高Q微谐振器作为量子网络基本功能单元(primitive)的稳健且可扩展的平台地位。

Abstract: Quantum networks require scalable photon sources combining narrow linewidth, high efficiency, and high indistinguishability. Microresonator photon-pair sources are promising candidates, yet a source-only description based on the joint spectral amplitude (JSA) predicts near-zero Hong-Ou-Mandel (HOM) interference visibility between photons generated by independent, CW-driven microresonators. In this work, we show that the observed HOM interference is not determined by the JSA alone. By combining finite-time detection with cavity-enhanced spontaneous four-wave mixing, we characterize a detector-conditioned heralded state governed by the idler-photon detection window. We further demonstrate that independently optimizing the idler and signal detection windows allows both heralded-photon indistinguishability and intrinsic heralding efficiency to approach unity, without spectral filtering or complex source engineering. Utilizing integrated high-$Q$ silicon nitride microresonators, we achieve HOM visibilities of 0.992(8) and 0.942(12), without background subtraction, at fourfold count rates of 4.5(3) and 12.2(6) Hz, respectively. Our work establishes CW-driven high-$Q$ microresonators as a robust and scalable platform for quantum-network primitives.

</details>


### [199] [From Reversible Quantum Dynamics to Statistical Probability: A dynamical solution to the origin of probability and Hilbert's sixth problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18593)
*Wei-Min Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于精确开放量子系统理论，构建了从可逆量子动力学到约化统计（热化）的严格动力学路径，为希尔伯特第六问题提供了可检验的动力学解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希尔伯特第六问题要求阐明可逆、确定性的力学动力学如何产生不可逆的统计概率与热力学。作者希望在开放量子系统框架下，给出从可逆量子动力学到约化统计的精确、可检验的动力学路径。

Method: 针对与任意环境双线性耦合的二次玻色子与费米子系统，推导由耗散传播子 u 和涨落关联函数 v 完全决定的精确约化密度算符；考察连续谱密度（无束缚态极点）与带隙谱密度（含局域束缚态）两种情形；并去掉初始热混合环境的假设，从系统与环境纯态的直积出发研究幺正演化产生的纠缠与准吉布斯态，再取环境自由度趋于无穷的极限。

Result: 对无束缚态极点的连续谱密度，u 在长时极限下消失，系统初态信息完全丢失，约化密度算符趋于热吉布斯态；带隙谱密度中的局域束缚态则保留长时记忆、阻止热化。从纯态直积出发时，幺正演化先产生系统-环境纠缠及有限环境下的周期性准吉布斯约化态；当环境自由度趋于无穷、谱变为连续时，回归消失，精确约化密度算符收敛于热吉布斯态。总熵保持为零，系统熵成为纠缠熵并达到最大。

Conclusion: 概率并非强加于幺正动力学的额外随机公设，而是复合系统中开放系统的客观统计结构。该结果给出了确定性到统计性转变的精确、可检验的动力学解。

Abstract: Hilbert's sixth problem placed probability and mechanics at the center of the axiomatization of physics. It asks how irreversible statistical probability and thermodynamics can produce from the reversible, deterministic dynamics of a system. Building on an exact open quantum system theory developed in the past two decades, we construct a dynamical route from the reversible quantum dynamics to reduced statistics. For quadratic bosonic and fermionic systems bilinearly coupled to arbitrary environments, we derive the exact reduced density operator determined completely by a dissipative propagator u and a fluctuation correlation function v of the system. For continuous spectral densities with no bound-state pole, u vanishes in the long-time limit, information about the system initial states is lost completely, and the reduced density operator of systems approaches a thermal Gibbs state. Gapped spectral densities with localized bound states instead preserve long-time memory and prevent thermalization. We then remove the usual assumption of an initially thermal, mixed environment, start from a product of pure states for the system and environment, unitary evolution first generates system-environment entanglement and a periodic quasi-Gibbs reduced state for a finite environment. When the number of environmental degrees of freedom tends to infinity, the spectrum becomes continuous, the recurrence disappears, the exact reduced density operator converges to a thermal Gibbs state. The total entropy remains zero, whereas the system entropy becomes entanglement entropy and reaches its maximum. Probability is therefore not an additional random postulate imposed on unitary dynamics, it is the objective statistical structure of an open system in a composite. These results provide an exact, testable dynamical solution of the determinism to statistics.

</details>


### [200] [Resonance-Protected Pointer States: Stationary-Phase Analysis of Entanglement in an Environment-Coupled Two-Spin System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18641)
*Kentaro Urasaki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究两个自旋-1/2系统分别非对易耦合静态环境场、外加自哈密顿量并互相通过σ_y^(1)σ_y^(2)耦合的动力学，利用宇称对称性精确解与鞍点近似证明：普通孤立驻点相干按t^{-1}衰减，而在两自旋局域有效场相互抵消的共振条件下，仅Bell型最大纠缠态对的相干存活并以t^{-1/2}异常慢幂律衰减；进一步指出该系统的pointer观测量由两环境间的统计共振条件决定，而非个体系统-环境耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Zurek指针基理论（1981、2005模型）中"pointer观测量由局部系统-环境耦合决定"这一机制的普适性，探索存在系统间相互作用的开放量子系统中退相干与指针态的新机制。

Method: 构建利用宇称对称性的精确解；在环境场连续极限下应用驻点近似（鞍点法）；通过对相干项进行渐近分析；并与Zurek等人的指针基理论进行对比。

Result: 普通孤立驻点对应相干按t^{-1}衰减；在局域有效场抵消的共振条件下，仅Bell型最大纠缠态（系统间耦合本征基）之间的相干存活，并以t^{-1/2}的异常慢幂律衰减。指针观测量由两环境间的统计共振条件决定，不能还原为局部耦合。

Conclusion: 约化出一种不可还原为局部耦合的退相干机制：pointer可观测量取决于两个环境间的统计共振条件，而非单个系统-环境耦合，扩展了Zurek指针基理论在含系统间相互作用情形下的适用范围。

Abstract: In this manuscript, we analyze the dynamics of a model in which two spin-1/2 systems are each coupled noncommutingly to a static environmental field and to a self-Hamiltonian, while also being mutually coupled through a $σ_y^{(1)}σ_y^{(2)}$ interaction. By constructing an exact solution that exploits parity symmetry, and by applying the stationary-phase approximation (the saddle-point method) in the continuum limit of the environmental field, we show that coherence associated with the ordinary isolated stationary point decays as $t^{-1}$, whereas, under the resonance condition at which the local effective fields of the two spins cancel each other, only the coherence between a pair of maximally entangled, Bell-type states- the eigenbasis of the inter-system coupling-survives, decaying via the anomalously slow power law $t^{-1/2}$. Through a comparison with the pointer-basis theory of W. H. Zurek and coworkers (the 1981 and 2005 models), we further reveal a mechanism not reducible to local coupling: in this system, the pointer observable is determined not by the individual system-environment couplings but by a statistical resonance condition between the two environments.

</details>


### [201] [Impact of a CSS quantum error correction code in underwater quantum key distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18666)
*Juliette Florin,Nicolas Le Josse,Arnaud Coatanhay,Gilles Burel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文评估两种四量子比特CSS量子纠错码在水下垂直BB84 QKD中缓解光子损耗与环境噪声的效果，发现QECC仅在信噪比边际区域有益，高SNR时原始BB84更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下量子密钥分发对海事基础设施安全通信至关重要，但水下光信道存在严重的光子损耗（擦除）和环境噪声，会显著降低QKD性能，因此需要研究量子纠错码能否缓解这些损伤。

Method: 建立包含光子损耗、几何扩散和太阳噪声的综合随机信道模型；在垂直水下通信BB84协议中，分析计算有无安全条件下的量子比特误码率（QBER）与安全密钥率（SKR）随信噪比（SNR）的变化，并用蒙特卡洛仿真验证；对比两种四量子比特CSS码（标准码与discard变体）的性能。

Result: 标准四量子比特CSS码在QBER 11%安全阈值处获得3 dB的SNR增益，discard变体获得4.5 dB；但QECC的编码开销会降低SKR。分析揭示了SKR与发送光子到达概率之间的关键关系。QECC仅在边际SNR区域有益，高SNR时原始BB84占优。在太阳位于大气层顶部的海洋Type III Jerlov水场景下，识别出QECC可实现原本不可行通信的工作深度窗口。

Conclusion: 纠错部署必须依赖具体场景：在SNR边际时以吞吐量为代价扩展工作距离，在高SNR时优先保证密钥生成速率。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) enables secure underwater communications essential for maritime infrastructure. Underwater optical channels introduce substantial photon loss (erasures) and ambient noise that degrade QKD performance. This paper investigates whether two four-qubit Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) quantum error correction codes (QECC) mitigate these impairments in vertical underwater communication BB84 QKD protocol. After developing a comprehensive stochastic channel model incorporating photon loss, geometric spreading, and solar noise, we assess the viability of QECC through the analytical study of the quantum bit error rate (QBER) and the secure key rate (SKR) with and without security depending on the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) validated against Monte Carlo simulations. The standard four-qubit CSS code achieves a 3 dB SNR gain at the QBER 11% security threshold; the discard code variant achieves 4.5 dB. However, QECC includes an encoding overhead that reduces the SKR. We demonstrate a crucial relationship between the SKR and the probability of arrival of the sent photon. Analysis shows that QECC is beneficial exclusively in marginal SNR regimes; at high SNR, raw BB84 dominates. For an ocean Type III Jerlov water scenario with sunlight coming from the sun located at the top of the atmosphere, we identify operational depth-range windows where QECC enables communication otherwise infeasible. This analysis establishes that error correction deployment must be scenario-dependent: extend operational range at the cost of throughput when SNR is marginal, or prioritize key generation rates at high SNR.

</details>


### [202] [Theory-agnostic nonclassicality certification in an integrated photonic circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18671)
*Vinicius P. Rossi,Emanuele Polino,Beatrice Polacchi,Valeria Cimini,David Schmid,John H. Selby,Giacomo Corrielli,Andrea Crespi,Roberto Osellame,Fabio Sciarrino,Ana Belén Sainz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在三模集成光子电路上实现了不依赖特定物理理论的广义语境性认证，方法结合理论无关层析与单纯形可嵌入性认证，实验数据排除了单纯形可嵌入性，从而给出理论无关的非经典性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非经典性认证通常依赖特定物理理论假设，这限制了器件无关协议的普适性。在可扩展架构中实现理论无关的认证，对于连接基础检验与新兴量子技术至关重要；集成光子电路是可扩展量子信息处理的领先平台，因此需要严格方法认证其非经典资源。

Method: 将理论无关层析（theory-agnostic tomography）与单纯形可嵌入性认证（simplex-embeddability certification）相结合，该框架不对底层物理理论作任何假设，并应用于由单光子驱动的三模光子电路的实验数据。同时独立构建该实验的量子模型，仅作为一致性检验，不作为认证的理论假设纳入。

Result: 该方法在实验数据上排除了单纯形可嵌入性（rules out simplex-embeddability），表明该光子电路展现出非经典性。

Conclusion: 研究提供了稳健的、理论无关的证据，证明三模集成光子电路存在广义语境性，为可扩展平台上的理论无关非经典性认证建立了可行途径。

Abstract: Certifying nonclassicality without assuming any particular underlying physical theory constitutes both a foundational challenge and a requirement for device-independent protocols. Realizing such theory-independent certification in scalable architectures is essential for connecting fundamental tests with emerging quantum technologies. Integrated photonic circuits are a leading platform for scalable quantum information processing, motivating the development of rigorous methods to certify the nonclassical resources underpinning quantum advantage. In this paper, we report a theory-independent certification of generalized contextuality on a three-mode integrated photonic circuit. Our approach combines theory-agnostic tomography with simplex-embeddability certification---a framework that requires no assumptions about the underlying physical theory---and applies it to experimental data from a three-mode photonic circuit seeded by single photons. An independently constructed quantum model of the experiment provides a consistency check on the observed nonclassicality, without entering the certification as a theoretical assumption. The method rules out simplex-embeddability for our experimental data, providing robust, theory-independent evidence that our photonic circuit exhibits nonclassicality.

</details>


### [203] [All coherent measurements provide observational ergotropic advantage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18735)
*Soumik Mahanti,Rakesh Saini,Alexei Gilchrist,Arindam Mitra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入一种可忠实地操作量化测量所提供功优势的度量，证明测量相干性是正优势的充要条件，并给出基于测量相干鲁棒性与$l_\infty$范数相干度量的上下界，最后从资源理论视角加以审视。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究确立了测量相干性对于从未知孤立量子系统中获取功优势的必要性，但该优势大小与既有测量相干性度量之间缺乏定量关系，本文旨在建立这一联系。

Method: 引入一个忠实的、操作性的功优势量化指标，据此分析测量相干性与正优势的关系，推导以测量相干鲁棒性和$l_\infty$范数相干度量为表征的上下界，并从资源理论角度考察该量化指标。

Result: 测量相干性是正优势的充要条件；获得了基于测量相干鲁棒性的上界与基于$l_\infty$范数相干度量的下界。

Conclusion: 结果为测量相干性作为功提取资源提供了操作性刻画，并揭示了其资源含量与热力学价值之间的非平凡关系。

Abstract: The use of quantum resources in the work extraction from a quantum system is an emerging research topic. Recently, [arXiv:2602.22893] established the necessity of measurement coherence for obtaining advantage in work extraction from an unknown isolated quantum system. However, a quantitative relation between the magnitude of this advantage and established measures of measurement coherence is missing. Here, we establish such a connection by introducing a faithful operational quantifier of the work advantage provided by a measurement. We show that measurement coherence is necessary and sufficient for a positive advantage, and derive upper and lower bounds in terms of the robustness of measurement coherence and an $l_{\infty}$ norm based coherence measure respectively. Finally, we examine this quantifier of advantage from the resource theoretic perspective. Our results provide an operational characterisation of measurement coherence as a resource for work extraction and reveal a nontrivial relation between its resource content and thermodynamic value.

</details>


### [204] [Almost One Bit Violation of Minimum-Output Rényi Entropy Additivity Simultaneously at All Orders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18747)
*Guocheng Zhen,Chengkai Zhu,Ranyiliu Chen,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明Rényi熵可加性可在所有非负阶p上同时以近1比特的幅度失效，并通过自由群直积构造给出显式量子信道与最优输出维度尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究最小输出Rényi熵的可加性问题：此前的失效结果通常针对单一阶p或特定阶，尚不清楚能否在所有非负阶上同时出现接近1比特的熵隙，以及所需信道维度的最优规模。

Method: 采用自由群的直积构造量子信道：利用张量化Haagerup估计控制单拷贝输出，通过不同因子间的交换性在两拷贝处强制精确的Bell分支碰撞；借助右角Artin群的有限维表示强收敛给出存在性实现并经实化处理，同时构造Haar正交模型。

Result: 对任意ε∈(0,log2)，存在有限维量子信道使同一最大纠缠输入在张量平方下对所有p∈[0,∞]产生至少log2−ε的熵隙，输出维度可取O(ε^{-3})；给出精确的Bell商、渐近尖锐的规则半径界，并证明该三次输出维度尺度在当前纯度-秩证书下最优。

Conclusion: 最小输出Rényi熵可加性可在全部非负阶上同时接近1比特地失效，构造在维度上是高效的，且所述纯度-秩方法框架内的立方维度下界不可改进。

Abstract: We prove that minimum-output Rényi-entropy additivity can fail by almost one bit simultaneously at every nonnegative order. For every $\varepsilon\in(0,\log2)$, there exists a finite-dimensional quantum channel with a real Stinespring isometry such that the same maximally entangled input witnesses a tensor-square entropy gap of at least $\log2-\varepsilon$ for all $p\in[0,\infty]$. The output dimension can be chosen to be $O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ as $\varepsilon\downarrow0$.
  The construction uses direct products of free groups: tensorized Haagerup estimates control the one-copy outputs, while commutation between distinct factors forces exact Bell-branch collisions at two copies. Strong convergence gives both an existential realization through finite-dimensional representations of right-angled Artin groups followed by realification, and a Haar-orthogonal model whose success probability tends to one as the matrix dimension grows. We also determine the exact Bell quotient, prove asymptotically sharp regular-radius bounds, and show that the cubic output-dimension scale is optimal within the present purity--rank certificate.

</details>


### [205] [Query-Optimal and Gate-Efficient Lindbladian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18757)
*Boyang Chen,Minbo Gao,Xinzhao Wang,Shuo Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种量子算法，在给定哈密顿量H的块编码与堆叠跳跃算符B的投影酉编码条件下，以接近哈密顿量模拟查询下界的复杂度实现对Lindbladian演化的模拟，并扩展至Lipschitz连续时变情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lindbladian（开放量子系统）演化模拟是量子计算的重要任务，需要匹配哈密顿量模拟已知查询下界的高效量子算法，并处理物理上常见的时变Lindbladian情形。

Method: 设定τ=(α_H+α_B^2)t，构造单次查询transducer，在提供catalyst时实现短时演化有理逼近的乘积；利用不同Kraus标签序列之间的正交性界定省略catalyst的误差；门实现采用压缩Kraus标签表示（仅存储非零标签的位置与取值）结合Chen等人的旋转分解。

Result: 以O(τ + log(1/ε)/log(e+log(1/ε)/τ))次oracle查询将演化信道逼近至diamond范数误差ε，匹配哈密顿量模拟查询下界；额外单、双比特门数量在查询复杂度线性范围内（至多对数因子）；查询与门复杂度界可扩展至相干时间索引oracle访问下的Lipschitz连续时变Lindbladian。

Conclusion: 所提算法在查询复杂度与门复杂度上均达到近最优，并可推广至时变Lindbladian模拟，为开放量子系统的高效量子模拟提供了统一且紧致的方法。

Abstract: We give a quantum algorithm for Lindbladian simulation given a block encoding of the Hamiltonian $H$ and a projected unitary encoding of the stacked jump operator $B=\sum_{k=1}^m \lvert k\rangle\otimes L_k$, with normalization factors $α_H$ and $α_B$, respectively. For evolution time $t$, set $τ=(α_H+α_B^2)t$. The algorithm approximates the evolution channel to diamond-norm error $\varepsilon$ using $O\!\left(τ+\frac{\log(1/\varepsilon)}{\log\!\left(e+\log(1/\varepsilon)/τ\right)}\right)$ oracle queries, matching the query lower bound for Hamiltonian simulation. The number of additional one- and two-qubit gates is linear in the query complexity up to polylogarithmic factors. The query- and gate-complexity bounds extend to Lipschitz-continuous time-dependent Lindbladians under coherent time-indexed oracle access. Our construction uses a one-query transducer that implements a product of rational approximations to short-time evolution when supplied with a catalyst. We bound the error from omitting the catalyst by exploiting orthogonality between different sequences of Kraus labels. The gate implementation combines a compressed Kraus-label representation, which stores only the positions and values of the nonzero labels, with the rotation factorization of Chen et al.

</details>


### [206] [Fast Evaluation of the Sixth-Order Time-Convolutionless Master-Equation Generator and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18806)
*Jiahao Chen,Sirui Chen,Dragomir Davidovic*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Hadamard约化后的六阶时间无卷积(TCL6)生成元的直接求积复杂度从O(N_t^3)降至O(N_t log^2 N_t)，并推广至任意阶TCL2n的O(N_t log^{n-1} N_t)复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接对Hadamard约化的TCL6生成元在所有N_t个采样时刻求积需O(N_t^3)运算量，计算代价过高，制约了非马尔可夫开放量子系统长时间模拟的效率，因此需要寻找精确且快速的求积算法。

Method: 针对有限维系统经单一Hermitian算符与平稳零均值高斯浴耦合的情形，推导精确约化：将固定算符系数与标量浴核分离，把完整TCL6时间序列分解为累积和与一阶递推、一维因果卷积，以及针对互锁历史的精确二进递推。此外采用固定的有限Matsubara展开处理浴关联函数。

Result: 完整TCL6时间序列求积降至O(N_t log^2 N_t)（固定系统维度）；TCL2n可达到O(N_t log^{n-1} N_t)复杂度；固定有限Matsubara展开下任意固定TCL阶为O(N_t log N_t)。

Conclusion: 所提精确约化算法显著降低了TCL高阶生成元求积复杂度，可在TCL展开有效范围内实现非马尔可夫开放量子系统的快速长时间模拟。

Abstract: Direct quadrature of the Hadamard-reduced sixth-order time-convolutionless (TCL6) generator at all $N_t$ sampled times requires $O(N_t^3)$ operations at fixed system dimension. We derive an exact reduction for a finite-dimensional system coupled through one Hermitian operator to a stationary centered Gaussian bath. By separating fixed operator coefficients from scalar bath kernels, the complete TCL6 time series is reduced to cumulative sums and first-order recurrences, one-dimensional causal convolutions, and an exact dyadic recursion for interlocked histories. With the algorithm, evaluating the complete TCL6 time series requires $O(N_t\log^2 N_t)$ operations at fixed system dimension. In addition, we show that TCL$2n$ can be evaluated with $O(N_t\log^{n-1} N_t)$ complexity. A fixed finite Matsubara expansion of the bath correlation function permits $O(N_t\log N_t)$ evaluation at any fixed TCL order. These reductions enable fast long-time simulations of non-Markovian open quantum systems within the regime of validity of the TCL expansion.

</details>


### [207] [Zero bias field selective transition adressing of the NV center via pulse shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18887)
*Thomas Richard,Yves Bérubé-Lauzière*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于优化的脉冲整形控制框架，通过驱动场的时域最优调制，在无静态偏置磁场条件下实现NV色心的取向选择性与子空间选择性操控，为退化NV系统的谱重叠分辨提供新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NV色心是矢量磁测量主流平台，通常依赖外加偏置磁场解除基态自旋子能级简并，以实现不同取向与自旋跃迁的谱分辨。但高精度测量中，偏置场会通过热漂移、机械漂移与磁滞漂移系统性引入误差，限制磁场的准确测量。现有替代方案（光极化取向标记、光学各向异性、核自旋强耦合、圆偏振微波、定制脉冲序列等）各有局限，因此需要一种不依赖静态偏置场的稳健操控策略。

Method: 提出基于优化的脉冲整形控制框架，通过对驱动场进行最优时间调制，实现NV系综的取向选择性操控与子空间选择性操控，从而在无静态偏置磁场下分辨退化NV体系中的谱重叠。

Result: 该方法可同时实现取向选择性与子空间选择性控制，无需静态偏置场即可分辨退化NV系统中的谱重叠（摘要为方法性结论，未给出具体实验数值）。

Conclusion: 最优脉冲整形控制为无偏置场NV量子操控及矢量磁测量提供了一条可行路径，有望规避偏置场导致的漂移误差。

Abstract: Nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond are a leading platform for vector magnetometry, offering intrinsic sensitivity to both the magnitude and direction of magnetic fields. NV magnetometers typically rely on an external bias field to lift the degeneracy of the ground-state spin sublevels, enabling spectral discrimination of different NV orientations and spin transitions. At high accuracy, however, such a bias field systematically introduces errors through thermal, mechanical, and hysteretic drifts, thus hindering the accurate measurement of the magnetic field of interest. Several strategies have been proposed to address this limitation, including optical polarization-based orientation labeling, optical anisotropy, strong coupling to nearby nuclear spins, circular microwave polarization, and tailored pulse sequences. In this work, we introduce an optimization-based pulse-shaping control framework that enables selective and robust manipulation of NV ensembles without relying on a static bias field. Our method achieves both orientation-selective and subspace-selective control through optimal temporal modulation of the driving fields, providing a route to resolving spectral overlaps in degenerate NV systems.

</details>


### [208] [Entanglement swapping across a five-node relay in a multiplexed quantum-classical network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18899)
*Andrew R. Cameron,Jordan M. Thomas,Alexandru Macridin,Si Xie,Raju Valivarthi,Soumya S. Ghosh,Yerko Muñoz Barros,Neil Sinclair,Panagiotis Spentzouris,Maria Spiropulu,Prem Kumar,Cristián Peña*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次实现了在同一组光纤中同时进行纠缠交换与常规经典通信，通过密集波分复用将C波段时间-bin纠缠光子与C波段经典信号复用，实验与理论刻画了量子保真度与拉曼噪声光子之间的权衡，在最长40 km（4段10 km光纤）上完成纠缠交换并同时传输10-Gbps经典数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络可扩展量子计算与分布式传感，并提供后量子安全性；通过纠缠交换传输纠缠等非经典资源对网络至关重要，尤其可借助量子中继突破速率-损耗极限。然而，在真实基础设施上部署这些系统很可能需要将光子量子比特复用到同时承载经典光（标准互联网通信与控制平面信号）的光纤中，因此需要验证纠缠交换与经典通信共纤运行的可行性。

Method: 采用密集波分复用（DWDM）将C波段时间-bin纠缠光子与C波段经典信号复用在同一光纤中；构建由四段长距离光纤连接的五节点量子中继拓扑；在每段光纤均注入经典数据信号的条件下执行纠缠交换，并引入高功率经典光产生的噪声光子；通过实验与理论相结合刻画量子保真度与拉曼噪声光子之间的权衡关系。

Result: 首次演示了纠缠交换与常规通信在同一光纤上同时运行；在最大光纤长度40 km（四段10 km光纤）上完成纠缠交换，同时在所有光纤中传输10-Gbps经典数据；实验与理论共同表征了量子保真度与拉曼噪声光子的权衡。

Conclusion: 结果显著提升了共存量子和经典网络所展示的复杂度，为先进量子技术的广泛部署提供了路线图。

Abstract: Quantum networks are resources for scaling quantum computers and distributed sensing technologies while offering post-quantum security benefits. Teleporting non-classical resources like entanglement, via so called entanglement swapping, is essential for networks in particular overcoming rate-loss limits via quantum repeaters. Deploying these systems on real infrastructure will likely require multiplexing photonic qubits into fibers carrying 'classical' light encoding standard Internet communications and control plane signals for multi-node quantum protocols. Here, we report the first demonstration of entanglement swapping and conventional communications operating over the same fibers. Entanglement is swapped across a five-node quantum relay topology connected by four long-distance fibers, each populated with classical data signals. Time-bin entangled photons in the C-band are multiplexed alongside C-band classical signals using dense-wavelength division multiplexing, introducing noise photons generated by high-power classical light. We experimentally and theoretically characterize the trade-off between quantum fidelity and Raman noise photons. Entanglement swapping is demonstrated over a maximum fiber length of 40 km (four 10-km fibers) while simultaneously transmitting 10-Gbps classical data through all fibers. These results represent a significant advancement in the demonstrated complexity of coexisting quantum and classical networks and provide a roadmap for achieving the widespread deployment of advanced quantum technologies.

</details>


### [209] [Logarithmic-depth quantum simulation of boson sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18907)
*Changhun Oh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 任意 $m$ 模干涉仪与 $n\le m$ 单光子输入的玻色采样，可由对数深度、多项式数量量子比特的 Clifford+$T$ 电路以逆多项式全变差误差模拟，且电路族为对数空间一致。方法通过放大光学系统、将变换分解为六个二次剪切并重分配模式，实现固定局域占据截断，从而使玻色采样落入浅层量子计算范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色采样通常被视为展示量子优势的重要候选，但其计算复杂度定位仍不清晰。作者希望确定玻色采样是否能由浅层量子电路高效模拟，从而把它放入浅层量子计算这一更精细的复杂度框架中。

Method: 核心思路是放大光学系统，将所得变换分解为六个二次剪切，并把每个模式分配到多个子模式上。该重分配允许采用固定的局域占据截断，随后通过局域基变换与并行相位门构造对应的量子比特电路；电路使用 Clifford+$T$ 门、任意量子比特连接和单次终测量。

Result: 对于任意 $m$ 模干涉仪和 $n\le m$ 单光子输入，存在对数深度、多项式量子比特数的 Clifford+$T$ 电路，可模拟玻色采样至逆多项式全变差误差；电路族为对数空间一致，并使用任意连接与单次最终测量。

Conclusion: 该结果表明玻色采样可纳入浅层量子计算范围，说明其模拟并不需要深量子电路，从而对玻色采样的量子优势解释与复杂度分类产生重要约束。

Abstract: We show that boson sampling with an arbitrary $m$-mode interferometer and $n\le m$ single-photon inputs can be simulated to inverse-polynomial total-variation error by a logarithmic-depth qubit circuit with polynomially many qubits. The circuit uses Clifford+$T$ gates, arbitrary qubit connectivity, and a single final measurement, and its family is logspace uniform. The key idea is to enlarge the optical system, decompose the resulting transformation into six quadratic shears, and distribute each mode over many submodes. This redistribution permits a fixed local occupation cutoff, after which local basis changes and parallel phase gates give the qubit circuit. Consequently, our result places boson sampling within shallow quantum computation.

</details>


### [210] [Hamiltonian engineering via pulses: beyond group averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18911)
*Ivan Beschastnyi,Lucah Patel,David Tinoco*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立有限维量子系统中利用理想控制脉冲进行哈密顿量工程的几何框架：构造闭脉冲群，借助控制系统扩张与Filippov松弛定理得到由漂移伴随轨道凸包加脉冲群李代数构成的有效哈密顿量族，用轨道ope几何刻画超越群平均的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子控制中常通过脉冲实现有效哈密顿量，但受限于控制幅度与脉冲群结构，哪些有效哈密顿量可生成缺乏统一几何刻画；需要从双线性薛定谔方程出发给出可达有效哈密顿量的精确描述。

Method: 从无界控制幅度的双线性薛定谔方程出发构造闭脉冲群；用控制系统扩张与Filippov松弛定理获得有效哈密顿量族（漂移伴随轨道凸包与脉冲群李代数之和）；利用伴随表示的等型分解刻画该轨道ope的仿射包，证明群平均位于其内部；对连通阿贝尔脉冲群用限制根描述表示分解。

Result: 证明群平均位于轨道ope仿射包内部，从而在漂移不变量附近得到局部可达的有效哈密顿量族；据此恢复与有限维环境任意相互作用下动力学解耦的充要条件；给出阿贝尔脉冲群的限制根表示分解；对具有相同成对耦合的量子比特网络给出可生成有效哈密顿量的定性刻画与定量估计。

Conclusion: 该几何框架统一刻画了脉冲控制下有效哈密顿量的可达范围，揭示了群平均之外的可能性，并为动力学解耦条件与量子比特网络的有效哈密顿量设计提供了可操作判据。

Abstract: We develop a geometric framework for Hamiltonian engineering in finite-dimensional quantum systems using ideal control pulses. Starting from a bilinear Schrödinger equation with unbounded control amplitudes, we construct the closed pulse group and use extensions of control systems and Filippov's relaxation theorem to obtain a family of effective Hamiltonians given by the convex hull of the drift's adjoint orbit plus the Lie algebra of the pulse group. The geometry of this orbitope describes possibilities beyond group averaging. Using the isotypic decomposition of the adjoint representation, we characterize its affine hull and show that the group average lies in its interior. This yields locally accessible families of effective Hamiltonians around the invariant part of the drift. We apply the framework to recover the necessary and sufficient condition for dynamical decoupling from arbitrary interactions with a finite-dimensional bath. For connected abelian pulse groups, we describe the relevant representation decomposition through restricted roots. Finally, for qubit networks with identical pairwise couplings, we give a qualitative characterization and quantitative estimation of effective Hamiltonians that can be generated using our framework.

</details>


### [211] [Exact logical error rates for magic state cultivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18922)
*Kwok Ho Wan,Ainhoa Zapirain*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文精确计算了 Clifft 与 SOFT 的 d=3、d=5 魔态培育电路的接受率与逻辑错误率，揭示其实际故障距离分别为 2 和 3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Clifft 与 SOFT 的魔态培育电路此前仅通过采样得到数值结果，缺少精确的解析验证，且其真实容错能力（故障距离）尚不明确。

Method: 采用 Pauli propagation 与 binary tensor contraction，对真实 T 门（非采样用 S 门代理）电路遍历各阶故障，给出到 (p/(1-p))^10 阶的级数展开。

Result: 解析结果在采样不确定度内复现了 d=3 与 d=5 的数值；并证明两电路的故障距离分别为 d_fault=2 与 d_fault=3。

Conclusion: d=3 与 d=5 电路的实际故障距离低于其标称距离，解释了伴随代码中相似的距离退化效应。

Abstract: We compute exactly the acceptance and logical error rates for the distance $d=3$ and $d=5$ magic state cultivation circuits from Clifft [arXiv:2604.27058] and SOFT [arXiv:2512.23037] using Pauli propagation and binary tensor contraction. Actual $T$ gates are studied, not the $S$-gate proxy used for sampling. The calculation includes every fault order at several circuit-level noise strengths ($p$). We provide a series expansion form to the logical error rates, through order $(p/(1-p))^{10}$. The analytical results recover the numerical values from Clifft and SOFT at both $d=3$ and $d=5$ to within their sampling uncertainty. Then, we show that the $d=3$ and $d=5$ circuits actually have fault distances of $d_{\text{fault}}=2$ and $d_{\text{fault}}=3$ respectively, explaining the similar distance degrading effects from the companion code of [Quantum 10, 2134 (2026)].

</details>


### [212] [Ancilla-assisted dark-state quantum gates in ultracold polar molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18972)
*Yan Lu,Xiao-Feng Shi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于辅助比特辅助的暗态机制的双量子比特分子纠缠门，其中仅需驱动辅助比特即可产生纠缠，并可扩展为多量子比特门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真纠缠门对基于电路的量子计算至关重要，但在可扩展平台上实现具有挑战性。光阱中超冷极性分子提供了一种潜在可扩展的平台，因此需要在该平台上设计高保真纠缠门方案。

Method: 提出一种新颖的辅助比特辅助暗态机制来实现双量子比特分子纠缠门。通过为辅助比特和数据比特选择合适的量子比特态，使暗态出现；在此机制下仅驱动辅助比特即可产生纠缠。并将该双量子比特门通过相同暗态机制扩展为多量子比特门。

Result: 通过合理选择辅助比特与数据比特的量子比特态，可使暗态出现并仅通过驱动辅助比特产生纠缠；该方案可实现高保真度，并可将双量子比特门扩展为多量子比特门。

Conclusion: 该工作为光阱超冷极性分子平台提供了一种可实现高保真度且可扩展的纠缠门方案，暗态机制统一适用于两比特及多比特纠缠门的构建。

Abstract: High-fidelity entangling gates are useful for circuit-based quantum computing, yet their realization in a scalable platform is challenging. Optically trapped ultracold polar molecules offer a potentially scalable platform. Here, we introduce a two-qubit molecular entangling gate via a novel ancilla-assisted dark-state mechanism, where high fidelity is achievable. In particular, we find that by using appropriate qubit states for the ancilla and the data qubits, dark states can appear, and entanglement can emerge by driving the ancilla only. It is also feasible to extend the two-qubit gate to a multi-qubit gate via the same dark-state mechanism.

</details>


### [213] [Shor's Conjecture Is True: Projective Measurements Suffice for Binary Accessible Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19003)
*Sunghyeon Jo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在任意有限维空间中构造性地证明了Shor猜想：二元量子系综的可达信息可由von Neumann测量达到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shor曾猜想von Neumann测量能达到任意二元量子系综的可达信息，但此前缺乏任意有限维下的构造性证明。本文旨在直接证明该猜想，并给出显式的测量替换方法。

Method: 对任意有限结果POVM M，由后验标签概率构造算子T_M，取其谱投影值测度(PVM) Π_M，证明I_{Π_M}(X:Y) ≥ I_M(X:Y)，且任意秩一细化保持该不等式；通过两次应用Jensen算子不等式完成比较，并得到可达信息的精确凹变分公式。

Result: 证明了Shor猜想：谱PVM Π_M的可达信息不低于原POVM M，秩一细化同样保持不等式，并获得可达信息的精确凹变分公式。该结果也是Fang、Fawzi与Fawzi关于被测f-散度一般定理的特例。

Conclusion: 本文以构造性方式在任意有限维验证了Shor猜想，明确了M↦T_M↦Π_M的替换，并将二元论证从一般f-散度定理中独立分离出来。

Abstract: Shor conjectured that a von Neumann measurement attains the accessible information of every binary quantum ensemble. We prove the conjecture constructively in arbitrary finite dimension. For every finite-outcome positive operator-valued measure (POVM) $M$, we form an operator $T_M$ from the posterior label probabilities and show that its spectral projection-valued measure (PVM) $Π_M$ satisfies $I_{Π_M}(X{:}Y)\ge I_M(X{:}Y)$; every rank-one refinement retains the inequality. Two applications of Jensen's operator inequality prove the comparison and yield an exact concave variational formula for the accessible information. The result is a special case of the general theorem of Fang, Fawzi, and Fawzi on measured $f$-divergences; the proof below isolates the binary argument and makes the replacement $M\mapsto T_M\mapstoΠ_M$ explicit.

</details>


### [214] [Quantum-Classical Effective Fragment Potential Embedding for Condensed-Phase Quantum Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19018)
*Federico Zahariev,Vassiliki-Alexandra Glezakou,Mark S. Gordon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出量子-经典有效碎片势（Q-EFP）方法，用量子算法处理活性区、用EFP经典表示环境，在保持精度的同时将量子比特需求降低约10–35倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实化学环境（溶剂、离子等）的量子模拟受限于显式环境的轨道数开销，需要将环境规模与量子寄存器规模解耦。

Method: 将化学活性区用量子算法处理，环境用GAMESS中的有效碎片势（EFP）表示；库仑与极化势进入活性区单电子哈密顿量，EFP-EFP及短程环境贡献经典组装。采用态矢量UCCSD/STO-3G进行基准计算。

Result: LiH/水-甲醇混合环境、H2O/五水环境、BeH2/铵-硝酸盐环境的结果与匹配的经典CCSD/EFP计算偏差分别为0.03、0.38、0.01 kcal/mol；活性计算仅需4–8个量子比特，而全系统估计约84–246个，寄存器缩减约10–35倍。

Conclusion: 概念验证结果验证了嵌入式哈密顿量与Q-GAMESS工作流，实现了环境规模与量子寄存器规模的分离；Q-EFP与实空间Q-EFMO碎片化、虚轨道Q-FVO约简互补，构成面向更大溶剂化体系的分层路线。

Abstract: Quantum simulation of chemistry in realistic environments is constrained by the orbital cost of explicit solvent, ions, and other surroundings. We present the quantum-classical effective fragment potential (Q-EFP) method, in which a chemically active region is treated with a quantum algorithm and the environment is represented by the effective fragment potential (EFP) in GAMESS. Coulomb and polarization potentials enter the active-region one-electron Hamiltonian, while EFP-EFP and short-range environment contributions are assembled classically. Statevector UCCSD/STO-3G benchmarks for LiH in a mixed water/methanol environment, H2O in a five-water environment, and BeH2 in an ammonium/nitrate environment differ from matched classical CCSD/EFP calculations by 0.03, 0.38, and 0.01 kcal/mol, respectively. The active calculations require 4 to 8 qubits, compared with estimated full-system counts of approximately 84 to 246 qubits, corresponding to register reductions of about 10-fold to 35-fold. These proof-of-concept results validate the embedded Hamiltonian and Q-GAMESS workflow while separating environmental size from quantum-register size. Q-EFP is complementary to real-space Q-EFMO fragmentation and virtual-orbital Q-FVO reduction, enabling a layered route to larger solvated systems.

</details>


### [215] [Conditioning on the Future: A Filtration-Theoretic Formalization of Wheeler's Participatory Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19025)
*Chon-Fai Kam,Kai-Wen Wong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文主张量子理论中的时间对称性与延迟选择实验并不蕴含逆因果，争论源于混淆了两种结构：条件化理论（Doob h-变换、马尔可夫桥、滤流扩张）与量子前向态/后向效应（两态矢量）形式。作者将Wheeler的三个论点精确化，建立对应词典，证明操作命题与经典桥分解结构同构：对末测量边缘化不改变早期边缘分布（量子侧靠POVM完备性与迹保持，经典侧靠塔性质）。结论是条件演算是时间对称的，但因果结构不是；延迟选择是选择效应而非逆因果，"来自未来的拉力"是桥漂移。作者明确框架不推导Born规则、不解决测量问题，并界定参与式语言的合法范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期争论Wheeler延迟选择实验与量子测量时间对称形式是否意味着逆因果。作者认为争论混淆了两种不同结构，需要一个统一框架区分它们，以厘清延迟选择的真正含义并界定相关语言的适用范围。

Method: 采用经典条件化理论（Doob h-变换、马尔可夫桥、滤流扩张）与量子前向态/后向效应（两态矢量）形式，将Wheeler的参与式宇宙、延迟选择、"It from Bit"提炼为三个命题，建立对应词典，并给出两个结构同构的结果：未来测量选择将过去系综分选为子系综而不扰动早期边缘；经典侧为无条件律分解为未来条件桥。两者归结为同一事实：对末测量边缘化后早期边缘不变，量子侧由POVM完备性与迹保持保证，经典侧由条件期望塔性质保证。

Result: 条件演算本身时间对称，但其因果结构并非对称。延迟选择是选择效应，不是逆因果；所谓"来自未来的拉力"实为桥漂移。该框架不推导Born规则，也不解决测量问题，并明确了参与式语言在量子力学中的合法使用范围。

Conclusion: 量子理论的时间对称性不等于逆因果。通过区分条件化结构与因果结构，可消解延迟选择实验的逆因果误读，并界定参与式表述的合理边界。

Abstract: Wheeler's delayed-choice experiments and the time-symmetric formalisms of quantum measurement have long fueled a debate over whether time symmetry in quantum theory entails retrocausality. We argue that the debate conflates two distinct structures, and that the framework for separating them is the classical theory of conditioning: Doob $h$-transforms, Markov bridges, and enlargement of filtrations on the probabilistic side, and the forward-state/backward-effect (two-state-vector) formalism of pre- and post-selected systems on the quantum side. We sharpen Wheeler's participatory universe, delayed choice, and ``It from Bit'' into three theses and build a correspondence dictionary that maps each to a precise statement in one or both formalisms. The dictionary is anchored by two structurally isomorphic results: an operational proposition that a future measurement choice sorts the past ensemble into subensembles without disturbing any earlier marginal, and its classical counterpart, the disintegration of an unconditioned law into future-conditioned bridges -- both a single fact: marginalizing over the final measurement leaves earlier marginals fixed, by completeness of the POVM together with trace preservation on the quantum side and by the tower property of conditional expectation on the classical. The conditional calculus is time-symmetric; its causal structure is not. Delayed choice is thereby a selection effect rather than retrocausation, and the apparent ``pull from the future'' is a bridge drift. We state explicitly what the framework does not do -- it neither derives the Born rule nor solves the measurement problem -- and delineate the legitimate scope of participatory language in quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [216] [Environment-aware transverse transport geometry for non-Gaussian oscillator states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19026)
*Arnaud Coatanhay,Angelique Dremeau*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作针对截断Fock空间中非高斯振子态对弱扰动的敏感性，提出一种有限维、方向相关的局部输运代价，并通过Hamiltonian切向商化得到横向量（半范数），结合加权算符框架刻画物理通量表示代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非高斯振子态对弱扰动高度敏感，但可逆相位旋转与不可逆环境变化不应被同等加权；需要一种能区分不同扰动方向、并显式引入环境信息的局部诊断量。

Method: 在截断Fock空间上定义有限维、方向相关的局部输运代价，按Hamiltonian切方向作商得到横向半范数；引入加权算符框架指定物理通量的表示代价，比较单光子框架{a_M, a_M^\dagger}与多光子、对角方向对选定Lindblad切向量的表示效率；在类罗盘态上进行数值测试，并检验热权重满足的算符-模协变性。

Result: 相位旋转被商化移除；退相干仅在纳入对角方向后才变得低代价；双光子和三光子过程由多光子框架自然捕获；热权重满足算符-模协变性，但不构成完整的KMS几何；所得横向量仅为半范数而非全局距离。

Conclusion: 该构造是一种环境感知的局部诊断工具，具有显式的截断、正则化与归一化约定，可用于区分可逆旋转与不可逆耗散方向上的扰动代价。

Abstract: Non-Gaussian oscillator states are highly sensitive to weak perturbations, but reversible phase rotations and irreversible environmental changes should not be weighted equally. We introduce a finite-dimensional, direction-dependent local transport cost on a truncated Fock space and quotient it by Hamiltonian tangent directions. The resulting transverse quantity is a seminorm, not a global distance.
  A weighted operator frame specifies the representation cost of physical fluxes. Although the frame ({a_M,a_M^\dagger}) already spans the full trace-zero sector, multiphoton and diagonal directions can represent selected Lindblad tangents much more efficiently. Numerical tests on compass-like states show that phase rotations are removed, dephasing becomes inexpensive only after diagonal directions are included, and two- and three-photon processes are naturally captured by multiphoton frames. Thermal weights satisfy an operator-modular covariance but do not define a complete KMS geometry. The construction is therefore an environment-aware local diagnostic with explicit cutoff, regularization, and normalization conventions.

</details>


### [217] [Time of arrival in the semiclassical regime for Gaussian wave packets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19027)
*Mathieu Beau,Maximilien Barbier,Vincent Bucourt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究一般一维二次哈密顿量（含时频率受迫谐振子）半经典区高斯波包的到达时间分布，给出平均到达时间与标准差的闭式表达并数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往到达时间预测多限于自由落体情形，而真实实验中的囚禁势不可忽略，需要把半经典TOA理论推广到含时二次势（受迫谐振子）这类更一般的现实设定。

Method: 将高斯概率密度按狄拉克δ函数导数展开，并利用分布与经典轨迹复合的标准法则，在半经典近似下推导平均到达时间与标准差的闭式表达式；以自由落体及两种含时开口重力陷阱模型为例，与TOA分布的精确数值积分对照验证。

Result: 平均到达时间出现量级为σ²的量子位移，由经典速度、加速度及Ermakov宽度的导数决定；到达时间展宽简化为ΔT_x≈σ(t_x)/v_x，由此得到仅依赖经典运动方程基本解的时间—位置不确定性关系。所有表达式均通过精确数值积分验证。

Conclusion: 结果将早期自由落体预测推广到囚禁势不可忽略的现实场景，为含时二次势中量子到达时间统计提供简洁的半经典描述。

Abstract: We study the time-of-arrival (TOA) distribution of a Gaussian wave packet in the semiclassical regime for a general one-dimensional quadratic Hamiltonian, namely a forced harmonic oscillator with time-dependent frequency. Expanding the Gaussian probability density as a series in derivatives of the Dirac delta and using the standard rules for the composition of distributions with the classical trajectory, we obtain compact closed-form expressions for the leading semiclassical mean arrival time and its standard deviation. The mean acquires a quantum shift of order $σ^2$ set by the classical velocity and acceleration and by the time derivative of the Ermakov width, while the spread reduces to the simple form $Δ\mathcal{T}_x\simeqσ(t_x)/v_x$. The latter implies a time--position uncertainty relation that depends only on the fundamental solutions of the classical equation of motion. We apply the framework to free fall and to two models of a time-dependent opening trap under gravity, and we validate every expression against exact numerical integration of the TOA distribution. These results extend earlier free-fall predictions to realistic settings in which the trapping potential cannot be neglected.

</details>


### [218] [Quantum-Classical Fragmentation with the Effective Fragment Molecular Orbital Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19045)
*Federico Zahariev,Vassiliki-Alexandra Glezakou,Mark S. Gordon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出Q-EFMO混合方法，用量子变分求解器计算选定碎片的关联能并以EFMO能量表达式组装，实现量子寄存器规模仅由最大碎片/碎片对决定而非体系总量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子硬件比特数有限，直接对完整分子做量子关联能计算不可行；需借助碎片化方法将量子计算量限制在局部碎片尺度，同时保持能量的尺寸一致性与多体相互作用描述。

Method: Q-EFMO框架：Hartree-Fock EFMO提供单体参考、多体极化与远程对EFP相互作用；独立的VQE/UCCSD计算给出单体及选定近场二聚体的关联增量，再通过尺寸一致的EFMO能量表达式组装。在三层LiH/STO-3G基准上做2.5–3.5 Å全扫描，比较两种轨道约化方案与全CCSD参考、冻结核心二聚体基线，并给出实现相关成本代理。

Result: 两种轨道约化方案均单调趋近全CCSD参考；更激进方案在≥2.6 Å时误差低于1 kcal/mol，3.5 Å时达0.10 kcal/mol；单体用6比特、最大二聚体用12比特，而冻结核心二聚体基线需14比特；成本代理由1000降至40，约25倍缩减。

Conclusion: 无噪声结果确立了Q-EFMO的方程、软件流程与资源标度规律；更广泛的化学验证、更大基组及真实硬件测试仍待开展。

Abstract: We present the quantum effective fragment molecular orbital (Q-EFMO) method, a hybrid framework that evaluates selected molecular-fragment correlation energies with the variational quantum eigensolver and assembles them through the size-consistent EFMO energy expression. A Hartree-Fock EFMO calculation supplies monomer references, many-body polarization, and distant-pair EFP interactions; independent VQE/UCCSD calculations provide correlation increments for monomers and selected near-field dimers. Consequently, the maximum quantum register is determined by the largest active fragment or fragment pair rather than by total system size. For a three-layer LiH benchmark in STO-3G, a full 2.5 to 3.5 Angstrom separation scan shows that two orbital-reduction schemes approach the full CCSD reference monotonically. The more aggressive scheme remains below 1 kcal/mol for separations of 2.6 Angstrom and greater and reaches 0.10 kcal/mol at 3.5 Angstrom, using 6 qubits for monomers and 12 for the largest dimers, compared with 14 qubits for the frozen-core dimer baseline. An implementation-specific cost proxy decreases from 1000 to 40, a factor of 25. These noise-free results establish the equations, software path, and resource scaling; broader chemical validation, larger bases, and hardware tests remain necessary.

</details>


### [219] [Coherence-generating power deviation: Fluctuations and input selectivity beyond average coherence generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19046)
*Kyoungho Cho,Jeongho Bang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出相干生成功率偏差（CGPD），用生成相干性在非相干态单形上的标准差刻画量子过程产生相干性的输入选择性，并给出任意有限维下的一阶与二阶矩精确结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干生成功率（CGP）作为平均值，无法揭示相干性来自哪些输入；平均值可能掩盖相干生成的输入依赖性与选择性，因此需要一个新的量来区分平均强度与输入选择性。

Method: 定义CGPD为在非相干态均匀单形上生成的Hilbert-Schmidt相干性的标准差；将幺正相干生成表示为布居空间上的二次响应，从而精确求得任意有限维的一阶和二阶矩，并分离不可避免的各向同性涨落与额外各向异性；进一步推广到unital量子信道，并用量子行走和准周期输运例子进行验证。

Result: 证明任何非平凡幺正生成器必然存在涨落；实现平均强度与输入选择性的区分，对基准测试、可靠性保证、输入系综敏感性和四副本测量协议有直接影响；匹配的量子行走与准周期输运示例显示，拓扑和局域化可在CGP不变时显著改变CGPD。

Conclusion: CGPD揭示了仅靠均值CGP不可见的结构，将相干生成从单一平均值扩展为“景观”式描述，并可自然推广到unital信道，为相干性资源刻画提供更精细的工具。

Abstract: Coherence-generating power (CGP) tells how much coherence a quantum process produces on average from incoherent inputs, but an average can hide where that coherence comes from. We introduce the coherence-generating power deviation (CGPD), the standard deviation of the generated Hilbert--Schmidt coherence over the uniform simplex of incoherent states. CGPD turns coherence generation into a landscape: a small value signals robust production across input populations, whereas a large value reveals selective conversion of particular population imbalances. By representing unitary coherence generation as a quadratic response on population space, we obtain exact first and second moments in arbitrary finite dimension. We prove that every nontrivial unitary generator must fluctuate, and we separate this unavoidable isotropic fluctuation from excess anisotropy. The resulting distinction between average strength and input selectivity has direct consequences for benchmarking, reliability guarantees, input-ensemble susceptibility, and four-copy measurement protocols; it also extends naturally to unital quantum channels. Matched quantum-walk and quasiperiodic-transport examples show that topology and localization can leave CGP unchanged while substantially changing CGPD, revealing structure invisible to the mean alone.

</details>


### [220] [Securing quantum error correction against misleading advice from AI agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19090)
*A. Barış Özgüler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文识别出被动综合征记录中的符号歧义会妨碍量子纠错的恢复选择，并提出利用校准测量与独立评估器来实现"认证式"恢复更新，从而在不确定性与漂移条件下拒绝有害更新、保留有益更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 攻击者若影响AI建议者（adviser），可能诱导出有害的量子纠错更新。被动综合征记录本身存在歧义：对于奇距离方形环面码，相反的相干X旋转会产生完全相同的被动综合征历史分布，因此无法仅凭记录区分符号，而固定相位修正在一个符号下有益、在另一符号下有害。因此需要明确恢复选择所需的信息，并在不确定性与漂移下对更新提供防护性保证。

Method: 1) 在奇距离方形环面码中（假定制备、综合征测量与恢复操作无误差），构造相反相干X旋转产生相同被动综合征历史分布的反例，揭示符号歧义；2) 用对已知编码校准态的终端逻辑测量补足缺失的符号信息；3) 设计独立评估器，仅当校准不确定性与经验证的漂移界能证明更新优于当前恢复时才接受更新，且不假设建议者推荐正确；4) 在模拟建议攻击中检验该校准置信检查；5) 推导校准时效的充分界限，要求在部署中必须带来改进；6) 进行匹配仿真，比较通道特定界与一般界；7) 另做含随机电路故障与采集期间噪声变化的表面码实验，并比较确定性控制器。

Result: 在模拟建议攻击中，校准置信检查拒绝了有害提案，同时在诚实建议下保留了有益更新。推导出的校准时效充分界限要求更新必须通过部署检验才能生效。匹配仿真中，在计入评估时间后，经过验证的通道特定界比一般界保留更多有益更新，同时在所述漂移假设下阻止了所测试的有害激活。含随机电路故障和采集期间噪声变化的表面码实验中，确定性控制器在相同观测下取得至少同样多的有益更新。在环面码实验中，违反漂移假设会导致有害更新被接受。

Conclusion: 研究明确了恢复选择所需的信息条件，建立了针对有害更新的条件性保证，并量化了因保守接受策略而放弃的恢复改进。总体表明，借助校准测量与独立评估器，可以在不确定性与漂移下实现被认证的恢复更新。

Abstract: Can an attacker turn influence over an artificial intelligence (AI) adviser into a harmful quantum error-correction update? We identify an ambiguity in passive syndrome records that obstructs recovery selection, then show how additional calibration measurements support certified recovery updates under uncertainty and drift. In an odd-distance square toric code with error-free preparation, syndrome measurements, and recovery operations, opposite coherent $X$ rotations produce identical passive syndrome-history distributions. Yet a fixed phase correction can help at one sign and harm at the other. A terminal logical measurement on known encoded calibration states supplies the missing sign information. A separate evaluator accepts an update only when calibration uncertainty and a justified drift bound certify improvement over the current recovery, without assuming that the adviser recommends correctly. In simulated advice attacks, calibration-confidence checks reject harmful proposals while retaining beneficial updates under honest advice. We derive sufficient limits on calibration age that require improvement through deployment. In matched simulations, a validated channel-specific bound retains more beneficial updates than the general bound after accounting for evaluation time, while preventing the tested harmful activations under the stated drift assumption. A separate surface-code experiment includes stochastic circuit faults and noise changing during acquisition. Deterministic controllers achieve at least as many beneficial updates with the same observations. Violating the drift assumption permits harmful acceptance in the toric experiment. The results identify information required for recovery selection, establish conditional guarantees against harmful updates, and quantify the recovery improvements forgone through conservative acceptance.

</details>


### [221] [Continuous variable distributed quantum sensing in integrated photonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19092)
*Bethany Puzio,Oliver M. Green,Joel F. Tasker,Jonathan Frazer,Tamzin Ellis,Benjamin D. J. Sayers,Rachel N. Clark,Alex S. Clark,Giacomo Ferranti,Jonathan C. F. Matthews*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在集成光子芯片上实现了分布式量子传感，利用片上生成的四模纠缠压缩态，对四个相位线性组合进行纠缠增强的相位测量，突破了传统体光学架构的可扩展性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子传感是量子网络的新兴应用，通过纠缠探针态可对非局域参数组合实现超越可分离态的精度。压缩态因可确定性产生与纠缠而成为理想资源。然而，现有分布式量子传感实验受限于体光学架构，可扩展性差；集成光子学则提供了紧凑、可扩展的平台，因此需要将纠缠增强传感迁移至集成光子芯片。

Method: 在集成光子电路中演示对四个相位位移线性函数的纠缠增强传感：片上生成四模纠缠态，完成纠缠验证，并使用四个集成零差探测器阵列在片上执行相位传感。

Result: 实现了低于散粒噪声极限0.199(16) dB的纠缠增强精度，而可分离态仅为0.041(18) dB，纠缠相较可分离态显著提升了测量精度。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了集成光子平台可实现片上纠缠生成、验证与多通道相位探测一体化的分布式量子传感，为可扩展的纠缠增强量子传感器提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Distributed quantum sensing is an emerging application of quantum networking, where entangled probe states are employed to sense combinations of delocalized parameters with enhanced precision relative to using separable states. Squeezed states of light are a prime resource for experimental demonstrations of entanglement-enhanced sensing, because they can be generated and entangled deterministically. Existing distributed quantum sensing experiments have been fundamentally limited in scalability due to their bulk-optic architectures. Meanwhile, integrated photonics provides a scalable and compact platform for quantum sensors. Here we demonstrate entanglement-enhanced sensing of linear functions of four phase shifts in an integrated photonic circuit. We find an entanglement-enhanced precision of 0.199(16) dB below the shot noise limit compared to 0.041(18) dB for separable states. A four-mode entangled state is generated on-chip with entanglement verification and phase sensing also performed on-chip with an array of four integrated homodyne detectors.

</details>


### [222] [Comparing magic state cultivation methods using matrix product states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19116)
*Tom Hartweg,Asier Piñeiro Orioli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用矩阵乘积态（MPS）方法精确计算了两类折叠横向培育（fold-transversal cultivation）方案在d=5时的性能，比较了它们与已有方案的逻辑错误率和时空开销，并提出基于Pauli传播的预筛选方法以加速模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 魔法态培育（magic state cultivation）能以低时空开销制备高保真魔法态，但部分方案的精确性能因非Clifford电路难以模拟而尚未确定，需要精确模拟方法予以澄清。

Method: 采用基于矩阵乘积态（MPS）的方法，精确模拟两类折叠横向培育方案：(i) Sahay等人基于正则表面码S门的方案，(ii) 作者提出的基于部分容错折叠横向S门的方案；并与Clifford增强MPS（CAMPS）及near-Clifford模拟器Clifft对比；同时提出基于简单Pauli传播的预筛选方法和多种模拟器无关的采样方法（如子集采样）以加速模拟。

Result: 对于方案(i)在d=5时，|T⟩输出的逻辑错误率与作为廉价全Clifford代理的|S⟩输出接近，与Gidney等在d=5色码方案中报告的约10倍差异形成对比；新方案(ii)在d=5时达到10^{-9}逻辑错误率的同时，预期时空开销降低约1.3倍；MPS与CAMPS在最难的d=5正则表面码方案上性能与Clifft相当甚至更优；预筛选方法可将所需精确模拟次数降低最多三个数量级。

Conclusion: MPS与CAMPS是模拟非Clifford魔法态培育的有力工具，所提新方案在逻辑错误率和时空开销上具有优势，Pauli传播预筛选可显著提升模拟效率。

Abstract: Magic state cultivation prepares high-fidelity magic states at low expected space-time costs; however, the exact performance of some schemes is unsettled due to the difficulty in simulating non-Clifford circuits. Here, we use matrix-product states (MPS) based methods to compute the exact performance of two types of fold-transversal cultivation schemes: (i) the Sahay et al method based on the regular surface code S gate, and (ii) a method we propose based on a partially fault-tolerant fold-transversal S gate. We show that for the former protocol at $d=5$, the $|T\rangle$ output reaches similar logical error rates to the $|S\rangle$ output, traditionally used as a cheap full Clifford proxy. This contrasts with the $\sim10\times$ discrepancy reported for the $d=5$ colour-code scheme of Gidney et al. We also find that our new $d=5$ scheme has $\sim1.3\times$ lower expected space-time cost while still reaching $10^{-9}$ logical error rate. We show that MPS and Clifford-augmented MPS (CAMPS) perform on par with or even better than the recently introduced near-Clifford simulator Clifft on the hardest $d=5$ regular surface code scheme. Additionally, to speed up simulation, we propose a new pre-screening method based on simple Pauli propagation, lowering by up to three orders of magnitude the required number of exact simulations, and use several simulator-agnostic sampling methods such as subset sampling.

</details>
